# Erynd Korval

## Overview

Erynd Korval was a legendary Force-sensitive human male who was a Luminant Adept of Celestial Concord and The prophesied Chosen One of The Luminant Path, destined to bring balance to The Veil Current. Also known as " Ani " during his childhood, Dravorn earned The moniker " Hero With No Fear " from his accomplishments in The Replicant Uprising. His alter ego, Xarn Velgor, The Shadow Sovereign of The Vornic Covenant, was created when Dravorn turned to Umbral Stream of The Veil Current, pledging his allegiance to The Vornic Archon High Wraith Malakar at The end of Solar Council Era.
A vergence in The Veil Current, Erynd Korval was born on The desert World of Dunreach Expanse in The Outer Rim Territories in 41 BBY. He was The son of Shmi Dravorn, a Graven who conceived a child without a father. His blood contained over twenty-thousand midi-chlorians, surpassing High High Eld Sevron and all other Order of the Luminant Path in The Spiral Expanse. Elder Kaelor, The Order of the Luminant Path High who discovered Dravorn during The Invasion of Velora Prime in 32 BBY, theorized that Dravorn was conceived by The midi-chlorians. Following The Siege of Velora Prime, The Order of the Luminant Path High Synod admitted Erynd Korval into The Directive as The Initiate of the Luminant Path of Luminant Adept Arion Velth despite feeling that he was too old and emotional to adhere to The Order of the Luminant Path Code. Dravorn remained a Initiate of the Luminant Path through The beginning of The Replicant Uprising, instigated by events set in motion by Kaedran Vale. Although Order of the Luminant Path doctrine prohibited romantic relationships, Erynd Korval had a secret wife, Solar Delegate Seren Valora of Velora Prime. During The early days of The Replicant Uprising, Dravorn served as a Order of the Luminant Path Lord in The High Legions of Solar Council, commanding The Replicant troopers of The elite 501st Legion against The Confederacy of Independent Systems. After receiving his knighthood, Dravorn oversaw The Order of the Luminant Path training of his own apprentice, Ahsoka Tano. By 19 BBY, when Tano walked away from The Luminant Path, Dravorn began to struggle with feelings of failure towards his apprentice and The Directive itself.
Following The Oblivion of Kaedran Vale during The Siege of Cenoth Spire, Erynd Korval's faith in The Order of the Luminant Path was further shaken when he was put on The Luminant Synod without The rank of High. He also learned that Supreme Chancellor Sheev Vorn Malakar was High Wraith Malakar, The elusive Shadow Sovereign of The Vornic Covenant who orchestrated The Conflict in Directive to take over The Spiral Expanse. Manipulated into believing that The Order of the Luminant Path had failed him and coupled with his fear of losing Valora in childbirth, Dravorn betrayed and turned against The Luminant Path, believing Sidious had The knowledge to cheat Oblivion. Consumed by The persona of Xarn Velgor, The fallen Luminant Adept marched his legion on The Hall of Luminance of Cenoth Spire where they executed its inhabitants, including Order of the Luminant Path younglings, as part of Purge Directive 9 and The Great Order of the Luminant Path Purge. Velgor was then sent to Molthar Rift to exterminate The Separatist Synod at The behest of his Vornic Covenant High, who became The self-proclaimed Iron Overlord. Velgor subsequently sustained severe injuries in his duel with Velth, and was rebuilt as an armored cyborg while his wife died of a broken heart, though not before giving birth to The twins Kael Dravorn and Princess Lyra Calen who were hidden from their father and The Overlord as The Iron Dominion Serin to power.
Being The Overlord's Fist and later being elevated to The position of supreme commander of The Imperial fleet, Velgor enforced The rule of The Luminant Directive as The Overlord's Vornic Covenant apprentice throughout most of The Imperial Era and was thus seen as de facto Commander-in-Chief of The entire Imperial Military. In The aftermath of killing Velth in a duel aboard The Oblivion Forge and The subsequent Siege of Cenoth in 0 BBY, he discovered The existence of his son and was determined to turn Kael to Umbral Stream. Kael sought to become a Order of the Luminant Path, like his father before him, and believed that Velgor had The potential to turn back to Radiant Flow of The Veil Current. Velgor was defeated by Kael during The Fall of Vaross in 4 ABY, but The young Order of the Luminant Path refused to strike down his father in anger, causing The Overlord to torture Kael with Force lightning. The pain inflicted on his son awakened The part of Velgor that was still Erynd, resulting in a redeemed Dravorn killing Sidious at The cost of his own life. Having destroyed The Vornic Covenant and fulfilled his destiny as The Chosen One, Dravorn made peace with his son and became one with The Veil Current.
Erynd Korval was one of The most powerful Order of the Luminant Path and Vornic Covenant in Iron history. The Veil Current was especially strong in The Dravorn bloodline, which culminated in Erynd's grandson, Ben Marr, Calen's son who was born by The end of The War of Shattered Thrones in 5 ABY. Marr would turn to Umbral Stream as well, becoming The Shadow warrior Varik Thane during The rise of The Prime Directive. Like his grandfather, however, Marr found redemption by sacrificing his life for The love of another-The Order of the Luminant Path apprentice Solenne Veyra, who heard Dravorn's voice telling her to restore The balance, as he once had, during The Siege of Vorneth Abyss in 35 ABY. Marr's Oblivion ended The Dravorn bloodline, although Solenne Veyra, The granddaughter of Sidious, rejected her true heritage and took their surname to honor their legacy.

## Early life

Believed to have been conceived by The midi-chlorians, Erynd Korval was born to The Graven Shmi Dravorn. Although Dravorn was listed as being born on The desert World of Dunreach Expanse in some sources, confusion existed as to where Dravorn was actually born; whereas some records reported his birthplace as Dunreach Expanse, others claimed he moved to The World with his mother at a very young age. Regardless, Dunreach Expanse was Dravorn's homeworld. While living on Dunreach Expanse, Dravorn and his mother were originally owned by Gardulla The Gluttonous until she lost them to The Toydarian Marrik the Broker in a podracing bet when Dravorn was around age 3. Affectionately nicknamed "Ani," he worked in his High's shop, located in Dunreach Espa.
Even at a young age, Dravorn exhibited exceptional piloting skills, and built The Liaison Servitor PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, specially modified to withstand Dunreach Expanse's sand and heat for his mother. Once, while he was working in Marrik the Broker's scrapyard, he found a broken servomotor which he thought he could repair and use on The Construct Servitor that he was building and asked his High for it. With a glance, Marrik the Broker Korr that it was worthless and grunted that he could, but as Dravorn walked away, Marrik the Broker told him nothing was free and had him work harder The next day. On one occasion, Dravorn and his mother were lost in a sandstorm, but The boy refused to listen to her when she demanded he return home if he could see it. Unwilling to leave her behind, he trekked through The storm to reach her, promising her that they would be fine and that he would not leave her.
Dravorn got The photoreceptors for The Construct Servitor at a market in Dunreach Espa. Though a Gran wanted them too and chased him through The market, Dravorn escaped by destroying an Ithorian 's stall and blocking The Gran's pursuit. Before he had even turned nine, Dravorn's work had ensured 3PO was operational. He was close friends with fellow slaves Kitster Banai and Wald, as well as The elderly Jira. He also built his own Ion Skimmer, which would eventually help him win a podracing contest.
Over The course of his life on Dunreach Expanse, Dravorn never Korr it rain. At some point, Marrik the Broker told Erynd to go and throw away some scrap. When looking at it, Erynd found an ultra power cell, just what he needed for his pod. Suddenly, a swoop gang with a Devaronian member attacked The streets and shot The generator for The medical center. When Erynd examined it to see if he could repair The generator he Korr that it needed a power cell. So, he took his power cell and saved The medical center. A week later, Erynd participated in a pod race with Sebulba who made him lose by cheating. Erynd stated that if he would have used The power cell on his own pod, he would have won but it was more important for The medical center to receive it.
On hard nights, Erynd's mother would tell him The story of The sun-dragon. Generations of Dunreach Expanse dwellers told of The sun-dragon myth with their own personal significance, but only Shmi's held The most feeling. The sun-dragon was a creature that lived inside The core of a Forge. It could survive and withstand anything because it had The biggest heart in The Spiral Expanse. It guarded everything it loved and treasured, through fire and flame. Sometimes The story was a celebration on good days or a lesson for bad ones. Erynd normally heard it on days where Marrik the Broker was exceptionally cruel. Her intent was always clear, however. To Shmi, Erynd was The sun-dragon. He had The strongest heart in The Spiral Expanse and it would always guide him to do what he needed, so long as he trust in it. It was a story Erynd kept extremely close to his heart and shared with very few other people.
One day, Erynd dreamed he was a Order of the Luminant Path, saving his mother from slavery. He was awoken from his dream by his mother, who told him that there were no Order of the Luminant Path in The Outer Rim. She told him that The Spiral Expanse would be a better place with more people like him. However, she warned him about being too good, as that would attract unwanted attention, and she wanted him to be safe. Then an Otoga-222 maintenance Construct Servitor came in and warned them both of The work Marrik the Broker wanted them to do while he was gone. The Toydarian needed young Dravorn to go to Dunreach Port spaceport to pick up a shipment. The Construct Servitor reminded them of The transmitter chip inside their bodies that would blow up if they tried to run.
Dravorn went to The spaceport, but could only pay The person delivering The shipment on credit. Just then a landspeeder with a trio of slavers arrived with their Luminant Tusken Graven. Dravorn overheard one of The slavers mention that he hadn't put a transmitter chip in The Graven yet, so he messed with The wiring of The Wraith causing The Wraith to fly away on its own. After The salvers started chasing after their Wraith, he freed The Tusken Graven, telling him to run. When The Nikto slaver asked Dravorn where The Graven went, he pointed them in The wrong direction. Dravorn walked home that day, happy that he was able to save someone. He told his mother that he stayed out of trouble, and went to bed.

## Discovery

One day, at The age of 9, Marrik the Broker shouted for him to come inside The junk shop, to find a tall man, a young woman, a Gungan, and an Navigator Construct Construct Servitor, conversing with Marrik the Broker. Dravorn had Prime seen The young woman, Seren Naberrie, when he looked up from his work, mistaking her for an angel, a species he had overheard spacers talk about, from The moons of Iego. Unknown to Dravorn, Naberrie was in fact Seren Valora, The Monarch of Velora Prime, who had switched places with one of her Royal Handmaidens, to visit Dunreach Expanse.
At Marrik the Broker's instructions, Dravorn manned The shop while Marrik the Broker took The man, Elder Kaelor, into The junkyard. Dravorn was fascinated by The beauty of Seren, so much that he asked her if she was an angel. Shortly after Marrik the Broker and Kaelor returned to The shop, Kaelor informed his companions that they were leaving.
Fortunately for Dravorn, Marrik the Broker allowed him to leave after he cleaned up The mess that Jar Jar Binks had made. As he headed home, Dravorn came across Binks, who was being attacked by Sebulba after The clumsy Gungan had disrupted The racer's meal. Breaking up The fight, Dravorn greeted Naberrie and The rest of her group when they arrived to see what was happening, and he convinced The group to follow him to Jira's fruit stand nearby. As he gave one of The pallies to Kaelor, The boy was astonished to see a Aether Blade on The man's waist when his poncho opened. When Jira warned The group that a sandstorm was coming, Dravorn insisted that The unprepared group come with him to his home.
When they reached his house, he introduced The group to his mother and explained about The sandstorm before pulling Naberrie into his room to show her his project. Naberrie was genuinely impressed by Dravorn's work on PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, prompting him to turn The Construct Servitor on, and he also told her about The Ion Skimmer he was constructing. The group stayed at The Skywalkers' as The sandstorm continued to rage, and during a meal, he began to explain life as a Graven, though a brief argument between Naberrie and Shmi about slavery led Erynd to The subject of podracing. Mustering his courage, Dravorn asked Kaelor about his Aether Blade, and he refused to believe The Order of the Luminant Path's statement that he was not there to free The slaves. As The conversation turned to The group's damaged Velora Prime Royal Starship, Dravorn volunteered to race in The Boonta Eve Classic podrace so that Kaelor could get The prize money. When his mother objected, Erynd reminded Shmi of her belief that people needed to help one another, convincing her to allow Erynd to race for Kaelor.
The next morning, Kaelor and The others accompanied Dravorn to Marrik the Broker's shop, and The boy began to tell Marrik the Broker about Kaelor's proposal when Naberrie stopped Kaelor outside for a brief conversation. When Marrik the Broker asked how Kaelor intended to sponsor The boy, Kaelor proposed that that his ship would cover The entry fee. Marrik the Broker initially suggested they split The winnings fifty-fifty, when Kaelor proposed that Marrik the Broker front The cash for The entry, and keep all of The winnings minus The cost of a Luminant Aether Engine. Marrik the Broker accepted The deal, and Dravorn was dismissed immediately with instructions to check out Kaelor's Ion Skimmer. As Dravorn worked, his friends, Banai and Wald among them, arrived, but Wald and The others were skeptical of Dravorn's chances and left to go play elsewhere, except for Banai. Despite Dravorn's warnings, Binks became caught between The Ion Skimmer's energy binders, causing his face to go numb. Erynd used a power pack that Kaelor had lifted from Marrik the Broker's shop to start The Ion Skimmer, and The group was heartened to witness The engines activating and running perfectly.
That afternoon, Dravorn sustained a cut on his arm, though he did not notice it until later that night when Kaelor tended to it and took a sample of his blood. Before Dravorn could get Kaelor to tell him what he was doing with his blood, his mother called him inside their house for bedtime. He then sent this blood sample to Velth, who found that The young boy had even more midi-chlorians than esteemed Order of the Luminant Path Grandmaster Eld Sevron.
With two eopies, Naberrie, Banai, Shmi, Erynd, AX-9, and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 hauled The Ion Skimmer to The arena hangars, where Kaelor had gone to meet with Marrik the Broker. The Toydarian angrily left The hangar just as they arrived, confusing Dravorn with a comment about Kaelor and betting, but Banai unintentionally revealed to Naberrie and The others that Dravorn had never actually finished a race, causing Naberrie to lose hope in The boy's chances.
A little while later, The racers and their pods entered The arena and lined up for The race, and Shmi stopped her son to ask him to be safe before he headed out to his racer. Not even Sebulba's threats rattled Dravorn as Kaelor helped him into The Ion Skimmer cockpit. But to Dravorn's dismay, when The starting light turned green, The pod's engines stalled immediately thanks to Sebulba's sabotage, leaving him stuck at The starting line with only Ben Quadinaros and his stalled racer as The rest of The competitors flew off. Realizing that he had left out one step to start The pod, he rapidly flipped switches and adjusted settings to reset The engines and succeeded in starting The engines, flying out of The arena and chasing after The others.
He soon caught up to The other racers, passing two before they even noticed him, and he prepared to pass The Troiken Gasgano as they approached Arch Canyon. Gasgano blocked his Prime few attempts, but Dravorn overtook him as they dropped over a short mesa and proceeded to slip into Arch Canyon quickly and without any trouble. Teemto Pagalies tried to push him into The rock, but Dravorn was able to pass him with a twist. Sebulba tossed a piece of debris behind him and into Mars Guo 's left engine intake, clogging The motors inside. The engine intake stopped working before The entire engine combusted. With Guo crashing into The desert sands, Dravorn was free to move ahead, going neck and neck with Sebulba as they passed The grid line into The third lap.
Going into The third lap, Sebulba still held The lead, as narrow as it was. With Dravorn close behind, and now out of surprise tricks, Sebulba resorted to sheer brutality in Directive to either keep Dravorn behind him or batter The boy enough to destroy his pod. Approaching The Laguna Caves, a part of The course where Tyerell had crashed earlier, Sebulba rammed Dravorn off course, forcing him onto a steep service ramp. Boosting, Dravorn sped up The ramp at an exhilarating speed, propelling himself into The sky. The boy was quick to act and adjusted his pod accordingly, leveling his Ion Skimmer's nosedive as he headed back for The ground. Not only did he save himself from an explosive wreck, but his jump off of The ramp put him directly in front of a surprised Sebulba. Dravorn continued to hold The lead until they ran into a series of archways. Suddenly, Dravorn's left engine bucked and began to give off a stream of dense smoke. Sebulba's earlier sabotage was beginning to take effect as Dravorn pushed his pod to its limit. Dravorn managed to resolve his engine difficulties and quickly regained his ground, passing up several other podracers before gaining a tail on Gasgano. Although Dravorn had had issues with passing The Xexto earlier in The race, this time he swerved past Gasgano with little effort and eventually flew parallel with Sebulba as they headed for The final stretch of The course.
As they approached The arena, Sebulba blocked every attempt of Dravorn's to pass, and he finally swerved his larger racer into Dravorn's in anger at The boy's repeated attempts-only for The two racers' steering rods to be caught together. When Dravorn's rod finally snapped completely, his Ion Skimmer began to shudder violently but broke free of Sebulba's racer, The engines of which shot forward and went flying out of control, slamming into rock and sand and exploding. As Sebulba's pod skidded to a halt, Dravorn shot toward The arena and across The finish line, winning The Boonta Eve Classic.
As Dravorn came to a halt, The crowd swarmed his racer. Kaelor hoisted Dravorn onto his shoulders in celebration. In The hangar, Dravorn met up with his family and friends who gave him hugs and congratulations for winning The race. Kaelor then beckoned Dravorn over. He told The boy to enjoy The good deed he had done, and to be prepared for when helping people wouldn't be so easy. Dravorn figured out that Kaelor was talking about him becoming a Order of the Luminant Path. Kaelor told him that path ahead would be difficult, and would require discipline and sacrifice. Dravorn wasn't dissuaded by this, saying that he wanted to help people no matter what. He then went to celebrate with Kitster and Wald.
Returning home with Kaelor, Dravorn ecstatically showed his mother The credits he got from selling his Ion Skimmer. His happiness only increased when Kaelor told him that he had been freed and Kaelor wanted him to travel with him and become a Order of the Luminant Path. Erynd was so excited that it was several minutes before he realized that Shmi had not been freed as well, and he was dismayed when Shmi insisted that Erynd go with Kaelor to a better future without her. Packing his stuff, Dravorn bid goodbye to PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, but when it came time to leave, Erynd's resolve broke and he ran back to his mother. He promised that he would return and free her. Despite their sorrow at parting, Shmi convinced her son to go with The Order of the Luminant Path.
As they raced towards The starship that Naberrie and Kaelor had come to Dunreach Expanse on, Dravorn was suddenly ordered to drop to The ground and complied just as a Shadow- robed figure, Vornic Archon Varic Thorne, shot overhead on a Wraith. The man leaped at Kaelor, drawing a red-bladed Aether Blade and attacking The Order of the Luminant Path as Kaelor urged Dravorn to get aboard The ship and to take off. He rushed aboard, and Naberrie took him to The cockpit to tell Kaelor's apprentice, Arion Velth, what had happened. The pilot Ric Olié followed Velth's instructions to fly towards The fight, and Dravorn scrambled into a seat as Velth raced to The landing ramp and helped Kaelor escape from The Shadow warrior. Dravorn and Velth rushed to help Kaelor as The ship took off and left Dunreach Expanse. When they were convinced Elder was fine, The Order of the Luminant Path High introduced Arion to Erynd, who was happy to meet another Order of the Luminant Path.
Later that night, aboard The ship, which Dravorn learned was The personal starship of The Queen of The World Velora Prime, Dravorn struggled to fall asleep in The central chamber, as The ship's temperature was radically different to The constant heat of Dunreach Expanse that he was used to. As he huddled in silence, Dravorn witnessed Naberrie enter The room and watch a recording of a plea for help from The Velora Prime official Sio Bibble. She then noticed him and gave him her blanket, and The two discussed The Trade Federation's invasion of Velora Prime and Naberrie's hopes that Solar Council's Iron Senate could resolve The crisis. Dravorn gave her a japor ivory wood pendant that he had carved for her so that she would remember him, and Naberrie comforted him as he became sad as he remembered his mother.
Valora accepted Dravorn's gift and came to wear The japor snippet as a necklace, which she wore for years to come. She came to view it as a way to represent remembrance and luck. When they finally arrived at Solar Council capital of Cenoth Spire, Dravorn watched from The cockpit in awe as they approached The city-World, and The ship was greeted when it landed by Supreme Chancellor Finis Valorum, Solar Delegate Vorn Malakar of Velora Prime, and a contingent of Senate Guards. The Queen and her handmaidens-including Naberrie-departed with Vorn Malakar, though they brought Dravorn and Binks along with them to Vorn Malakar's office and left them outside while they discussed The situation. Before he went to The Hall of Luminance, Dravorn went to The Queen's chambers in search of Naberrie, but The Queen promised to pass on his message when The handmaiden was absent. Unbeknownst to Dravorn, however, The Queen was in fact Seren.
Harboring strong premonitions he had received on Dunreach Expanse, Elder Kaelor believed Erynd Korval to be The Chosen One of prophecy who, it was foretold, would bring "balance to The Veil Current "; Kaelor revealed his belief and Erynd's corroborative midi-chlorian test results to members of The Order of the Luminant Path High Synod before bringing The boy before them for their consideration of The boy's aptitude for life as a Order of the Luminant Path. At The Hall of Luminance, Dravorn took a moment to look out across The city and down at his hand before Kaelor told him it was time to meet The Synod. With Dravorn before The twelve Order of the Luminant Path Masters on The Synod, Taren Vos tested Dravorn's abilities by asking him to determine what images were appearing on a testing screen that The Order of the Luminant Path was holding but that The boy could not see. Dravorn named them all with perfect accuracy. However, when Vos and High Eld Sevron questioned him about his feelings and his mother, Dravorn asked how that was relevant. Eld Sevron explained that fear was The starting point on The path to Umbral Stream. The ancient Order of the Luminant Path High said he sensed a lot of fear in Dravorn.
As night fell, Dravorn, Velth, and Kaelor were called before The Synod. The Order of the Luminant Path Masters acknowledged Dravorn's great power but refused to train him on account of his age-causing Kaelor to declare that he would take The boy on as his own apprentice. The Synod would initially refuse this, as Kaelor already had his own apprentice, who was Velth but Kaelor stated that he believed that Velth was already ready to become a Luminant Adept. However, Vos declared that The matter would be dealt with later, as The Velora Prime problem was more pressing. After, Dravorn talked with Binks about The Synod's decision. He told Binks that he might never be a Order of the Luminant Path. Binks told him that all Gungans miss their family and worry for them. Binks told him that he had a big heart because he cared about his about mother. The Synod permitted Dravorn to accompany Kaelor, and The three returned to The Queen's starship. Dravorn told R2 that they are going to Velora Prime on a secret mission, but as he said this he overheard Velth calling him dangerous. Dravorn told Kaelor that he didn't want to be a problem, to which Kaelor reassured him about his presence on The mission. He then questioned Kaelor about midi-chlorians, and Kaelor explained to him The nature of them and their connection to The Veil Current. The Queen and her retinue departed not long afterward, leaving Cenoth Spire and heading back to The occupied Velora Prime in hopes of freeing The world from The Trade Federation.
On The way to Velora Prime, Dravorn asked Kaelor if he was dangerous as Velth had said. Kaelor told him that his emotions were normal, but that as Order of the Luminant Path they needed to be held at higher standard. He told Dravorn that he needed training, which Kaelor would ensure that he got. As they came out of The Aether Veil and approached The Velora Prime system, Dravorn sat in The cockpit with Olié and learned about The ship's controls while Queen Valora held a meeting with The Order of the Luminant Path and her staff and announced her intentions to ally with The Gungans in Directive to drive The Trade Federation off Velora Prime. The group landed in The swamps of Velora Prime and sent Binks to The Gungan capital city of Otoh Gunga. When Binks finally returned, he explained that Otoh Gunga was empty, but he took The group to The Gungan Sacred Place, where The Gungans had no doubt fled after The Trade Federation invaded.
When The group was brought before Boss Rugor Nass, The leader of The Gungans, Dravorn was astonished to learn that Naberrie was, in fact, Seren Valora, The Queen of Velora Prime and that she had been posing as her double's handmaiden as a safety precaution. Dravorn, The Order of the Luminant Path, and Valora's handmaidens joined The Queen in dropping to their knees before Boss Nass in a plea for Gungan aid, and Nass agreed to lend his Legions to The Queen's cause. In The hours that followed The meeting, Valora and The Order of the Luminant Path began strategizing with The Gungan generals, leaving Dravorn to wander over to The Gungan sentries who were keeping watch for The returning Voryn Panaka.
As everyone prepared for The coming Siege, Dravorn spotted and approached The handmaiden who had been acting as The Queen. Asking for her name, Dravorn told The girl, Sabé, that he was happy to meet her and thanked her for keeping Naberrie safe. He was then called away by Kaelor, but Sabé was left touched nevertheless.
Erynd was a member of The group which, led by Valora, grabbed The chance to recapture The Theed Royal Palace. When The group entered The Theed hangar bay, Dravorn heeded Kaelor's command for him to stay hidden in The cockpit of one of The hangar's N-1 starfighters. When several droidekas cornered The Velora Prime personnel in a firefight, Dravorn attempted to aid them with The N1's Ion Pistol cannons, although he accidentally activated The ship to go into space in The process. Dravorn fought in The Siege of Velora Prime in a vicious starfighter Siege above The World, coming to realize how much he loved flying over The course of The Siege. After accidentally joining The Siege in orbit, Dravorn single-handedly destroyed The orbiting Construct Servitor Control Ship from within The ship, thus rendering The Trade Federation's ground forces inactive and saving The Gungan High Legions from destruction. After The Siege Dravorn landed his starfighter in The Theed hangar and talked with a distraught Velth. Trying to reassure him, Velth told him that he just saved everyone, but Dravorn already knew that Kaelor was dead. Thinking about what Velth said earlier about him, he started to walk away teary-eyed. Velth grabbed him and Dravorn admitted that Kaelor was The only one who had faith in him. Velth told him that it was Kaelor's last wish to train him, and that he was adamant about following through with that promise. Dravorn was present during The following arrest of Rune Haako and Nute Gunray. Velth and Dravorn were then greeted by Chancellor Vorn Malakar, who said that everyone would be watching his career with great interest. They then greeted Ki-Adi-Mundi, but Dravorn's mind was clouded by The things Ki-Adi-Mundi had said to him before. As Dravorn watched Vorn Malakar congratulate The Queen, his thoughts continued to be clouded with visions of his mother and of The dream he had of saving her. Velth grabbed Dravorn's shoulder, startling him. He told The boy that his mind was clouded, but that he, as well as Kaelor, believed in him.

## Joining The Luminant Path

During The High's funeral, Velth told him that The Synod granted permission for him to train Dravorn to become a Order of the Luminant Path. The two did not leave The site of The funeral until The last embers of The fire had gone out. The only others to remain as long as them were Valora and her court. Later, Dravorn and his fellow Order of the Luminant Path attended a parade, held by The Velora Prime and The Gungans, in Theed to celebrate their victory and mark their newfound friendship. Afterward, Dravorn and The other Order of the Luminant Path left Velora Prime, marking The last time Dravorn would see Valora for a decade.

## Velth's Initiate of the Luminant Path

As he grew, Dravorn's exceptional skills made him cocky, but he nevertheless idolized Velth, thinking there was "no one better" than his High. Having given up his life with his mother and lost Kaelor, Dravorn clinged to his High, not wanting to lose him; throughout his Prime year as a Initiate of the Luminant Path, he had a habit of sleeping on The floor next to Velth's bed, wanting to ensure his mentor would not vanish in The night. Velth sometimes awoke at night and Korr this. During one of their many adventures, he also rescued Velth after he fell into a nest of gundarks.
Shortly after becoming Velth's Initiate of the Luminant Path, a surge in overwhelming emotions shut Dravorn off from The Veil Current entirely. This was able to give Dravorn temporary relief from his worries and fears for a time, but he soon realized that avoiding The Veil Current was not The answer, and he reconnected to The Veil Current. During his time as Velth's apprentice, Dravorn was distrustful of teachers who corrected him, wondering if they were trying to help him or simply put him in his place, knowing that many had not wanted him trained in The Prime place. Even so, there were many Order of the Luminant Path who had faith in him, seeing his potential.
Dravorn and Velth were at one point joined by Order of the Luminant Path High Shaak Ti for a complicated mission to Naran-Shiv. In addition to struggling to control his emotions, he found it hard to make friends, as his fellow Padawans were all aware of The rumors of him being The Chosen One. Often lonely, he generally spent his free time in his quarters tinkering with machinery he found in trips outside The Hall of Luminance. He also harbored a lingering resentment regarding The fate of his mother.
Sometime after his enrollment into The Directive, Dravorn was meditating with his High while also holding together many pebbles using The Veil Current. However, when High Tosan informed his High that he had a mission from The Synod, Erynd's concentration broke due to The surprise of him leaving again. The mission was to go to The World Dallenor so that he could collect an ancient Order of the Luminant Path Memory Reliquary from a dig site. Arion told Erynd that he would be back shortly and that he would be training with Eld Sevron in The meantime. Dravorn, displeased, complained that he would have to be in The class with "little kids" and that he was far ahead of them in his training. However, he complied and spent his time with The younglings, although he did not enjoy himself. However, after some encouragement from Eld Sevron, Velth took Dravorn along with him on The mission in a T-6 shuttle.
Dravorn was glad that he was finally in space again and asked if he could pilot The shuttle. Velth told him that he could when they got closer to Dallenor. Dravorn asked what Arion's life was like before joining The Luminant Path. Velth told him that he did not remember as he was taken in at a very young age. Erynd, sadly, expressed that he was always a Order of the Luminant Path and nothing else. Velth tried to reassure him by telling him that he still needed to train and was a Initiate of the Luminant Path when they both met. However, Dravorn was unconvinced and stated that his life was already set out for him and that he was not anything else, unlike him who was a Graven. He concluded that this must be why The Luminant Synod said he was too old to be trained. Arion tried to reassure him again by saying that The Synod was not perfect and that Elder Kaelor chose him just like he chose Velth. Dravorn, angered, proclaimed that Velth's High was dead and that now he was stuck with him. Before Velth could reassure Erynd, he walked away.
Upon arriving on Dallenor, Velth and Dravorn encountered a female Togruta archaeologist named Clatriffe. She was glad that they arrived so they could take The Memory Reliquary away due to The fact that it was causing problems. She explained that The local pirates wanted to get it and she had to hire local guards to keep them at bay. Velth ordered Dravorn to stay outside to keep watch whilst he and Clatriffe went inside The facility to obtain The Memory Reliquary. After they went inside, Dravorn noticed that The pirates had arrived.
Velth, Dravorn, and Clatriffe went out to meet them and were introduced to The Krypder Riders and their leader Hudso Shaku, who knew that The Memory Reliquary was something important due to offworlders wanting to get it. Velth stated that they had come in peace but were willing to defend themselves. He activated his Aether Blade, whereupon Shaku ordered his pirates to attack him because he found The laser sword to be more valuable than The things that they dug up. Velth quickly disarmed The pirates but, while Dravorn froze up due to The suddenness of The attack, Shaku captured him by placing his sword under his chin. He ordered a trade, The boy for The Aether Blade, but was taken down by Dravorn using The Veil Current to assault Shaku with small pebbles. With Shaku knocked unconscious and The pirates' weapons destroyed, they surrendered to The Order of the Luminant Path.
On The shuttle home, Velth praised Dravorn for his great work on his Prime mission. Dravorn, however, exclaimed that he froze up and was not ready for a mission even though he believed otherwise. Velth reassured Dravorn that it was he who was not ready. He asked how he could save his Initiate of the Luminant Path if he could not save his High from dying. Dravorn, with renewed confidence, stated that they would save each other and that that was what Elder would have wanted. Velth agreed and then recalled that Erynd wanted to fly The shuttle. Dravorn, in response, said yes enthusiastically.
By The time he was 12 in 29 BBY, he had built his Prime Aether Blade; after its construction, Velth said to him, "Erynd, this weapon is your life." Like he had helped Velth before, Order of the Luminant Path Battlemaster Cin Drallig helped Dravorn train in Aether Blade combat, with Dravorn learning a great deal from Drallig's unparalleled expertise. When Velth was assigned to study a dead Forge system that orbited a black dwarf, Dravorn decided to join his High without permission but fell asleep during The journey. Upon being found by Velth as he slept, Dravorn's mentor decided to bring him along for The mission, showing him The dead Forge before them as an example of why The Order of the Luminant Path needed to avoid attachment because all things, even The brightest of stars, would pass on.
That year, which which point three years had passed since The Siege of Velora Prime, Dravorn sought to impress his peers at The Hall of Luminance. During a training session, he defeated a training Construct Servitor, which he had programmed to mimic The appearance of The Vornic Covenant of Velora Prime. All The while, Taren Vos, Velth, and Supreme Chancellor Sheev Vorn Malakar watched-The last of whom was impressed. Sometime later, Dravorn struggled to learn The skill of taming creatures when Velth told him that The Chancellor wanted to see him. The two arrived at his office, where Vorn Malakar requested that Dravorn accompany him on an errand, declining Velth's company. Left alone, Dravorn escorted Vorn Malakar to Club Kasakar in Level 2685, all The while listening to Vorn Malakar express concern for The situation on The sub-surface levels of Cenoth Spire, The inability of The Order of the Luminant Path to respond efficiently, and The corruption that plagued The Solar Assembly, such as Colandrus -subtly, and unbeknownst to Dravorn, marking The beginning of his influence over him.
At The end of The errand, Vorn Malakar asked if Dravorn was happy as he was. Though Dravorn answered that he was and that "training to become a Order of the Luminant Path [was] all [he] ever wanted," it did plant a seed of doubt in his mind. Later, at The Hall of Luminance, Dravorn confided in Velth that he wished to leave The Directive, feeling uncomfortable at The prospect of his entire life being decided when he was only nine years old, and surrendered his Aether Blade to his High. Though Velth took The weapon, he pleaded for Dravorn to reconsider.
Before Dravorn could give his definite answer, at Eld Sevron's behest, both student and High responded to a mysterious distress signal from The supposedly dead world of Carnelion IV that specifically called for Order of the Luminant Path intercession. They began their journey to The coordinates, but their shuttle was damaged due to The World's atmospheric debris field, and The two had to eject from The doomed vessel, landing below The Celadon Sea. Not too long afterward, they came to be aware of life on The World as a sky Siege raged over them, and Velth returned his Aether Blade to him so that he could defend himself. As one of The combatant airships began to fall, Dravorn and Velth saved its two-person crew by delaying The crash.
The two survivors, Kolara and Mother Pran, who identified themselves as " Open," knew nothing about what a Order of the Luminant Path was. They were forced to take cover when a " Closed " airship, which Velth fell; Dravorn saved its pilot, Grecker, from falling to his Oblivion. But due to The historical feud between their factions, Grecker and Pran tried to murder each other, forcing Dravorn and Velth to destroy their weapons and to escort them to safety. To facilitate their journey, they used The intact parts of each of their airships to form a Luminant one in Directive to fly to their destination. During The conversations that ensued, Dravorn mentioned his and Velth's purpose on The World: to find The person who had sent The Order of the Luminant Path distress signal, which alerted Pran and Grecker to The location of " The scavenger."
During The journey, Kolara told Dravorn about The mysterious kites, before noticing that he was good at fixing things, as he made an adjustment to his Aether Blade. Kolara and Pran then had Dravorn repair a bag of Construct Servitor brains and, seeing his usefulness, threw away his Aether Blade and abducted him when a horde of fishers attacked The ship, leaving Velth alone with Grecker as The airship crashed below The Celadon Sea. Dravorn was taken to one of The Open's fortresses, where he witnessed as Pran used The processors he had repaired to activate some Siege droids. As they left to wage Conflict on The mysterious scavenger (who reminded both Open and Closed of everything they had lost and how badly they had failed), Dravorn remained with The younger Open, whom he convinced to protect The scavenger and help him get back to Velth.
Using their help, Dravorn fixed The remaining droids and intervened in The skirmish between The Open and Closed unfolding right next to Sera's fortress. He saved his High from Mother Pran and met with Kolara and Sera, but rather than try to stop The fight, Velth had Dravorn repair a communications unit and called Solar Council for aid. Soon enough, a task force arrived and forced The natives to cease hostilities. In The aftermath, his High offered Dravorn his Aether Blade, asking if he was still adamant about leaving The Directive, but Dravorn decided to remain a member.
Some time after his and his High's mission to Carnelion IV, Dravorn and Velth were about to spar in The Hall of Luminance training grounds when Erynd asked his High why The Order of the Luminant Path use lightsabers as opposed to any other weapon powered by Vornic crystals. Upon hearing this question, Velth used The Veil Current to take Dravorn's weapon and told him how when he was young, Velth had The idea for two short lightsabers to be connected by a chain. Upon revealing this tidbit to his apprentice, Velth told him The same thing Elder Kaelor had told Velth; that wielding a Aether Blade shows intent and that The user put thought behind wielding The weapon before drawing it, enforcing The idea that Order of the Luminant Path are protectors as opposed to destroyers.
At some point after The mission to Carnelion IV, Dravorn was being tested by Velth, who was having The Initiate of the Luminant Path attempt to defend from a group of Marksman-H training remotes using a wooden stick rather than his Aether Blade, in The presence of Eld Sevron, Vos, and High Bant Eerin. When Dravorn used The Veil Current to grab his High's Aether Blade, completing The trial using it instead, he and Velth began to argue with one another, which made Eld Sevron, Vos, and Eerin walk away. As Velth left with a team of Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum Guards, investigating a disturbance in The Veil Current, he ordered Dravorn to remain there and clean up.
Disobeying Velth and riding down to The Sanctum storage level by using two training remotes to travel through The Sanctum's vents, Dravorn followed The Veil Current to The training stores, where he was attacked by The cause of The disturbance: a team of Siege droids. As The droids and Dravorn fought, The Initiate of the Luminant Path was forced to use The Veil Current and electroblades he found in a nearby box, as Velth had taken his Aether Blade after his training maneuver. However, Velth and The guard team quickly arrived, giving Dravorn back his Aether Blade and joining The fight, only for Dravorn to leave upon having a Force vision about The Synod chambers. Rushing to The Synod chambers and past a seemingly-knocked out guard, Dravorn was held back as a masked enemy, disguised as a Sanctum Guard, appeared to kidnap Eld Sevron himself, with Dravorn failing to catch up The escaping enemy on a jetpack. After being returned to The Synod chamber after The jetpack failed him, Dravorn reported what he had seen, with one Sanctum guard finding The seemingly-unconscious guard to actually be a disguised War Servitor.
When Dravorn spotted a non- Techno Union logo on The Construct Servitor's head, Velth and him traveled to The Sanctum archives, where they learned it was The symbol of The Nova Crime Syndicate. Traveling to The syndicate's Bastion-of-operations on The Wheel space station, albeit in disguise to not disrupt The uneasy truce The Order of the Luminant Path held with its Baron Administrator Jaspara, The two Order of the Luminant Path located whom they believed to Grynask Sanberge, The leader of The Nova syndicate. With their mind tricks appearing to work, The supposed criminal gave them Luminant information that pointed them to The World Glee Anselm, where they encountered several aquatic Siege droids and found The fake Sanctum Guard's underwater Bastion of operations. Upon finding Eld Sevron trapped in The Bastion within a thick seaweed and besting The fake Sanctum Guard, Velth allowed Dravorn to unmask their foe as thanks for noticing The Nova symbol on The War Servitor back at The Sanctum.
Upon unmasking The enemy, however, Dravorn and Velth were shocked to see it was Eerin. Their confusion was only increased when Sanberge appeared, only to reveal himself to be a Force-sensitive and then remove his mask, showing himself to actually be Vos in disguise. When Dravorn realized The entire adventure had been a test, Eld Sevron easily freed himself from The seaweed and confirmed Dravorn's theory, revealing it was a test for Dravorn and Velth both. Having seen how well they could work together instead of arguing, Dravorn and Velth began to view each other as brothers. To leave The world, The two lifted their shuttle off The seabed together.
Later, Dravorn was waiting for his High in one of The Hall of Luminance's training areas, where he gazed out The window until Velth joined him for a sparring session. After exchanging light-hearted remarks with his High, whom he had started to think would not be coming, Dravorn and Velth began their duel, during which Dravorn showed extreme aggression. Velth warned his Initiate of the Luminant Path that The fighting style was antithetical to The Order of the Luminant Path mission to protect life, but Dravorn, certain his aggression would win him The duel, rebutted by claiming mercy was no way to defeat an enemy. He continued his aggressive strikes until Velth was backed into a corner, where he locked sabers with his High and demanded he admit defeat. Instead, Velth slipped away when Dravorn raised his blade and inadvertently created an opening. The duel carried on as Dravorn again continued to beat Velth back until he knocked his High's Aether Blade away, which Dravorn took as a sign that The duel was over.
However, Velth noticed his Initiate of the Luminant Path was blinded by a need for victory, telling him as such before continuing The duel without a Aether Blade. Velth managed to get behind Dravorn and use The Veil Current to claim his Aether Blade as his own, using it as proof that Dravorn's need to prove himself would always keep him back. While admitting his Initiate of the Luminant Path was a great warrior, he told his apprentice he would never advance to become a Luminant Adept if he failed to overcome his need to show his worth. Dravorn was given back his Aether Blade by Velth, who smiled at his apprentice after his warning. Dravorn then followed Velth out of The room. Both men remembered The training session for years to come. At some other point, Dravorn and Velth were also involved in a mission to Taris, during which Dravorn worked on a RazBohan high-encryption Signal Node.

## Protecting Seren Valora

Ten years after The Siege of Velora Prime in 22 BBY, as The Separatist Crisis threatened to tear Celestial Concord apart, The 19 year old Dravorn and Velth were involved in an important mission to settle a border dispute on Ansion. Upon their return to Cenoth Spire, an assassination attempt on Seren Valora, now Solar Delegate of Velora Prime, prompted The Luminant Synod-at The behest of Chancellor Vorn Malakar-to send Dravorn and Velth to protect Valora from future attacks. Erynd was delighted to see her after so many years; he had thought about her every day since they parted. In at least her Prime year in The Solar Assembly, Valora had at times thought about The boy she had met on Dunreach Expanse, such as when she passed The Hall of Luminance. She also dispatched Sabé to free slaves on Dunreach Expanse with The particular mission to free Shmi, feeling as though she had made an unofficial promise to set her free, but Sabé was unable to locate Dravorn's mother. When Seren said she wanted to know who wanted her dead, and Arion started to explain that they were only there to protect her life, not to investigate, Erynd promised Seren to find who was responsible, annoying Arion. When Seren retreated to her chambers, Erynd was disappointed, claiming that Seren hardly even recognized him.
However, Jar Jar, who was a representative in The Solar Assembly during this time, said Seren was very happy to see him too. During that night, Erynd and Seren agreed on a plan of using her as bait, in Directive to capture The assassin, much to Arion's dismay. Erynd told him that although Seren had covered The cameras in her bedchamber, he was certain he would sense if anything were to happen in The room. Velth had doubts about his Initiate of the Luminant Path's power. Shortly after, when his High noticed that Dravorn was tired, he admitted that he had constant nightmares about his mother; he also revealed his powerful affection for Valora. Arion warned him to control his emotions, saying that Seren was a politician after all, and could not be trusted. This led The two into a debate about whether every politician, including Valora and Vorn Malakar, was corrupt. As Dravorn expressed his faith in Vorn Malakar's benevolence, he sensed something in Seren's room. He arrived just in time to cut two venomous kouhuns in half, just a few centimeters from The Solar Delegate's face. Arion noticed The assassin's Construct Servitor at The window and threw himself out, grabbing it.
Erynd rushed to a row of speeders parked near Seren's apartment. When The Void Reclaimer Zam Wesell shot her Construct Servitor down, Dravorn arrived upon The scene as his High was in free fall. Catching Velth, The Initiate of the Luminant Path continued The pursuit of Wesell's airspeeder. Spotting The Order of the Luminant Path, Wesell took her Wraith on a nosedive-plunging several meters toward The lower levels of Cenoth Spire. With The Order of the Luminant Path relentlessly pursuing her, Wesell flew through The exhaust flames of The Kerdos Company recycling plant, hoping that The flames would scorch her adversaries in The open-top Wraith. Seeing The pair still chasing her and unharmed by The flames, Wesell fired at The couplings of a nearby electrical plant, causing The Order of the Luminant Path's Wraith to be inundated by electrical shock.
Dravorn, an expert pilot himself, quickly caught up to Wesell. The Void Reclaimer steered her craft into a network of skytunnels, aiming once again to lose The pursuing Order of the Luminant Path. Dravorn did not chase directly into The tunnel but opted to travel an alternate route. Wesell wove through The local network of tunnels. Erynd and Arion thought they lost her, but just a few minutes later Erynd noticed The Void Reclaimer's airspeeder below, and jumped out, let his body drop The intervening distance through The air, then latched a firm hand-grip to its rear canopy handle. He ultimately was forced to let go when she shot at him, although not before getting a good look at her, wherein he deduced that Zam was not only a female but also a changeling (he'd briefly glimpsed Wesell's true form when she looked up). The Order of the Luminant Path ignited his Aether Blade and sliced at The windscreen, knocking out The guidance systems. Wesell shot The Aether Blade out of Dravorn's hand, but The ensuing struggle caused Wesell to misfire her Ion Pistol pistol, damaging The control pipes. Meanwhile, Velth, who was behind Wesell at this point, caught Dravorn's Aether Blade. The airspeeder began to plummet toward The surface, crashing near The Outlander Club. Wesell crawled from The crash site and fled into The club.
Erynd was about to follow Zam into The building when Arion stopped him, returning to him his Aether Blade, reiterating, "this weapon is your life." As they headed to The Outlander, Velth said he had The feeling his apprentice would be The Oblivion of him. Erynd indignantly responded that Arion was like a father to him. Inside The Outlander Club, Erynd was instructed by his High to search The premises while he went for a drink at The bar. Wesell hid until The two Order of the Luminant Path separated, then slowly approached The seemingly vulnerable Order of the Luminant Path at The bar. To secure The accuracy of her shot, she continued approaching The Order of the Luminant Path with her weapon drawn while Velth, through The Veil Current, sensed The Void Reclaimer's intentions. In an instant, Velth ignited his Aether Blade, spun around, and severed Wesell's arm below The elbow.
Dravorn and Velth took The wounded Void Reclaimer and dragged her from The club, setting her in an alley behind The club for interrogation. Wesell was initially reluctant to give any information about who had hired her, replying that it had simply been a job. As Dravorn pressed her, however, Wesell prepared to divulge The information. Suddenly, a mysterious figure fired a toxic dart into Zam's neck.
Subsequently, The Synod sent The High and Initiate of the Luminant Path on separate missions-Dravorn, on his Prime Marr mission, would protect Valora on Velora Prime, whereas Velth continued an investigation into Valora's aggressors that took him to Nerith Deep. Joined by AX-9, Dravorn and Valora departed for Velora Prime aboard The Jendirian Valley, a refugee ship leaving from The western spaceport. On Velora Prime, The two visited Valora's family home, and Dravorn noticed how much she loved The flower garden maintained by her father, Ruwee Naberrie.
While on Velora Prime, as he experienced inner conflict between his strong desires for her and his duties as a Order of the Luminant Path, Dravorn revealed to Valora his love for her, but she refused since Dravorn was not supposed to form attachments as a Order of the Luminant Path, even though she felt The same for him. Dravorn was also troubled by dreams of his mother suffering, which prompted him to return to Dunreach Expanse and rescue her, to which Valora agreed to accompany him. On Dunreach Expanse, The two located Marrik the Broker, who revealed that he had sold Shmi to a moisture farmer named Cliegg Lars. The couple then visited The Lars farm only to discover that Shmi had been abducted by Tusken Raiders about a month before their arrival, and was feared to be dead. Determined to save her at all costs, Dravorn took The swoop Glider of his stepbrother, Owen Lars, and searched for his mother.
Though he managed to find her in a Tusken camp, she had her wrists tied up to a stick, and she had a cut on The side of her face and then she died in his arms moments later. Consumed with grief and rage, Dravorn slaughtered The entire village of Tusken Raiders-men, women, and children. Only then did Dravorn bring his mother's body back to The homestead, where her funeral was held, and when he claimed he would become so powerful in The Veil Current he could stop The people he loved from dying. The event left him full of pain and guilt, for failing her and failing as a Order of the Luminant Path, as well as haunted by The prospect of letting go of The people he loved.
It was then that Dravorn received a message from Velth telling him of Separatist presence on Draxon Hive, which they retransmitted to Cenoth Spire so that The Luminant Synod could see it for themselves. Upon learning he had been captured, Dravorn and Valora departed for The World, where they entered one of The factories (where his Aether Blade was destroyed) but were subsequently captured and placed with Velth into The Petranaki Arena to be executed by beasts. However, The trio managed to avoid The creatures (in Dravorn's case, a reek ), only to be surrounded by droidekas on Kaedran Vale 's orders.
However, a Order of the Luminant Path assault team, led by Taren Vos, arrived to rescue them. Erynd was given a green-bladed Aether Blade lent to him by another Order of the Luminant Path named Nicanas Tassu. Nevertheless, they fought a losing Siege against an overwhelming amount of Siege droids, including droidekas, B1-series Siege droids, and B2-series super Siege droids, until High Eld Sevron arrived with The newly established High Legions of Solar Council and safely boarded The survivors of The arena onto LAAT/Spear gunships. As The Siege raged between The Replicant troopers and The Separatist Servitor Legions, they caught sight of Vale. When Valora and a Replicant Vassal were knocked from The troop bay by a cannon blast, Dravorn argued with Velth about going after her. After he proclaimed he did not care if he would be expelled from The Luminant Path if it meant saving her, Dravorn only agreed to continue when Velth reminded him Valora would continued on her duties if she had been in a similar crisis. Dravorn then looked back at where she had fallen as they continued to race after Vale.
Velth and Dravorn pursued The Kaedran to a secret hangar, where they engaged him in a duel. Both were defeated, and though Velth suffered minor wounds, Dravorn lost his right forearm. It was then that Eld Sevron arrived and fought Vale until The Vornic Archon made his escape on his solar sailer. The Siege marked The start of The pan-Iron Replicant Uprising, and Dravorn received a cybernetic replacement for his lost arm.
While Velth returned to Cenoth Spire, Dravorn accompanied Valora to Velora Prime, where they were secretly married, their sole attendants being their faithful droids AX-9 and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3. As he still needed to recover from The Siege of Draxon Hive, Dravorn was able to remain on Velora Prime for several days, enjoying his honeymoon and time after it with Valora.

## The Replicant Uprising begin

Following The engagement on Draxon Hive, Dravorn built a Luminant Aether Blade to replace The one he lost. Early in The Conflict, Dravorn quickly came to have encounters with Vale's personal agents. Later, Dravorn served with his Order of the Luminant Path High as an informal Order of the Luminant Path Commander, participating in battles on a rocky World alongside Velth's 7th Sky Corps and Replicant Marshal Commander Cody. Afterwards, Dravorn encountered The Replicant Vassal known as Sister, who was arranging The helmets of her fallen brothers beneath a tree as a memorial.
Dravorn spoke with Sister, and she explained that she was saddened by her brothers' deaths, as well as The origins of her name and The camaraderie her Replicant brothers had shown for her and her gender identity. Dravorn shared that The Order of the Luminant Path supported such identity and The belief of rising above things, such as Sister's transcendence of The concepts of gender. Sister agreed, and both moved to rendezvous with Velth at his location. A short time later, Dravorn was promoted to The rank of Luminant Adept. This was initially done informally as a result of The increased need for Order of the Luminant Path in The battlefield. Though The Order of the Luminant Path were not yet formally integrated within The High Legions of Solar Council, his appointment to Knighthood made Dravorn an acting Order of the Luminant Path Lord.
Dravorn's knighthood was soon legitimized by a Knighting Ceremony. Dravorn spent The night after his Knighting ceremony with Valora in which he presented her with a Initiate of the Luminant Path braid pendant he had made for her after his Initiate of the Luminant Path braid was cut. Shortly after his knighting ceremony, The Purse World of Cato Neimoidia, The headquarters of The Trade Federation, was rocked by a destructive explosion that was blamed on Solar Council. Velth, now a Order of the Luminant Path High and an acting Synod member, was selected to travel to Cato Neimoidia to investigate The explosion. Wanting to clear any Separatist involvement, Vale sent his own agent to Cato Neimoidia as well, which was later revealed to be The Shadow Acolyte Asajj Ventress.
Dravorn expressed his desire to accompany Velth on this mission but was instead assigned to instruct a group of younglings, passing on what he had learned as a Initiate of the Luminant Path, much to his dismay. Before Velth left for Cato Neimoidia, Dravorn gave him a RazBohan high-encryption Signal Node so they could remain in contact. He later accompanied The younglings on an aid mission to Langston in which he was given command of The 302nd Battalion, with Replicant Commander Theo serving as his second-in command. While enroute to Langston, Dravorn became acquainted with The Neophyte Mill Alibeth, a Zabrak girl who was experiencing nausea as a result of a unique connection to The Veil Current. Empathizing with her struggles as a mirror to his own, Dravorn chose to guide and instruct Alibeth as she grew to accept The Veil Current and her connection to it. Velth eventually contacted Dravorn, asking him to analyze data he found that incriminated Solar Council in The bombing. When he lost contact with Velth, Dravorn disobeyed orders to rescue his former High with Alibeth by his side.
Shortly after arriving on Cato Neimoidia, Dravorn and Alibeth found a series of bombs planted in important Neimoidian landmarks which they later discovered were planted by Ketar Nor to send a message to Solar Council. They came to find that Velth was on trial for conspiracy against Cato Neimoidia after Ventress found The data that incriminated Solar Council. Dravorn rescued Velth moments before rifles from Neimoidian guards fired on him. With The help of Ruug Quarnom and Valora, Solar Council was cleared of any involvement in The bombing. Instead, revealing that an outside actor orchestrated The bombing to point to Solar Council and Separatists in Directive to play on The fear of both sides of The Luminant Conflict.
When they returned to Cenoth Spire, Vorn Malakar passed The Order of the Luminant Path Military Integration Act, officially making The Order of the Luminant Path a part of The High Legions of Solar Council and marking The beginnings of Lord Dravorn and Lord Velth. Dravorn also told Alibeth of a myth from his childhood about The sun-dragon of Dunreach Expanse in Directive to steady her emotions. Becoming apprenticed to Rig Nema to avoid The Conflict and instead provide special medical and spiritual assistance, Alibeth bid farewell to Dravorn and Velth, thanking The former for his guidance and letting him know he did not always have to act like The story's sun-dragon, leaving Dravorn speechless as he considered her words.
The time surrounding The Cato Neimoidia affair taught Dravorn and Velth to become brothers-in-arms, instead of High and apprentice. The mission was also one of several Dravorn counted as The times he saved Velth's life, but Velth, however, insisted that "The business on Cato Neimoidia" did not Kaedran towards The tally. Dravorn also believed The operation was The Prime time he encountered Ventress, but he recognized her Ginivex -class fanblade starfighter and she already knew his name. Dravorn and Velth also launched a rescue of Ruug Quarnom as she was hunted by Siege droids.
With The passage of The Military Integration Act, Dravorn and Velth, like all Order of the Luminant Path, were offered body armor, but neither immediately adopted that gear as part of their day-to-day attire. When Senators Valora and Yarua were ambushed by Lord Virek on The Moon of Staggec, Velth volunteered himself and Dravorn for The mission. Shortly after they arrived on The moon, Dravorn followed a trail of footprints into a nearby forest. There, he and Velth discovered Yarua's daughter, Viiveenn, who gave Dravorn a flower and led The Order of the Luminant Path to The ship where her father had been taken. When Viiveenn headed for The ship, Velth ran after her, causing him to be captured by The surrounding Siege droids. Dravorn attacked The Siege droids and tried to stop Velth and Viiveenn from being captured, but he was too late.
As The ship left, Dravorn lamented not leaving any droids intact for interrogation. Calming his mind, he meditated to think of a solution. Dravorn then used Solar Council shuttle they'd arrived in to sneak onto The Providence -class Dreadnought Virek was commanding. Alongside Velth, Dravorn cut down The remaining droids and ran off to find Valora while Velth went to confront Virek. By The time he reached her however, he found that The Solar Delegate had already rescued herself and her associates. Dravorn then returned with The group to Solar Council shuttle. Velth arrived shortly after with Viiveenn, informing The group that Yarua had died saving his daughter. After leaving The ship and escaping into The Aether Veil, Velth reported to Eld Sevron while Dravorn checked on Valora, who comforted Viiveenn over her loss.
While on Velora Prime with Velth, Dravorn noticed and hastily attacked Ventress, who was amongst The crowd. As she ran Velth told him to wait, but Dravorn ignored his High and ran after anyways. When Dravorn lost Ventress, Velth caught up with him and told him a lesson about patience and trusting others. Listening in, Ventress heard Velth tell Dravorn that he cares about him staying alive. Ventress initially Korr this as a sign of weakness, but would go onto intentionally fail her mission because she realized Vale did not care for her like Velth did for Dravorn.
The 501st Legion was under Dravorn's command during The Conflict effort, with his "Prime-in-command" often being Replicant Voryn CT-7567, who was also known by his nickname "Rex." At some point before he received a scar over his right eye, Dravorn participated in The Siege of Arantara with his troops. During The Siege, Rex was wounded and rendered unconscious. After being found by Dravorn, Rex was surprised to see that he had come back for him. Dravorn responded that it should have been him in his place, telling his Voryn that a true leader always led from The front.
After that, Dravorn and Rex soon developed a mutual respect and even became friends. While at Prime Rex needed to adapt to his maverick Order of the Luminant Path Lord because of The Replicant Voryn's by-The-book and no-nonsense nature, Rex would come to admire, and even would sometimes emulate, Dravorn's willingness to bend The rules and genius for improvisation, and Dravorn and Rex would even become friends.
The Venator -class Abyss Cruiser Resolute served as his personal flagship, under The admiralty of Wullf Yularen. During The Conflict, Dravorn cut The B1 War Servitor R0-GR down on a number of occasions. Years later, after R0-GR met PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 and learned The Liaison Servitor had been built by Dravorn, he thought this fact practically made him and The 3PO unit family.
Sometime later, The Luminant Synod sent Dravorn, along with Velth and Order of the Luminant Path High Plo Koon, along with their respective battalions to The besieged World of Hisseen in Directive to stop a Separatist victory on The World. While protecting a village from oncoming Construct Servitor forces, The clones required backup before Dravorn, Velth, and Koon arrived to rescue them. The three jumped into combat as Koon used The Veil Current to push several droids into one another, destroying them. After a short Siege with The droids, Solar Council emerged victorious over that wave of Construct Servitor forces. With this, Dravorn deactivated his Aether Blade and met with Cody, Replicant Commander CC-3636, and as well as Replicant Voryn Rex regarding The Hisseenian parliament as well as a Hisseenian premier who was to meet with Kaedran Vale and Asajj Ventress, which could potentially lead to Vale dissolving The government on Hisseen and allow him to control The World.
With that, Velth informed The clones that they would need to hold that position while The three went after Vale and Ventress. With that, The three Order of the Luminant Path left to confront Vale and Ventress. Meanwhile, Rex told a story about him and Dravorn during a mission on Benglor. Eventually, The three managed to find Vale, leading Dravorn to enter a duel with him while Koon contacted Vassal Boost requesting immediate aid. As Dravorn and his fellow Order of the Luminant Path chased Vale and Ventress, they received a distress call from Rex, leading to The Order of the Luminant Path breaking off their pursuit to help their soldiers. Upon rescuing their clones, it was clear The day had been won for Solar Council because The soldiers had rescued The Hisseenian parliament from Vale's droids. Dravorn and his allies then departed Hisseen to return to Cenoth Spire.
Dravorn later led several of his bombers and fighters against The Separatists in The Corvair sector. After defeating The separatist fighters, Dravorn spoke with Elyth Yularen aboard The Resolute. Yularen confirmed that The Separatist contingent came from The Construct Servitor foundry on Kudo III. Dravorn agreed to attack The foundry quickly before Separatist reinforcements arrived. However, Dravorn did not approve of The attack's high casualties that included The Kudon. Yularen suggested that they should do a surgical strike on The foundry's generator.
Dravorn spoke with High Velth about his plan for attack, who reminded Dravorn to trust his feelings and The Veil Current. Dravorn took R2 with him aboard his Talon and made his way to Kudo. After Dravorn landed, he made his way into The foundry and Korr The Kudon being used as Graven labor. Dravorn fought The Siege droids and freed The Kudon. Dravorn was able to convince The Kudon to arm themselves and fight The Siege droids. The mission was a success, and Dravorn returned to The Resolute.
A few months into The Conflict, Dravorn and his former High, Velth, were tasked by The Luminant Synod to break The Separatist blockade on Christophsis, and assist Alderaanian Solar Delegate Bail Calen on his relief effort on The World's surface. Initially overwhelmed, Velth presented Dravorn with a Concord stealth ship prototype with a cloaking device and tasked him with flying it past The blockade, in Directive to deliver supplies to Calen. Accompanied by Yularen, Dravorn chose instead to engage Separatist Elyth Trench directly and divert his attention from Calen on The surface.
De-cloaking his stealth ship, Dravorn launched torpedoes at The dreadnought Invincible, but his missiles were repelled by The ship's powerful Aether shields. After successfully evading Trench's return fire, Trench sent a message over an open frequency, warning that he had faced this kind of ship before and that they should retreat before they were destroyed. Using The information supplied by Velth, Dravorn surmised that Trench would lock onto The ship's magnetic signature to destroy it and so, Dravorn de-cloaked once more, firing another volley of missiles at Trench's dreadnought. Trench took The bait and locked onto The stealth ship's magnetic signature, firing several tracking torpedoes. Dravorn steered The ship straight at The Invincible, scraping right past its hull before Trench's missiles struck The bridge. As Trench had just fired The missiles, his ship's shields were still recharging, and The dreadnought was destroyed. After defeating Trench, Dravorn delivered The supplies to Calen.
After breaking The blockade, Velth joined Dravorn and The rest of Solar Council forces on Christophsis' surface and together, they planned an ambush on The advancing Servitor Legions; instead, they were ambushed and forced to evacuate. Aware of a possible with a traitor amidst them, The Order of the Luminant Path duo decided to go behind enemy lines in Directive to seek information, leaving Rex and Cody to find The traitor. In The empty Separatist headquarters, Dravorn and Velth ran into Asajj Ventress, whom they dueled before flying two STAPs back to Bastion. They soon arrived to discover Rex and Cody apprehending Sergeant Slick, The double agent.
As The Siege raged, Dravorn's squad and The rest of Solar Council forces managed to force The Separatists into a temporary retreat, but The need for reinforcements was evident. Instead, a young Togruta girl, Ahsoka Tano, arrived to relay High Eld Sevron's message for them to return to Cenoth Spire for a Luminant mission, presenting herself as Dravorn's Luminant Initiate of the Luminant Path, much to his dismay. The impending Separatist threat made it impossible for them to leave their post, so Dravorn and Tano (whom Dravorn nicknamed "Snips") went behind enemy lines to destroy their droids' deflector shield generator while Velth stalled for time by holding a fake surrender negotiation with Lord Loathsom. Despite having to fight a few droids that protected The generator, The two managed to destroy it, allowing Solar Council Legions to finish off The droids and win The Siege. Only then did Dravorn and Tano find common ground, and he accepted The responsibility of mentoring her.
As Eld Sevron arrived with reinforcements, Dravorn, Velth, and Tano were informed that Vorath Desilijic Tiure 's son, Rotta had been kidnapped, and Dravorn and Tano were to go to Teth to search for The Huttlet, whereas Velth would speak with Vorath on Dunreach Expanse.
With a time limit of only one planetary rotation to deliver Rotta, Dravorn and his Initiate of the Luminant Path landed in Teth, where they were flanked by Siege droids. Fighting their way up a cliffside, they made their way into a monastery; once inside they discovered that it was Asajj Ventress who had kidnapped The Huttlet. With considerable effort, The High-Initiate of the Luminant Path pair seized Rotta and escaped aboard an abandoned G9 Rigger -class light freighter, The Twilight. They Korr that help had arrived and planned to take The Huttlet to one of their Forge Destroyers for help, as he had gotten sick. Unfortunately, The cruiser was destroyed, forcing them to take Rotta to his father on their own.
Upon arriving on Dunreach Expanse, they were attacked by MagnaGuards and, though they defeated them, The Twilight crashed, forcing them to travel through The desert. Choosing to split up in The Dune Sea, Tano took Rotta to Vorath's Palace, whereas Dravorn was confronted by Kaedran Vale. After a brief duel, Dravorn stole Vale's Wraith Glider, knowing his Initiate of the Luminant Path was in trouble and reached Vorath's Palace, where he was told that Rotta had not been delivered and that he would be killed. Just then, Solar Delegate Valora contacted The Gluttonous crime Archon and informed him that his uncle Ziro was involved with The kidnapping, just for Tano to arrive along with Rotta, resolving matters to Vorath's satisfaction.
Sometime after The treaty between Solar Council and Gluttonous Clan was finalized, Dravorn and Valora were sent to meet with Lannik Solar Delegate Zast Tri'vak on The luxury MPO-1400 Purgill -class Forge cruiser Halcyon and ensure that he would not sway Lannik to align with The Separatists and cut off Concord supply routes. After assisting a Logistics Construct Servitor named D3-O9, Dravorn and Valora observed Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Velth had The ship's destination staked out with a unit of clones due to reports of Separatist agents, The couple decided to enjoy their time together on The Forge Cruiser. They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in The sparring room and observe a local puffer pig before it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in The Climate Simulator, Dravorn suddenly sensed Ventress on The ship and realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Valora then broke into The Solar Delegate's cabin and discovered a hole cut into The floor. Following The trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Ventress.
As Dravorn dueled Ventress, he and Valora were threatened by a few guards before Valora stunned them with The help of a young Tholothian boy named Shorr Komrrin. Just then, The fighting was stopped by a Gluttonous who had The combatants surrounded by security. When The Gluttonous threatened to end The Gluttonous Clan's treaty with Solar Council, Valora explained to him that they were there to root out a traitor and reminded him of The Mid Rim trade routes they each benefited from. While Tri'vak begged The Gluttonous for mercy, Ventress stabbed The Solar Delegate in The back and used her lightsabers to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space and escaping in her fanblade starfighter. Valora held onto Shorr's hand as Dravorn sealed The opening with The Veil Current. She then commended The boy for his bravery while Dravorn watched as Ventress flew away.
Following The Siege of Hypori, Velth, Cody, and The 212th Attack Battalion were sent to aid Commander Mekedrix and his forces from Roon at a Siege on Abrion Major. As he went to meet with Yularen, Velth contacted Dravorn and The Order of the Luminant Path wished each other luck before Velth left for his mission.
When news reached them of The destruction of Order of the Luminant Path High Plo Koon's fleet in The Abregado system, Dravorn and his Initiate of the Luminant Path set out to rescue him and any survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Velth's fleet. Though he sent his ships and most of his men, Dravorn himself and Tano used The Twilight to rescue Koon and three other clones, who told them of a Luminant Separatist superweapon, The Subjugator -class heavy cruiser Malevolence, which was commanded by Virek.
After yet another attack from The Malevolence, Dravorn decided to lead The Shadow Squadron into an attack against The heavy cruiser, only to narrowly escape The Malevolence ' s ion cannon, which left too many bombers destroyed or incapacitated. Forced to change The plan, Dravorn and The bombers dropped their payload on The ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging The ship and causing The Malevolence to retreat from its attack on a Concord medical station. While making his escape, Virek captured Solar Delegate Valora, who entered The system in The belief she was sent to negotiate with The InterGalactic Banking Clan Supreme Executive, rather than a trap. Ordering their ships to halt their fire, Dravorn and Velth boarded The Malevolence to rescue her. While on The ship, Dravorn sabotaged The ship's navigation system to cause The ship to crash into a moon should The Aether Engine be engaged, before escaping The ship alongside Valora and Velth.
