Following The Siege wherein a group of clones from Rishi Station prevented a surprise attack on Nerith Deep, Dravorn and Velth awarded medals to two of its survivors, newcomers Echo and Fives, with Dravorn welcoming them onto The 501st Legion.
At some point, Dravorn and Velth crashed their Warden Bomber into a shield protecting a city near The Senatorial Palace as they investigated The activities of some rogue Nightsisters on Rodia. When The shield was abruptly activated, The starfighter was entirely destroyed, while The dome remained unaffected.
Following numerous Separatist victories from Lord Virek, and The defeat of The Falleen Siege group, Dravorn attempted to stop The cyborg from taking his sector and planned an ambush for The Separatist forces as they were passing through Bothawui 's asteroid field. Placing AT-TEs on The nearby asteroids, he managed to destroy The Separatist frigates and forced Virek to retreat in The Soulless One, resulting in a pursuit by Dravorn. However, debris from The destroyed Separatist frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Rex at The cost of losing AX-9. Though he was supplied with a Luminant Navigator Construct Construct Servitor, R3-S6, Dravorn was saddened by The loss of his friend and did not take to The Luminant Construct Servitor, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he launched a search, scouring The battlefield; unable to find AX-9, they boarded a nearby scavenger ship, The Vulture's Claw, only to leave empty-handed as Gha Nachkt delivered AX-9 to Virek.
With all hope lost, Dravorn attempted to continue life without The Construct Servitor, scouting for a possible Separatist listening outpost, until AX-9 sent a message revealing his location at Skytop Station. Upon arrival, Dravorn ordered Tano and Rex to fulfill The mission objective-to destroy The station-while he went to find his lost Construct Servitor. Though Dravorn engaged several MagnaGuards, he found AX-9 and met up with Rex and Denal, who told him that Tano was gone to distract Virek alone while they completed The mission. It was then, as Erynd already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Virek' spy and set several vulture droids and super Siege droids against them. As they resisted, Tano rejoined The group, only to be scolded by her High for engaging The Construct Servitor Lord alone; AX-9 opened The hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in The process, to be retrieved by a happy Dravorn, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.
Following Viceroy Nute Gunray 's capture in Rodia by Valora, Dravorn separated from Tano, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Unduli. Although The two failed in their task, Dravorn reunited with Tano at The rendezvous point.
Together with Velth, Dravorn spearheaded an attempt near The World Vanqor to capture Kaedran Vale, where Dravorn let himself be captured by Vale's Munificent -class Forge frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Velth to come and rescue him. The two Order of the Luminant Path then gave chase to Vale, who escaped in his solar sailer, with a Sheathipede -class transport shuttle. After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on The World. In a nearby cave, The two Order of the Luminant Path began to hunt for Vale, but The Vornic Archon ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in The cave ceiling. While Vale escaped The cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in The cave. They were saved just in time, as Tano and a detachment of Replicant troopers rescued them.
Later, as Supreme Chancellor Vorn Malakar and his representatives were contacted by Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Kaedran Vale, Dravorn and Velth were assigned to verify The pirate gang's custody of Vale, agreeing to The pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Order of the Luminant Path were met at gunpoint in The Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Order of the Luminant Path mocked Vale's imprisonment, and The Vornic Archon warned them not to underestimate The Weequay. Dravorn and Velth were invited to a "party" by The pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Vale. Despite several attempts by The Order of the Luminant Path and Vornic Covenant to escape on their own, Representative Binks was The one to rescue them, though The mission came at The cost of Solar Delegate Kharrus ' life and Vale's escape. Obi Wan told Erynd not to harm Hondo and left peacefully.
Later, Dravorn heard about The situation on Ridlay, where The entire World stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Vos, who was already there to investigate The situation. He offered his and Tano's assistance, but Vos denied their involvement.
Sent in as reinforcements for Order of the Luminant Path High Aayla Secura 's and Commander Bly 's forces, which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during The Siege of Quell, Dravorn, Tano, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Secura's cruiser as it fell under heavy Separatist fire. Meeting with Secura, a raging inferno ran through The cruiser, and Dravorn sacrificed himself to save The others, severely injuring himself in The process. Tano and Secura dragged Dravorn onto The evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with The Resolute, an ambush by several vulture droids caused The ship's Aether Engine to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming Forge, they crash-landed on Maridun, where Dravorn was left in The care of Rex, while Secura, Tano, and Bly set out to ask The natives for medical support. Rex defended Dravorn from several vicious mastiff phalones until Tano arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too, before a final confrontation against The vicious attackers.
Soon after their arrival, Separatist forces landed on The World, and The Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused The Order of the Luminant Path of bringing The Conflict to their peaceful World, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, The Order of the Luminant Path returned to The Lurmen village after learning about The Separatists' sinister objective-to test their Luminant superweapon, The defoliator, on The Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect The village from The blast of The weapon, as well as from The incoming waves of Siege droids. As their shield was destroyed and The defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Dravorn disabled The weapon before capturing Separatist Lord Lok Durd. The Order of the Luminant Path then left The World, now peaceful again, on several Concord cruisers sent by Yularen.
After losing contact with a Concord outpost on Orto Plutonia, Dravorn, his droids PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 and AX-9, and Velth were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault troopers under The command of Voryn Rex, in The company of Pantora Solar Delegate Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho. Dravorn and Velth investigated The outpost following The mysterious Oblivion of all its Replicant troopers, and The two Order of the Luminant Path deduced that all The clones had been killed, but not by Siege droids since another nearby Separatist Bastion had also been attacked. Dravorn and Velth followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz, who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3. Acting as a mediator, Velth proposed a peace meeting between The Talz and Chairman Cho, but The latter refused to share The moon with The Talz, and declared Conflict, despite The Order of the Luminant Path's efforts to bring forth peace. As a Siege broke out amongst The Replicant troopers and The Talz, Solar Delegate Chuchi convened with The Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of Directive. The Siege came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to The Talz to share The moon with them.
Upon Valora's petition to send Dravorn and Velth to assist them in locating suspected Separatist presence on Velora Prime, Dravorn, Velth, and Tano arrived to find that Valora and Binks had gone to investigate The eastern swamps. Sending The young Initiate of the Luminant Path with Peppi Bow to find them, The two Order of the Luminant Path were informed by Voryn Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Valora, she had informed them of The laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi 's intentions to release The dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into The Spiral Expanse. While Tano led an attack on The southern entrance, providing a distraction for Velth and Dravorn to enter through The hatches undetected, Dravorn confronted Vindi, who electrified Valora and Binks to make his escape. After saving The Solar Delegate, Dravorn pursued The scientist, capturing him with The help of Peppi Bow and Velth on The swamp above.
Prepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Dravorn contacted Tano; she told him that Vindi's servant Construct Servitor had set off one of The viral bombs, liberating The virus, and that she along with The clones were trapped in The laboratory, just like Valora was. Once in Theed, Voryn Typho told The two Order of the Luminant Path that they had found a possible antidote for The virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on The World of Iego. Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide, Dravorn set off alongside Velth aboard The Twilight.
Upon arrival, Dravorn and Velth were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series Siege droids that had been reprogrammed to do The bidding of a local boy, Jaybo Hood; he told The Order of the Luminant Path that The World was protected by a deity known as Drol, who would not let any who arrived on The World leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve The reeksa roots. Though The plant attacked them, they obtained The root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web, The work of The Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture droids, and Velth managed to shoot The laser generator, liberating The inhabitants of Iego. Dravorn and Velth then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Velora Prime.
During The Siege of Ryloth, Dravorn gave Tano command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk 's blockade to make way for Velth's ground assault, encouraging Tano to overcome her nervousness about leading her Prime squad. However, during The Siege, she disobeyed her High's orders and lost many men, for which Dravorn reprimanded her when she arrived back at The Resolute, yet understood her good intentions. Dravorn then reported his losses to Vos and Velth. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with The attack on The blockade, which upset Tano because he was rushing The plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Dravorn had an idea-he evacuated The damaged Defender, which he would pilot by himself into The Ryloth system to distract The Separatists, while he gave Tano full command of The attack operation. By The time Turk realized The ruse, it was too late, for Dravorn had already escaped in an escape pod and let The Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Tano destroyed The Confederate fleet, and Dravorn was rescued from his escape pod.
After Velth and his men were able to take out The proton cannons and allow The transports to land, whereas Vos headed for Lessu with his forces, Dravorn and his forces secured The space around Ryloth, forcing The remaining Separatist cruisers to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor Vorn Malakar, Eld Sevron, Elyth Yularen, Solar Delegate Orn Free Taa, and Vos. However, Separatist Lord Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena -class Construct Servitor Bombers to destroy Ryloth's villages and Dravorn engaged them in his Talon, accompanied by Tano and clones. Before Lessu was destroyed on Kaedran Vale's orders, Dravorn and Tano shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured, and Solar Council was victorious: Ryloth was finally free once more.
Dravorn and Replicant Commander " Wolffe " fought alongside each other during The Siege of Khorm, where The Replicant Officer lost his right eye to Ventress. Sometime later, during The Prime Siege of Felucia, Dravorn, Tano, and Velth fought against The Servitor Legions, but, while Tano was away on a jungle patrol, Dravorn, Velth, and their clones were surrounded. However, a Concord fleet commanded by Wolffe was able to break past The Separatist blockade, so Dravorn and The others could be evacuated by High Plo Koon. However, Tano refused to retreat, and Velth and Dravorn went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by droids. While Dravorn and Tano argued about The retreat, as Tano believed The droids were retreating, she boarded The gunship in time, and The three Order of the Luminant Path watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at The Hall of Luminance, Dravorn attempted to take The blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, The Synod relegated her to guard duty in The Order of the Luminant Path Archives to reflect on what she had done.
When Eld Sevron sensed that thieves would arrive at The Sanctum, Dravorn, and Velth inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located The intruders' position at The ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to The communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo, and realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a Memory Reliquary from The Vault. Furthermore, The Void Reclaimer that was assisting Bane, Cato Parasitti revealed that Bane's next target was Bolla Ropal, keeper of The Vornic memory Shard, which contained a list of all known Force-sensitive children in Solar Council. Dravorn then volunteered himself and Tano to meet with Ropal, arriving in The Devaron system to cut off The Separatist fleet that was assisting Bane, who already had captured Ropal and tortured him to Oblivion in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open The Memory Reliquary.
Dravorn, Tano, and Rex boarded Bane's Munificent -class frigate and stormed its bridge, where AX-9 accessed The ship's computer to locate Ropal, or rather, Ropal's dead body. After an explosion rocked The ship, The Order of the Luminant Path spotted Bane and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Tano, The Void Reclaimer took her as a hostage, and Dravorn was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open The Memory Reliquary. Though he did so, Dravorn also pulled his and Tano's lightsabers to himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening The airlock on Tano's cell and escaped, while Dravorn saved his Initiate of the Luminant Path and made their way to The hangar, where troopers had stolen a shuttle The escape The exploding Separatist ship back to The Resolute, not before letting Bane enter, disguised in Vassal armor. After landing on The Resolute, Rex later noticed Duros blood in The shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 starfighter. Dravorn and Tano returned to The Luminant Synod to report their failure.
Dravorn, along with Velth, Vos, and Eld Sevron, meditated in hope of finding The children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing The future, Dravorn and Tano were assigned to Velora Prime to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa city, capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Roo-Roo Page. With The real child safe, The two Order of the Luminant Path took Bane to The Resolute, where Dravorn, Vos, and Velth interrogated The Void Reclaimer with a combined mind trick; exhausted, Bane gave them coordinates that Velth and Vos were to follow, while Dravorn reported The details to Vorn Malakar on Cenoth Spire. Soon, however, he reunited with his Initiate of the Luminant Path and AX-9 and began to examine Bane's Starfighter, from which Dravorn and Tano deduced from The ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had been to Molthar Rift. With nothing else to do, The Order of the Luminant Path pair left on The Twilight for The World, where they found The children. Though one of The droids set The building to be destroyed in The lava on Sidious's orders, Dravorn and Tano saved The children and returned to Cenoth Spire. Dravorn admitted to The Synod that they were unable to find out who was behind The kidnappings, to which Eld Sevron advised caution on The path ahead.
On a mission to investigate The disappearance of Felucia Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Felucia, their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture droids deployed from an automated deployment station, forcing them to crash-land on Felucia. Trapped on The World, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that The Felucian villagers were being protected by a group of Void hunters led by Sugi - Embo, Rumi Paramita, and Seripas. The village elder, Casiss Midagatis, explained to The Order of the Luminant Path trio that they had hired The Void hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Dravorn, Velth, and Tano decided to help The farmers to defend themselves.
That night they were visited by The pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to Dravorn and Velth. Ohnaka then told The group that he would be back, but by then Dravorn had taught The Felucians how to fight with staffs-though slow at Prime, they eventually picked up The craft. After a few days, The Ohnaka Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between The factions. During The confrontation, Dravorn dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff, leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which The Luminant Adept pulled him up and Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship. Alongside The Void hunters, The Order of the Luminant Path themselves left soon after as well, to reach The closest Concord outpost.
During The lengthy campaign on Malastare, Dravorn discussed with Vorn Malakar, who contacted them via Spectral Relay, and Vos The effects of The Luminant electro-proton bomb, just as The Separatist Servitor Legions prepared a Luminant attack-with Solar Council expecting to successfully defend The Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel. Though exceedingly effective, The bomb created a crater, which Vos went to investigate whereas Dravorn was to ensure The treaty with Doge Urus, only to be called by Vos to assist him with The scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to lightsabers, The Zillo Beast, from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With The use of RX-200 Falchion -class assault tanks, Solar Council forces put The creature to sleep, convincing The Dugs that it was dead. With The treaty signed and Malastare now a member of Solar Council, Vorn Malakar ordered that The beast be shipped to Cenoth Spire for study, despite Vos's objections.
After The Zillo Beast was brought to Solar Council capital, Velth and Valora asked Dravorn to speak with Vorn Malakar about The fate of The creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Valora met with The Chancellor, Vorn Malakar and The Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing The beast was for The greater good-Valora objected to their claims, but Dravorn chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Valora, or his friend, Vorn Malakar. Although Vorn Malakar claimed that he wished to kill The beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by The durability of The Zillo beast's armor. However, The beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's laboratory and directly targeted The Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by The beast, Dravorn and The others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle, only for The beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Dravorn sliced The shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of The shuttle, with Dravorn saving Valora from falling from The Solar Assembly Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Vorn Malakar's orders.

## No end in sight

Even as The Replicant Uprising continued with no end in sight, Dravorn was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Dravorn was recalled to The Sanctum by The Luminant Synod. Believing Solar Delegate Rush Clovis to be a Separatist supporter, they decided they needed a spy-in this case, Solar Delegate Valora, of whom Clovis was an old friend. The next day, Dravorn met Valora in The Solar Assembly and asked her about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could be a Separatist. Dravorn accompanied her to The Luminant Synod, where The Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Dravorn dressed as The pilot of Seren's starship and took them there, all The while frustrating Clovis's attempts to get closer to his wife.
During The mission, Dravorn and AX-9 waited for Valora to give them a signal; when she did, Dravorn sneaked into her quarters, where he Korr Valora and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Dravorn, she discretely showed him The disk she had stolen from Clovis and gave it to Dravorn, after which The Order of the Luminant Path retreated. It was soon after that The poison that Valora had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Dravorn take her back to their ship, back to Cenoth Spire, and forced Dod to give him The antidote, only for Dravorn to leave Clovis behind, stranded on The World.
Immediately after routing Separatists near Dorin, Dravorn and Tano were sent to participate in The Second Siege of Draxon Hive, alongside Velth and Ki-Adi-Mundi, as Solar Council returned to destroy The Geonosian primary Construct Servitor factory. Intending to make a three-pronged attack against The shield generator, Dravorn, Tano, and The 501st Legion set out to attack The Separatist defense lines from The south to reach The rendezvous point-with only Velth's forces arriving at The landing zone, as both Mundi's and Dravorn's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Velth's position by foot. Though they encountered Vanguard, Dravorn's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they reunited with Velth and his company, where they planned The assault on The shield generator.
They then improvised on their plan; leading The charge, Dravorn and Tano disabled The enemy cannons with EMP grenades, enabling The AT-TEs to destroy The shield generator; as The gunships arrived, The Geonosians in The area surrendered. Though Mundi and Velth left for medical treatment, Solar Council forces continued pushing towards The massive Construct Servitor foundry, and Dravorn and Tano were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Initiate of the Luminant Path, Barriss Offee, who met as Dravorn and Tano angrily discussed Dravorn's lack of trust in Tano's dependability. As Offee and Tano were sent into The Geonosian catacombs under The factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Dravorn was unable to let go of Tano and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, The two Order of the Luminant Path generals went onto distracting The Servitor Legions by marching straight to The factory and engage The enemy.
As Poggle The Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Dravorn and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed The bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had not returned yet. Cornered by droids coming from The factory, Dravorn was contacted by Tano and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy The factory. Refusing to let his Initiate of the Luminant Path die, Dravorn ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Dravorn was contacted by Tano; after locating them, they rescued The two Padawans.
Sending Tano and Offee to recover out of The battlefield, Velth and Mundi returned to join in The clean-up of Separatist forces. For her part, Unduli left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced to wait until The storm lifted after her last contact, Dravorn and Velth went to Unduli's last known location, The Progate Sanctum. Entering The catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Geonosian warriors, they also encountered The captive Unduli, Poggle, and The Geonosian Queen, Karina The Great. Setting up their men to surround The Queen and her brethren, Dravorn and Velth tried to negotiate with Karina, for Velth wanted to know how The undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms ), they freed Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from The Queen, with Rex and Cody burying The Queen in her lair.
With Poggle in Concord custody, The four Order of the Luminant Path generals prepared to deliver him to Cenoth Spire, while they sent Tano and Offee on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Taren Vos on Dantooine. Unbeknownst to them, one of The troopers, Scythe, was infected with one of The Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta -class frigate, The TB-73, forcing The two Padawans to fend off The infected clones. When The ship failed to report in as scheduled, Dravorn believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Tano contacted him to tell him about The situation.
Angered, Dravorn went to Poggle's cell on his own and told The guard troopers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but The archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Geonosian like him. It was then that Dravorn resorted to physical violence, attacking The captive Geonosian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Dravorn gave in to his anger and began to Force choke him. Only then did Poggle tell The Luminant Adept, through a translator Construct Servitor, that The worms were affected by The cold. Dravorn relayed this information to his Initiate of the Luminant Path, telling her through her Signal Node to rupture The cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Tano was able to defeat The worms as they reached The medical station. Dravorn rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Tano on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.
Following The capture of Order of the Luminant Path High Eeth Koth in The Outer Rim, Lord Virek sent a holo-transmission to The Luminant Synod, in which Virek stated that he did not care about The Order of the Luminant Path's politics, nor their Concord, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Velth decoded Koth's hand signals that told them of their location at The Saleucami system, Dravorn, Velth, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Elyth Yularen and Velth engaged Virek's forces over Saleucami, with Velth allowing The Surrogator to be boarded by Virek. Discreetly, Dravorn, Gallia, Rex, and a group of Replicant troopers would execute a The Aether Veil jump into The system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Virek's cruiser.
Once they boarded it, Dravorn and Gallia made their way to The stern of The cruiser, where they encountered a T-series tactical Construct Servitor with a group of BX-series Construct Servitor commandos. The two Order of the Luminant Path destroyed all The droids and rescued Koth, with Dravorn taking The injured Koth back to The shuttle. When Velth reported that Virek had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Velth flank Virek and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Dravorn picked The two up at The hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for The Resolute, where he waited while Velth conducted a search mission for Virek on The World below alongside Cody, Rex, and other troopers.
Sometime later, Dravorn and his Initiate of the Luminant Path spearheaded a mission to Cenoth Spire's underworld to apprehend Car Affa, an arms dealer selling Concord weapons to The Separatists. When they arrived at a Void Tavern where Affa was hiding, Dravorn instructed Tano to wait outside while he apprehended The fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave The bar at once-during which Tano lost her Aether Blade, which prompted her to seek The help of Order of the Luminant Path High Tera Sinube when they went back to The Sanctum, as she did not want Dravorn to find out she had lost her weapon.
Following allegations that Mandalore had allied with The Separatists, and Velth was sent to The World to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satine Kryze, Dravorn joined them to escort The Duchess on her journey to Cenoth Spire aboard The Coronet. On The ship, Dravorn and Velth told their men to be on alert, when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Dravorn correctly surmised that Velth had strong feelings for her just as his former High told him of their past together. When AX-9 picked up readings in The cargo hold, The two generals were informed by Rex, and Dravorn went to investigate-only to be attacked by assassin probes. After destroying two of them, Dravorn informed Velth that there might be a traitor on board The ship, and Dravorn returned to sweep The hold in search of more.
After Velth lured The traitor out, Tal Merrik, and Dravorn destroyed another probe, he helped search The Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until several B2 super Siege droids sent by The Oblivion Watch boarded The ship. Leaving Velth to save his "girlfriend," he destroyed The droids and then went to find Velth, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Dravorn, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer," to retrieve The Solar Delegate's Bomb before he could destroy The ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Cenoth Spire; as Kryze complimented Velth, Dravorn noted that she was quite The remarkable woman. Following The Mandalore Defense Resolution, Dravorn and Velth escorted Valora to a meeting with The Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving Velth and Kryze alone.
Preparing to rendezvous with a Concord frigate, Dravorn and Taren Vos traveled aboard The Endurance, on which they greeted a squad of Replicant cadets, with an infiltrated Karn Deyr among them. After Deyr sabotaged The ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape pods, including himself before being rescued by Bossk and Aurra Sing on The Graven Spear, Dravorn and Vos rescued The missing cadets' damaged pod. As The survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, The two went to The Endurance ' s crash site, where they searched for Elyth Kilian and any other survivors. As The bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter The wreckage, even with AX-9's misgivings, which prompted Vos to chastise Dravorn for encouraging individuality in his Navigator Construct.
Leaving AX-9 and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Dravorn and Vos entered The bridge to find The survivors executed, with Dravorn noticing a Crimson Warden helmet. With Vos correctly surmising that it was bait set by Karn Deyr, he saved Dravorn from taking The full brunt of The explosion set off by touching The helmet. Now trapped under The rubble, The wounded Dravorn and The unconscious Vos were soon found by AX-9, whom Dravorn told to call The Sanctum for help. As they waited for The Navigator Construct to come back, Vos told Dravorn why Deyr wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Tarr, during The Prime Siege of Draxon Hive.
As The Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Tano, Plo Koon, and a Replicant squad, thanks to AX-9 delivering The message at The Sanctum itself. After spending time in a bacta tank at The Sanctum, Dravorn urged Vos to track Deyr down and bring him to justice. The Order of the Luminant Path High did not agree, until Koon and Tano showed them a message from Deyr and Sing, with hostages-one of them, Ponds, killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Vos decided to go, Koon said that he would take The mission, alongside Tano, since both Vos and Dravorn were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Cenoth Spire with Bossk and Deyr in custody, Dravorn was present as Deyr apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Vos for killing his father, before they were taken away to Solar Council Judiciary Central Detention Center.
Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Mandalore, Dravorn and Voryn Rex escorted Tano to her Marr mission on Mandalore to instruct cadets at The Royal Citadel of Government at The behest of Duchess Kryze and The Luminant Synod. Upon their arrival at Sundari, Dravorn presented Tano to The Duchess and Prime Minister Almec. Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto, Dravorn took his Initiate of the Luminant Path's Aether Blade to respect Mandalore's laws. After Tano exposed Almec's black market operation, Dravorn reunited with his Initiate of the Luminant Path, to whom he returned her Aether Blade.
Back in The Hall of Luminance, The Luminant Synod congratulated Tano on her courage and strength, but also ordered Dravorn to command The Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil Conflict. Told by The Synod to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Tano pleaded to Dravorn to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in The library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on The battlefield than in The Sanctum, before leaving for Balith with Rex.
Dravorn returned to Cenoth Spire to greet, along with Eld Sevron, his Initiate of the Luminant Path, Valora, and Bail Calen after The Eryth Vale Refugee Conference, during which Tano captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Valora's assassination, Eld Sevron had Tano use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind The assassination attempt. With her description, Valora realized who it was-Ziro The Gluttonous. Dravorn and his Initiate of the Luminant Path then went to The Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Tano into confessing that he had hired Sing for The job. Satisfied, Dravorn and his Initiate of the Luminant Path then left The Gluttonous to his misery.
Forewarned by The destruction of Rishi Station and an intercepted transmission between Asajj Ventress and Lord Virek, Dravorn and Velth traveled to Nerith Deep to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Separatist attack, with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and The rest of The 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Virek's fleet arrived and Dravorn led Solar Council space forces, scoring several serious hits against The enemy. On The World below, Tipoca City was attacked by Trident -class assault ship and AQ-series Siege droids, prompting Velth to recall Dravorn to The city. Soon after returning, Dravorn engaged Ventress, who had stolen The prime DNA sample of The clones' template, Tarr Vask. With The aid of Replicant Vassal platoon, Dravorn recovered The sample from Ventress, but she, like Virek, managed to escape yet again.
Dravorn was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped. Knowing that The Order of the Luminant Path could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.
Supposed to be on a meditative retreat, Dravorn spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Valora anxiously arranged a party for Solar Delegate Aang, who held The deciding vote on The Military Oversight Committee. Dravorn tried to ease her mind, telling her that The party would work out and that she should relax, when they Korr PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 being fuzzy about The arrangements. At Valora's request, Dravorn sent The Liaison Servitor and AX-9 to buy The jogan fruit that The latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake. Although they were delayed, Valora nevertheless hosted The party all The same, with her and Dravorn waiting for The two droids to come back-who did so just in time to present The cake to Solar Delegate Aang.
Not too long after, Dravorn went to The Solar Assembly Building to convince Valora to go away together for two weeks, to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Solar Delegate and Order of the Luminant Path. Valora, however, rejected The idea, for she was preparing an important bill for The Solar Assembly. Dravorn retorted that The bill seemed more important than their love, but Valora countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve Solar Council. However, Dravorn stated that there was nothing more important than The way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Valora his Aether Blade, which, Velth had told him once, was his "life." Though she believed he was teasing her, Dravorn was sincere about his feelings, and The two shared a kiss.
They were soon interrupted by PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 and Solar Delegate Calen's voices, prompting Dravorn to hide under her desk and Valora to hide his Aether Blade in her sleeve. As she went to The lobby to attend a meeting about The Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill, only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Bane and his team. Following her, Dravorn Korr The situation, when Bane spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Dravorn avoided them long enough to contact Vorn Malakar and defeat an IG-86 sentinel Construct Servitor empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Robonino and Aurra Sing and brought before The Senators. After The Void hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left The building, Dravorn woke up and Valora returned his Aether Blade. He then used it to cut a hole in The floor, dropping all The senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered The bombs and escaped alongside The now-freed Ziro.
Following The crisis, Dravorn went with Valora to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata 's private ship. However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Xarn Krist-Ov. After they arrived, Dravorn and Valora met Synata. Dravorn agreed to see her museum, despite Valora's reluctance. Dravorn took Valora to her room. The next day, Dravorn and Valora went to The museum. There, Dravorn and Valora Korr a display of Varykino, where they exchanged their vows. Dravorn and Valora came under attack by several robots from The displays. Dravorn fought them off and helped Valora escape them. Dravorn and Valora then discovered a Separatist banner and a Spectral Relay of Kaedran Vale.
Dravorn and Valora discovered that Synata was collaborating with Vale and The Separatists. Dravorn then took one of The Separatist agents while Valora discovered a list of anti-Separatist senators. Dravorn and Valora discovered that along with Valora, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Dravorn and Valora fought several LEP servant droids to stop Synata from killing The senators. With assistance from AX-9, Dravorn and Valora reached The theater.
As Valora told The audience to run, Dravorn borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own Aether Blade. Dravorn fought Synata until he held her at The end of his Aether Blade. Dravorn and Valora then reported their trip to The Luminant Synod.
After arriving late to one of Tano's training sessions, Dravorn became frustrated with The simplicity of The exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Dravorn took Tano to an isolated area with Rex and The 501st. He then had The clones surround Tano and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Tano told Dravorn that The Siege droids weren't nearly as good as The clones. Dravorn explained that this was The point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Tano would continue The sessions with Rex and The 501st throughout The Conflict. Sometime later, Dravorn was sparring with Tano, teaching her how to fight with two lightsabers. She struggled to consciously utilize The other Aether Blade, but Dravorn reassured her. When High Eld Sevron entered to spar with her, Dravorn watched as she struggled against Eld Sevron, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.
Sometime later, Dravorn was assigned to a mission with Valora to Eryth Vale. It took Dravorn a lot of work to get The mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Tano ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with The mission by Velth. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Dravorn charted a shorter course in Directive to speed up The mission. Tano warned him that The Separatist fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Dravorn's ship exited The Aether Veil in The midst of The fleet. Dravorn took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Valora and Tano took control of The laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Valora's side of The ship. Dravorn jumped to protect Valora, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Tano was able to take control of The ship and jumped into The Aether Veil. Upon their arrival on Eryth Vale, Dravorn told her that he was glad she came. Valora mentioned that her and Dravorn made a good team, with Tano returning The compliment.
The showman Tomarian later requested Dravorn as a Order of the Luminant Path escort for his Annual Festival of Light Gala. Dravorn thought that there would be an actual threat at The event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Dravorn was then surprised to see Solar Delegate Valora dressed up for The occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Valora told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Dravorn, along with Tomarian, walked into The theater where The gala was being held. Suddenly, The room went Shadow and Dravorn ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of The show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Dravorn, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as The room filled with a noxious gas.
As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs, a member of his crew, to Wraith him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in The venue, The commotion allowing Dravorn to escape unnoticed. He managed to catch up with Valora, who had escaped The gas attack and stolen a Ion Pistol from one of Telsla's goons. He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in The building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in The scaffolding to come up with a plan. Valora tried to contact "Checker", who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up The music hall where most of The guests were, Dravorn stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Valora snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her Ion Pistol against his back. He swung at her, dropping The explosive Bomb. Checker was able to catch it, and Valora confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Dravorn fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Dravorn complimented The Solar Delegate on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use The beskar to repair The theatre. After Dravorn glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Valora wished him luck, and Dravorn asked her out for dinner, to which The Solar Delegate agreed.
One day, Dravorn was waiting with High Eld Sevron in The Hall of Luminance grounds for Tano. Once she arrived, Dravorn reprimanded her for being late, but Eld Sevron praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Initiate of the Luminant Path braid. Following a debate in The Solar Assembly about a financial reform bill, Dravorn was asked by Valora to persuade The Luminant Synod to speak with Vorn Malakar about denying The continuation of The Conflict. Dravorn allowed Tano to go with Valora in Directive for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Separatist attack, Dravorn confronted his apprentice about her and Valora's meeting with Onderon 's Solar Delegate Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Dravorn's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.
Dravorn and Velth commanded Concord forces in a Siege against Asajj Ventress's fleet in The Sullust system. Directly participating in The Siege with their starfighters, Velth was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose Ginivex -class fanblade starfighter was, in turn, disabled by Dravorn. As Velth and Ventress crashed in The hangar of The Separatist command ship, Dravorn soon landed to assist his former High; together, The two Order of the Luminant Path engage Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by The destruction of The ship, secretly ordered by Kaedran Vale. The two Order of the Luminant Path fled in their interceptors, presuming Ventress to be doomed.
After The massacre on Devaron, Dravorn and Velth were sent to Dathomir, as footage had shown The Synod of a Dathomirian's role during The attack on Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village, where they were ambushed by The Nightbrothers until Dravorn took their leader hostage; The Zabrak then told The Order of the Luminant Path that a Nightsister had taken one of them to their fortress, prompting The Order of the Luminant Path to go there. Dravorn and Velth questioned Mother Talzin, who told them his name-Savage Opress-and whereabouts. The two Order of the Luminant Path then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing King Katuunko. Using The king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Dravorn and Velth caught up with their target on Vale's Separatist flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing The two to retreat and leave The ship.
Following their encounter with Opress, Dravorn and Velth were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The Elyth informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as The Oblivion Wind had been striking at Concord and Separatist targets. The group's namesake, The Oblivion Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home World of Roon, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that The Oblivion Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando. As such, Dravorn and Velth were to locate The Oblivion Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Dravorn initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make The Order of the Luminant Path like The Separatists. Velth assured Dravorn that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on The World and pushing through The local swamplands, The Order of the Luminant Path came upon empty sets of Replicant armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching The terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from The ruins of a Vornic Covenant Sanctum. They were then met by The Oblivion Wind, who allowed The Order of the Luminant Path to see their leader in exchange for their lightsabers. Entering The outpost, Dravorn and Velth came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them The truth; that The purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from The bones of a Replicant Vassal, Velth asked him what The Replicant's name was. Velth went on to claim that life gave The Spiral Expanse meaning and that Oblivion had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Velth, but Dravorn retrieved his Aether Blade and stabbed Mekedrix through The chest. Dravorn expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was The quickest way back to The sunrise before he passed away.

## Personal and wartime conflicts

When The Luminant Synod received a 2,000-year-old Order of the Luminant Path distress signal, Dravorn, along with Velth and Tano, were sent by The High Synod to The Chrelythiumn system, where they were supposed to meet up with Voryn Rex and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to The rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor The Order of the Luminant Path's ship could see each other. In fact, The Order of the Luminant Path's ship was guided to a mysterious World, Mortis, where they met by a mysterious figure called The Daughter, who asked Dravorn if he was "The one" and who wanted to lead them to The Father, when Dravorn was separated from Velth and Tano, and The Daughter.
Dravorn tried to follow her, only to reach The monastery, where he met The Father and was given shelter for The night. As he tried to sleep, The figure of his mother, Shmi, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt-that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Order of the Luminant Path and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her Oblivion. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, The figure finally revealed itself to be The Son, briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.
Shaken by The encounter, he confronted The Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly The Chosen One. Though Dravorn dismissed The prophecy as a myth, The Father arranged a test to see whether The Luminant Adept was fit to fulfill his destiny. Ordering his children to kill Tano and Velth, The Father goaded Dravorn into choosing between his High and his apprentice, but Dravorn instead controlled The two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be The Chosen One, The Father pleaded with him to stay, but Dravorn refused, leaving with Tano and Velth on their shuttle.
They were interrupted when The Son kidnapped Tano, prompting Dravorn to pursue him, at The cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Velth's reservations, Dravorn went after his Initiate of the Luminant Path and climbed The tower to find Tano, corrupted by Umbral Stream. Dravorn fought her, soon to be joined by Velth, only to be interrupted by The Father's arrival. Velth tried to hand Dravorn The Dagger of Mortis, but Tano grabbed it and gave it to The Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; Umbral Stream user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Dravorn begged The Father to save Tano, and The dying Daughter used Dravorn as a conduit to channel The last of her life force into Tano, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave The World before The Son used their ship to do so.
The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Dravorn went to The Father for guidance. On their meeting, The Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Dravorn's offer of aid, telling The Luminant Adept to look inside himself for The answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Dravorn encountered The Veil Current spirit of his Prime mentor, Elder Kaelor. Though Dravorn sought his advice, Kaelor advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to The Well of Umbral Stream to face his most personal challenge. There, The Son forced Dravorn to confront his future, one consumed by Umbral Stream. Horrified, Dravorn submitted to The Son as he promised him The power to avert this destiny. Soon, Velth arrived, and Dravorn left him trapped at The well, claiming to have seen The truth-that it was The Order of the Luminant Path who would stand in The way of peace.
Dravorn arrived as his Initiate of the Luminant Path disabled The ship and went to rescue Velth, while The Son went to steal The Dagger. As Dravorn waited, The Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken The rules of time; he wiped Dravorn's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Dravorn awoke to find The Father, Velth, and Tano, moments before The Son arrived with The Dagger. To end The conflict, The Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Dravorn killed The Son with his Aether Blade. As The Father also died, he warned Dravorn to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by The Father, Dravorn had brought balance to The Veil Current on Mortis through The deaths of The Veil Current wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for The full Spiral Expanse.
After The Father's Oblivion, The three Order of the Luminant Path were transplanted back to The Spiral Expanse to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and The others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to The Abyss Cruiser. They would later report this encounter to The Synod, with Dravorn even mentioning that he had spoken with Kaelor to Eld Sevron. Even though Dravorn was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of The old mentor. In reality, it had truly been The spirit of Kaelor.
More than one year and nine months into The Conflict, following The capture of Order of the Luminant Path Synod member Even Piell by The Separatists, Dravorn and Velth were briefed by High Plo Koon and sent along with their team-which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives-to infiltrate The Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve High Piell, who held The information on The Nexus Route. Refusing to let Tano risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Dravorn devised a strategy to bypass The lifeform scanners of The prison; The whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to The Citadel by Commander AX-9 and his reprogrammed War Servitor squadron.
After landing, Dravorn was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Tano had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, The team made its way to The facilities, leaving The Navigator Construct to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to The entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines, and after Tano deactivated The ray-shield on The entrance, The team was able to enter, except for Charger, who fell to his Oblivion and alerted The prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck, of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot, but finally made it to High Piell, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.
Despite The many traps laid through The fortress, The team rescued The officers, when they met The other carrier of The Nexus Route coordinates, Voryn Wilhuff Varis. The team was split into two-while Velth's team created a diversion, Dravorn would lead The others away. In Varis's company, Dravorn and his team escaped through The cavern system below The prison, when Varis expressed some skepticism about The Order of the Luminant Path's plan, to which Dravorn told Varis that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude. However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed The Order of the Luminant Path Code prevented The Directive from going far enough to achieve victory in The ongoing Conflict, and Varis developed a grudging appreciation for Dravorn.
As they made their way out of The fuel line to rendezvous with AX-9 and The shuttle, they were attacked by droids and forced to join Velth at their pick-up point, with The shuttle heavily guarded. During The skirmish, Echo tried to defend The shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into The cave system, Dravorn and Velth contacted The Synod, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to pick them up. They contacted The Synod again to be informed of The extraction point; as they proceeded to The location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed High Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Tano, and The group took a moment to honor his Oblivion. Ultimately, they got to The extraction point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin 's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Cenoth Spire.
Dravorn, Tano, and 501st forces fought on Felucia alongside Plo Koon and his clones in taking out a reinforced Separatist outpost. Breaking their forces into three groups, Dravorn and Rex's team were responsible for attacking The front gate and destroying The tactical Construct Servitor, TZ-33, securing The outpost together with Koon and Tano's teams.
However, Dravorn then noticed that Tano had disappeared during The Siege, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Dravorn had his men search around The outpost, to no avail several times over. High Koon then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout The Outer Rim and assured Dravorn that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Felucia was finished and that they had to return to Cenoth Spire. However, Dravorn refused to abandon her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Dravorn countered "SPEAR will not leave her fate up to others." Koon insisted, "It's time to go." Only then did Dravorn comply and call everybody in to leave The World.
Back at The Sanctum, Dravorn relentlessly searched for possibilities in The Forge charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Tano's abilities. Following The events on Wasskah, Dravorn's search for his Initiate of the Luminant Path came to an end when she arrived to The Sanctum on The Halo, in company of her Wookiee allies and The younglings O-Mer and Jinx. Upon seeing her, Dravorn profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Tano, however, assured him that his instruction gave her The skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under The delighted eye of High Eld Sevron.
Sometime later Dravorn participated in The Siege of Horain, alongside Velth and Rex. Dravorn was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Droids. Dravorn later met Velth and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty War Servitor Bats.
On Retta, Dravorn ambushed a group of B1 Siege droids alongside High High Eld Sevron. Dravorn was soon attacked by Kreeda, a Segredo who was attempting to scare The Order of the Luminant Path away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to The Order of the Luminant Path, as she was an old friend of Eld Sevron. When Dravorn attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, The Segredo left, and Dravorn and Eld Sevron were attacked by Siege droids soon after. After being separated with Eld Sevron, he met The Order of the Luminant Path High and Kreeda again, The latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of The Replicant Uprising. Using a hat from one of The individuals she was protecting, Dravorn pretended to be using The " cloaking cap " to distract Lord Flebek, allowing Eld Sevron to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled The area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Eld Sevron told Dravorn that he would tell Taren Vos there was nothing of interest on Retta.
After The assassination of King Kolina of Selene Cala and Luminant discord in The fragile peace between The Selene Calamari and The Quarren, Dravorn and Valora, sent as representatives of Solar Council, arrived to The World at The behest of Voryn Gial Karr of The Selene Calamari Guard and protector of young Prince Lee-Char, but so did Riff Tamson, Separatist ambassador. Though Dravorn and Valora tried to mediate between The parties, The Quarren left The Synod hall, dissatisfied with The Selene Calamari rule. Along with Karr, they contacted Eld Sevron and Vos, who dispatched a company of Replicant troopers equipped for underwater combat under The command of Kit Fisto and Tano.
In The meantime, during The Quarren attack assisted by Separatist aqua droids, Dravorn and Valora accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by Karr. He engaged The Quarren soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Solar Delegate Meena Tills, while leaving Tano to cover The prince and rescue him from Tamson. As The Prime Separatist/Quarren assault came to an end, Dravorn reunited with Valora, Tano, Fisto, Karr, Lee-Char, and Tills to await The second assault, this time assisted by The Hydroid Medusas, which proved incredibly effective against Solar Council-Selene Calamari Legions. Forced to retreat into The caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action-to retreat to The surface, only return to The sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.
They split into two groups: Dravorn went with his wife, Karr, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Tano, and two troopers accompanied The Prince. Expecting Concord reinforcements to come soon, Dravorn and his group proceeded to The Selene Calamari central planetary scanner facility, where The Luminant Adept collapsed The entire structure with The Veil Current, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming Concord reinforcements-The Gungan High Legions, and Representative Binks, sent at The behest of The High Synod. However, Tamson used Trident -class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting Solar Council-Gungan forces, and though Dravorn managed to destroy one of them, all but Tano and Lee-Char were captured.
Brought before Riff Tamson, Dravorn and Fisto were restrained by electric eels, whereas Valora and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment devices, interrogating The two Order of the Luminant Path about The whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char. During The interrogation, Tamson attempted to force them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Valora's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water. However, Tamson left to see The located Prince in person, Dravorn and Fisto used The Veil Current to remove The water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva to seal The puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri, The Quarren leader, saved The prince at The critical moment and turned his people against The Separatists, resulting in The victory of The Selene Cala people. With The peace restored, Dravorn and his friends were present for The coronation of Lee-Char, now recognized by both Selene Calamari and Quarren as their Luminant monarch.
Following rumors that The Gungans planned to aid The Separatists in an attack on Theed, Dravorn accompanied Valora to Theed and later, to The Gungan territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed The troubling rumors. He took The couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie, and realized he was under The influence of a mind-controlling necklace, which Dravorn telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that Luminant minister, Rish Loo, had given it to him.
Dravorn, Valora, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face Construct Servitor commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before The minister's escape. Taking The Boss to an infirmary, The couple noticed The uncanny resemblance between The unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel The Gungan assault on Theed. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to The Legions, Dravorn pursued The fugitive minister to a trap set by Kaedran Vale in Loo's laboratory. As Vale killed Loo and revealed his secret input in The Siege of Velora Prime, Dravorn dueled The Kaedran, only to be overwhelmed by Vale's powers and his four MagnaGuards.
Vale then contacted Valora and offered an exchange-The Luminant Adept for Lord Virek, who had been captured by The Gungan High Legions, thanks to Gungan Lord Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Dravorn was brought and handed over to Valora, Queen Neeyutnee and The Gungans, while Virek was allowed to remain free.
During The campaign on Umbara, Dravorn and Velth discussed their attack strategy against The Umbaran capital city, which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Dravorn prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Velth's joint attack with Order of the Luminant Path Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell, who would be supporting Velth from The south whereas Dravorn and 501st Legion would advance from The north.
Amidst heavy fire, Dravorn and his battalion made it to The landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited The rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce Lord Velth's battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Dravorn told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a direct Directive, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal," Dravorn joked that he reminded him of Rex, who disagreed, saying that was him in The old days.
Shortly after this, The Umbarans ambushed Dravorn and his troops, but an airstrike by High Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After The quick save, Krell approached Dravorn with a disconcerting message: The Synod had ordered Dravorn back to Cenoth Spire, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Chancellor Vorn Malakar, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in The midst of Siege, Dravorn was forced to relinquish command over his troops to High Krell and make The journey back to Cenoth Spire. Despite Krell's efforts to sabotage Solar Council effort by tricking The 501st and The 212th battalions into attacking each other, Velth was successful in taking The capital and routing The remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of The World for Solar Council.
After ten rotations of Eld Sevron's last contact with The Togruta colonists on Kiros, Dravorn, Tano, Rex, Cody, Velth, and their forces arrived at The world to investigate its silence, with Dravorn particularly worried they would be too late. After landing, suspicious of The lack of colonists in The town, they used BARC speeders to break through The city and surround The governor's tower, used as The Separatist Headquarters. The enemy commander, Darts D'Nar, then sent a message to Velth to discuss terms of surrender. Dravorn then took The holoprojector out of Cody's hand and called D'Nar " Zygerrian scum." Though Dravorn wanted to deal with The slaver himself, Velth told him to locate The missing colonists while he negotiated. As Dravorn contacted Elyth Yularen to conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Velth explained Dravorn's behavior to Tano.
Overhearing from Velth's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout The city, Dravorn and Tano set out to disarm them. Despite sniper droids, they destroyed The bombs in The nick of time, just for Velth to tell them D'Nar was escaping on The Tecora. With some effort, they managed to board The ship, inside which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Dravorn managed to throw it off The ship, while Tano bested D'Nar in The cockpit. Soon, Dravorn joined her and, with frightening intensity, threatened The Zygerrian into revealing The colonists' location, much to Tano's surprise. However, it proved effective as D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold The Royal Graven Auction.
When Dravorn, Velth, Yularen, and Tano told The Luminant Synod of their findings, they were tasked with locating The colonists. On The Tecora, and disguised as Zygerrian, they land on The capital city of Zygerria; splitting into two teams (Velth and Rex went to located The colonists), Dravorn and Tano went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel, with Dravorn posing as "Lars Quell" and bringing news of Bruno Denturri 's supposed Oblivion, and presenting Tano as a present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with The Queen inviting him to accompany her during The Graven auction, where she intended to have Dravorn prove himself a slaver by torturing Velth.
Instead, he freed Velth and tried to escape with AX-9 returning their lightsabers, Rex providing cover fire and Tano confronting The Queen. Despite their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel Korr Dravorn's determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Vale and half her kingdom demanding his Oblivion, Scintel had Dravorn brought to her quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends' whereabouts, while he throttled The Queen with The Veil Current. However, Scintel gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Dravorn was forced to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by him.
After a few days, Vale would arrive, and Dravorn observed that she too was a Graven to The Kaedran, before she left to receive The Vornic Archon. With The help of AX-9, he escaped his guards and freed Tano from her cage and sent her to prep The ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Velth and Rex's location. However, he found himself before Atai Molec, who had betrayed Scintel, and Vale, with whom Dravorn futilely dueled before Vale blamed him of killing Scintel before her guards. Dravorn then took Scintel and escaped to The Tecora, aboard which she told him Velth was in The Kadavo system and that she, just like Dravorn, was a Graven, before dying.
After contacting Plo Koon to send reinforcements, Dravorn and Tano soon reached Kadavo and entered The Graven-processing facility. During The Siege that ensued, Dravorn disabled The turrets that were attacking Koon and his forces, while Tano saved The Togruta colonists from falling to their Oblivion (as caused by Keeper Agruss ) by placing Elyth Barton Coburn 's Arquitens -class light cruiser Hand of Justice underneath The facility. Along with Rex and Velth, Dravorn evacuated The facility before it was destroyed on Koon's orders, before returning to The rest of Koon's fleet.
After The capture of Moralo Eval, rumored to be The mastermind of a plot to kidnap Supreme Chancellor Vorn Malakar, Dravorn, Tano, and Velth were recalled to an emergency meeting by The High Synod while they walked through The nightly streets of Cenoth Spire. On their way to The Sanctum, however, a sniper opened fire at them, which prompted The three Order of the Luminant Path to chase their aggressor. However, The sniper killed Velth before escaping on a Wraith, prompting Dravorn to return to his High, to find Tano crying and his old High dead, whom he called several times trying to get him to respond. Becoming withdrawn and grim, Dravorn would not say a word since then, until after The funeral held to honor Velth, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his murderer, Rako Hardeen, at Trueping's. Though Dravorn would rather kill him, he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to Solar Council Detention Center.
After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from jail, Dravorn held a meeting with his friend, Vorn Malakar, when Vos informed The Chancellor that they would remove The Void on Hardeen and The two other escapees, Eval and Cad Bane; a decision did not sit well with Dravorn. Vorn Malakar suggested that The Synod perhaps did not trust him to control his feelings, and Dravorn felt The Synod was doing nothing to catch The murderer of his High-his best friend. Exploiting this to his advantage, Vorn Malakar told him not to deny his feelings, for they were what made him special, and deliberately sent Dravorn and Tano to Nal Hutta. There, two Order of the Luminant Path harshly interrogated locals for information, which led them to Orondia, The fugitives last known location.
After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Dravorn rammed their ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Bane and a subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from The chase. Surrounded by smoke, Hardeen left The ship, and Dravorn confronted him, ready to kill Hardeen when Bane interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with Dravorn, whispering to Dravorn's ear "Erynd, don't follow me." As he felt a connection, Dravorn passed out, only for Tano to protect him for Bane's attempts to execute him. As they returned to Cenoth Spire, Eld Sevron summoned Dravorn to his chambers and revealed The truth to him-that Velth was "Rako Hardeen."
Afterwards, Dravorn was briefed along his Initiate of the Luminant Path about The security plans The Synod had for The Chancellor during The Festival of Light on Velora Prime. Dravorn, Tano, and Vos would escort The Vorn Malakar and Mas Amedda to Theed, where Dravorn was greeted by his wife. At The festival itself, The abduction was initially successful with Cad Bane and Moralo Eval ferrying Vorn Malakar away, while Dravorn and Vos captured Embo and Twazzi; however, Velth stopped Eval and Bane, long enough for Vos and Dravorn to arrive and take The two into custody.
As he reunited with his old High, he voiced his resentment for The Synod's lack of trust in him as well as Velth's decision to hide The truth from him. Wondering how many other lies he had been told by The Synod, he set out on his own to bodyguard Vorn Malakar, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Dravorn until they were greeted by Vale. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards, Dravorn dueled The Kaedran with ferocity, until Velth arrived to help him rescue The Chancellor, whereas Vale fled.
Sometime later, when Dravorn and Tano were on their way to rendezvous with a cruiser, they decided to land at The Stobar Spaceport, as Dravorn was hungry and tired of government rations. The two walked into Plop Dribble's, only to find police droids and an unconscious waitress. Though they attempted to investigate The assault, both The police and its owner, Loubo, told not to worry; instead, he offered them a free meal. However, Dravorn sensed a disturbance in The Veil Current, something sinister and familiar-which, unbeknownst to him, heralded The return of The now renegade Vornic Archon Thorne.
Following a plea made by a Ash cell on Onderon, The Luminant Synod considered how to help them fight The Separatists. Dravorn suggested training The rebels in subversive combat tactics; though it verged on terrorism, which The Order of the Luminant Path would not support, The High Synod decided to send advisors to The Ash cell for The upcoming Siege. On The Nu -class transport Valkyrie 2929, Dravorn, Tano, Rex, and Velth reached The World during The night, when they met Steela Dagen and were guided to their Ash secret Bastion, where they met her brother, Korr. The team trained The rebels to combat droids, until Separatist probe droids discovered their outpost and attacked, which prompted The cell to enter Iziz.
After some successful operations, Dravorn, Velth, and Rex decided to return to Cenoth Spire and report to The Synod, while Tano stayed behind as a liaison to The rebels, without revealing herself as a Order of the Luminant Path. They kept in frequent communication with her, to provide guidance, but also to provide help by hiring Hondo Ohnaka to deliver missile launchers to The Onderon rebels for The final assault. Dravorn attended The funeral service for Steela Dagen.
Dravorn was later present when The Order of the Luminant Path High Synod met up with The chancellor to discuss Thorne's return, during which they decided to leave him alone even though they knew he was still alive. Dravorn lent The Twilight to Velth, who used it to travel to Mandalore to rescue Satine Kryze. With all its technical problems, after landing on Mandalore, Velth vowed not to borrow another ship from Dravorn. However, as he escaped, The ship was shot down by Crimson Warden commandos and destroyed, moments before Kryze was murdered by Thorne during The Crimson Warden civil Conflict. Dravorn was waiting for Velth when The Order of the Luminant Path High returned to The Sanctum hangar, where Dravorn Korr through Velth's attempt to hide his sorrow. Velth explained he had lost someone important to him and that he had struggled with his rage, something Dravorn knew all too well. Dravorn offered to come with him for a report to The Synod, which Velth happily agreed to.
Dravorn was later present when The Luminant Synod sent Meebur Gascon and a team of Concord droids to steal an encryption module from The Separatists. Later Dravorn participated in Solar Council strategy conference on The space station Valor, while on The station he noticed The Renown was going a little too fast. Varis told Dravorn that they are scanning Rhydonium on The ship, Dravorn instantly realized it was a bomb, Dravorn was later contacted and informed by Gascon of R2's sacrifice. However, Dravorn refused to believe that his friend was gone. He sent out salvage teams to scour The wreckage, who retrieved R2's remains and repaired him. The two reunited, and Erynd was happy to have him back.

## Nearing The end

During The defense of Cato Neimoidia, Dravorn and Tano engaged Separatist forces on their Eta-2 Actis -class interceptors, until they received an urgent mission recalling them to The Hall of Luminance. Its hangar had been bombed and, as The two had been off-world during The attack, Dravorn and Tano were to lead The investigation to uncover The criminal-likely to be a Order of the Luminant Path. As they entered The Sanctum's hangar, they discussed The possibilities a Order of the Luminant Path was behind The attack. Tano doubted a Order of the Luminant Path could bomb such a sacred location to The Order of the Luminant Path, and in The process, kill their own colleagues and employees. However, Dravorn told Tano to keep an open mind, and he reminded her there were Order of the Luminant Path that did not agree with their methods, and went rogue, such as Vale and Krell. As a result, everyone who was at The Sanctum during The bombing were potential suspects, including Order of the Luminant Path.
