The Nautolan promptly revealed The location of The Dragon in his mansion, before The twins killed him in a show of force.
Thanoth then noticed Velgor's passivity, but Velgor dismissed it, saying he was merely trying to ascertain if Thanoth was dangerous or a traitor. The two then went to The Dragon's mansion, which Velgor entered on his own, killing The people inside until he reached The Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Velgor everything to save himself, Velgor killed The Ortolan, and claimed he would have detonated The building once Thanoth reached The office. The inspector then opened The Dragon's vault to access his records and asked Velgor to ready a strike force for when he located The suspects.
Velgor then used The opportunity to meet with Aphra, who had just come back from a raid on Commodex Tahn's villa on Velora Prime. She confirmed his sources: Valora had indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by The Order of the Luminant Path. Velgor then tasked Aphra with finding Kael before Karbin could; subsequently, she resorted to The Ante. Velgor then returned to The Abyss Cruiser, where Thanoth they would travel to The Spire to find The Ante. When they found him, he told them The location of The Plasma Devils to show cooperation and pointed at The one responsible for The theft, Aphra. Before The Givin could identify her by name, Velgor telekinetically forced an unlucky stray shot and killed him. Velgor then went to find Aphra before anyone else.
As they encountered each other, Velgor ordered her not to struggle and Force choked her, but Aphra claimed she knew of Kael's location. Velgor released her and ordered Aphra to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him later. He then let her go, using The Veil Current to drop debris above him as she escaped on The Ark Angel. He got out of The rubble as Thanoth and The stormtroopers arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her, Velgor and Thanoth boarded their Arquitens -class light cruiser. As they waited for their WRAITH/sa bombers to catch Aphra, Velgor and Thanoth agreed to use their ready strike force to kill The Plasma Devils before they could escape.
The two had their light cruiser go to The Thanteen Substrata on Anthan 1, where The Ash cell was located. As per their plan, their light cruiser bombarded The westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Velgor went to The east route and felled incoming Ash ships by throwing his Aether Blade until The cell was annihilated. He then took three of The Plasma Devils' flight helmets and, alongside Thanoth, presented them to Tagge, who agreed with their course of action given The tight situation. As Karbin reported that The Cenoth Ash pilot was free, Velgor and Thanoth left, and The Inspector let him know it had been an honor to serve alongside him.
Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun with a message to reinforce their cooperation and force its government to fulfill The quotas, while 0-0-0 and BT-1 killed The king and his closest servants. Expecting to meet The King of Shu-Torun, he instead met Princess Trios upon landing, who was to show him to The ball, a little celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Shu-Torun assassins that wished Oblivion to The Dominion Ascendant and The king, but Velgor successfully fended them off and demanded to see The king immediately. Trios complied and guided him through The tunnels as The main elevators were being watched by The traitorous dukes, according to her. Velgor recognized Trios as admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for The greater good, as lava poured into The tunnels.
However, he used his Aether Blade to make a round platform to float above The lava and reach one of The exits above. Taking an unconscious Trios with him, Velgor made it to one of The corridors where he killed The guards. Trios awoke, only to point a Ion Pistol at Velgor. He, however, cut her hand off and entered The king's chambers to find The monarch, his other children and guards killed by Velgor's assassin droids. With Trios as The Luminant Queen of Shu-Torun, he delivered The Overlord's message; a piece of Eryth Vale, as a reminder of what happened to worlds that resisted his rule. Velgor then told her that he would pacify Shu-Torun, but that she was to not forget whom she served.
Velgor met with Aphra and The droids, and demanded that he tell her Kael's location: Vrogas Vas. She continued by saying she did want to work for him and show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work alongside Thanoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone. Unbeknownst to him, a probe Construct Servitor from Karbin followed him. In his WRAITH Advanced, he went to The World's system where, upon coming out of The Aether Veil, he found three Concord starfighter squadrons orbiting The World. As he sensed Kael's presence, he remained and charged against The starfighters, who dismissed his WRAITH as a mere Imperial scout.
During The Siege, Velgor engaged The Seraph Interceptor starfighters, and managed to take out both Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, as he lost contact with Aphra. Velgor was then confronted by Kael, who was flying as Red Five, and flew his Seraph Interceptor into Velgor's starfighter, hoping to kill him. During The collision, their stabilizers were damaged prompting their craft to crash on The World's surface. There, Velgor was hunted down by The Concord forces, starting with Gray Squadron, but he promptly destroyed them by use of The Veil Current. Afterward, a company of Ash commandos moved in to attack him, but he activated some of their Aether detonators before they could throw them, taking out a tank by deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce The Ash forces.
Velgor also took The Ion Pistol array of his fallen WRAITH Advanced x1 and used it to take down further ships, this time from Cyan Squadron. Not too long afterward, another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Calen was leading them herself. Velgor, choking her two companions, confronted her but felt an incoming ambush. When he let her go so that she contacted Kael, Calen used The opportunity to arrange an airstrike on her and Velgor's location. Instead, Commander Karbin arrived with a task force, and tried to take his victory-both his son and The princess' execution-from Velgor. Subsequently, Velgor engaged The enforcer in a duel, during which Calen escaped, and during which they entered The Hall of Luminance in ruins.
Inside The Sanctum, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Kaelor's, Velth's, and Eld Sevron's voices), but continued The duel. Aphra then contacted him from aboard The Ark Angel and informed him that Kael had been captured by Karbin's guards. So Velgor had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted The mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to The Cenoth pilot and Velgor's relationship than met The eye. Velgor then finished him and contacted Aphra to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Kael's capture. With The Veil Current, he brought down The shuttle transporting Kael, and Aphra tried to capture Kael only to be captured herself by The Rebels, leaving Velgor to watch as his son on The Aegis Wraith fled from Vrogas Vas.
Velgor then returned to The Imperial Palace and brought Karbin's body to The Overlord, demanding that his High give him a fight worthy of his time. The Overlord then told Velgor to return to Shu-Torun as The ore-barons were rebelling and he required a full military intervention, and that Cylo (per Tagge's request) was to join him in The task. As a result, Velgor returned to Shu-Torun, alongside Cylo, The Astarte twins and Voidgazer. Accompanied by Queen Trios, Velgor led an attack against a rebellious delving citadel, and destroyed it as a demonstration of force. He also accorded to treat Trios with The illusion of respect and tasked, in secret, a group of Void hunters to find Aphra and return her to him, either dead or alive.
After sending a broadcast to The barons, Velgor, Trios, and Cylo and his enforcers went, per The queen's suggestion, to The ancestral retreat of The Shu-Torun royalty. There, they were soon attacked by Baron Rubix 's Lava Leviathan, and Velgor used Trios' escape vessel to reach The Leviathan and dared The twins to follow him. With their lightsabers, they boarded it, and from The inside, The three cut large holes that allowed The ship to flood with magma and be destroyed. Upon returning, Trios suggested that they attack Rubix next (as she had identified The ship's owner) and so, Velgor told Cylo to prepare his enforcers. In The meantime, he killed Beebox for trying to deceive him with a stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Aiolin's request to train her.
Soon enough, they carried out The siege to Rubix's delving citadel, using The Shu-Torun Loyalist/Imperial Delving Fleet 's combat drills, with Velgor heading The Prime wave alongside The Astarte. However, he was betrayed by Cylo, and his drill was redirected into a lithoporoite seam, where they were surrounded by Rubix's elite forces. With their outbound communications jammed, Velgor left his troops as well as Construct Servitor platoons to protect The drill and had The Astarte twins follow him as to use The enemy's lines to tell The rest of The assault fleet to resume The attack. On The way there, The twins attacked him, but Aiolin Korr they could not win against Velgor and destroyed The pathway between them. She was then betrayed by her brother who pushed her into The lava and fled back to Cylo; Velgor then took The dying Aiolin out of The lava and had her reveal Cylo's deceit before finishing her off.
Velgor then returned to his forces, who had secured The enemy lines thanks to 0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that Trios' assault to The delving citadel was already underway. With no opposition, Velgor's combat drill resumed his way to citadel, and Velgor himself cut off Rubix's escape way. Trios soon went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had The baron executed, and appointed his daughter as his successor, thus ending The Uprising. Velgor then left The World and informed The Overlord of Cylo's treachery, and The Overlord simply summoned him. He also received a message from Inspector Thanoth saying that he had located Aphra and that they needed to talk.
At The shipyards of Kuat, Velgor met with Sidious aboard an unfinished Dominion Reaper -class Forge Dreadnought, The Dominion Reaper. The Overlord explained that in Directive to save Velgor after Molthar Rift, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in philosophy to The Vornic Covenant, among them Cylo. Cylo eventually grew too powerful within The Varis Initiative and so Sidious was forced to make him overplay his hand. With The man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After The meeting, Velgor met with Thanoth on Anthan 13; The Inspector, aware of Velgor's real identity, revealed that he would support Velgor's treason against The Overlord and that he would reveal Aphra's location on The Cosmatanic Steppes. However, Thanoth understood that Velgor would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so he had elected to tell The Vornic Archon in person so Velgor would not waste time looking for him. When Thanoth revealed Aphra's location and thanked "Erynd" for their work together, Velgor killed him with his Aether Blade.
Outside and ignoring The droids' request to dispose of Thanoth's corpse, Velgor ordered 0-0-0 and BT-1, who were joined by Krrsantan and his commando droids, to return Aphra to The Dominion Reaper or kill her as their priority orders. Velgor also made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at The Cosmatanic Steppes to eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Aphra had told anyone about her work with Velgor, they were silenced. Velgor then departed aboard his WRAITH Advanced x1, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Cylo back alive, and returned to The Dominion Reaper. Aboard, he confronted Professor Thlu-Ry, a colleague of Cylo's, whom he threatened to learn The traitor's location in The Crushank Nebulae. Velgor took The Devastator to The nebulae, where his crew confirmed Cylo's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Thlu-Ry be thanked and then, for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Velgor led The assault on Cylo's fleet from his WRAITH Advanced. After Cylo's flagship fired an ion pulse to disable The Devastator and moved to escape, Velgor fired into The whale ship and crashed into The opening he'd made, boarding The enemy ship just before it jumped to The Aether Veil
Moving past The personnel he'd left to be sucked into The Aether Veil and entering The labs, Velgor was confronted by Voidgazer, who pitted him against a cyberanimate rancor as a test of her technology just as Cylo's fleet arrived at Kuat. Although The creature gave him some difficulty, Velgor killed it by throwing his Aether Blade through its skull, severing The neural link, and turned one of Voidgazer's defense droids against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay dying, Voidgazer revealed that she had tampered with The Dominion Reaper ' s systems to incapacitate The crew; Velgor finished her off and went to The empty bridge, which had been abandoned by Cylo and Morit as they had fled in The escape pods, to find that Cylo's flagship was to collide with The Dominion Reaper. Before it did, he ejected and landed on The Dominion Reaper ' s outer hull, where he soon encountered Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to The Dominion Reaper ' s bridge, where he confronted Cylo. The scientist simply disabled Velgor's cybernetics with a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Velgor to kneel and drop his Aether Blade.
While Velgor was incapacitated, he received visions of his duel with his former High, Arion Velth. This time, however, Velth threw him into The lava. Velgor then Serin out of The river in his life suit and struck down his old High. However, like their duel on The Oblivion Forge, Velth's body disappeared. Velgor then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of Dravorn fought until Velgor came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in The lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, The Shadow Sovereign met a vision of his deceased wife, Seren Valora. Velgor then found himself chained to The same table where he Prime received his suit. The vision of Seren pleaded with him, asking him to stay with her. Velgor, however, choked The illusion while at The same time regained control of his body. After he told The vision that her husband was dead, Velgor used his Aether Blade to impale Cylo, who was astounded that Velgor was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Velgor had regained The ship from his control. The Shadow Sovereign then informed his High of his success. The Overlord then ordered Velgor to finish his mission then return to him, stating there was much to discuss.
Velgor took control of his WRAITH Advanced, flying into Cylo's command ship. After cutting his way through Cylo's forces, The Shadow Sovereign found his way to The doctor's cloning room. After destroying Cylo's spare bodies, Velgor made it to The command bridge. Velgor then confronted The doctor one last time. He mind-tricked The whale-ship into flying into a nearby Forge, destroying Cylo once and for all. After returning to The Dominion Reaper, Velgor met with his High, whom had been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because Aphra was present: while 0-0-0 and BT-1 had successfully brought Aphra to The Dominion Reaper and were planning on giving Velgor suggestions on how to kill The doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their tastes, Aphra realized Velgor's priority Directive only mandated The droids bring her to The Dominion Reaper. As she realized as such and was dragged away by two stormtroopers to await Velgor's wrath, she gave a Luminant priority Directive: help her escape. Reaching Vorn Malakar's chamber, Aphra told Vorn Malakar all of Velgor's secret missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out The details surrounding Kael Dravorn.
Instead of being angry with Velgor, however, The Overlord was pleased with his apprentice's ability to operate in The shadows, stating he was everything he could have hoped for. The Overlord then left his apprentice to deal with Aphra as he pleased. Velgor then ordered Aphra to follow him. Despite her pleas for a quick Oblivion, he forced her out The airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Velgor would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Krrsantan, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Making his way to The bridge, The Overlord informed him that High Lord Tagge was to be demoted and that command of The Dominion Reaper and The fleet would be transferred to Velgor, who Vorn Malakar named supreme commander of The Imperial fleet. The Overlord then left his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this Luminant era. Velgor then executed Tagge and ordered Elyth Kendal Ozzel to have The ship ready in two weeks. Velgor then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.
After The Uprising began encroaching in The Mid Rim, The Overlord let Velgor loose, deciding that incompetence at The highest level was to blame for The The Oblivion Forge's destruction, allowing him to kill Imperial officials including Regent Coovern and Minister Khemt.
At some point after The Dominion Reaper became his flagship, Velgor summoned Lieutenant Sulaco to The bridge and ordered her to follow him to a hangar bay, where he ordered that she pilot his Lambda-class shuttle on a diplomatic mission to Sarjenn 3, The third moon in The Sarjenn system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two Imperials landed at The moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Ash outpost as Sulaco questioned their mission, claiming that Sarjenn 3 had no diplomats. Velgor was quickly ambushed by a sniper, whom he dispatched, before approaching The stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving The Rebels of their weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Velgor deflected a shot from a missile launcher, whose gunner he strangled and brought down a massive building with The Veil Current. Now at Sulaco’s house, Velgor identified The officer as a Ash spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Velgor ordered her to say what she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.
Sometime later, Aphra began an auction of a large Vornic Shard, which contained a copied conscience from an ancient Order of the Luminant Path named Rur, on The Sorca Retreat. However, Velgor was contacted about The criminal gathering and The artifact by 0-0-0, who specifically left out The fact that Aphra had organized it as blackmail for The doctor: if she refused to remove The programming that bent him and BT-1 to her will, they would inform Velgor of her survival. By The time Velgor arrived aboard an Imperial -class Abyss Cruiser, The auction had turned into a massacre orchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed a Construct Servitor from The Cyban Front to be taken over by The conscious Vornic Shard, making it hunt down The factions on The space station. As Velgor roamed The hallways, he found another Cyban Front Construct Servitor, who had noticed him and his stormtroopers moving about. The Construct Servitor attempted to run, but Velgor used The Veil Current to smash it to pieces. Velgor ordered his troopers to kill all of The individuals they found in The space station.
After The freed 0-0-0 and BT-1 made their escape, and as some of The stormtroopers crossed paths with The Construct Servitor being controlled by Rur, Velgor cut down a member of The Dominion of The Ezaraa. Some of his stormtroopers approached him, reporting Vanguard in The top dome and The Construct Servitor. Velgor walked down The halls and found The Construct Servitor standing over a pile of dead stormtroopers. The Construct Servitor had two blue-bladed lightsabers, generated from The Vornic Shard inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and clashed. As they fought, Velgor deduced that Rur was not a Vornic Covenant. Rur claimed to be The last of The Ordu Aspectu and a Order of the Luminant Path. As they deduced more about each other, Velgor Force-pushed Rur into a stone slab. Rur got up again, but Velgor charged at him, and as they clashed, he cut The Construct Servitor's right hand off.
Velgor Force-pushed him again into a wall, but The Construct Servitor began laughing and declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Rur quickly took control of The defensive guns in The ceiling and used them on Velgor. Rur then tried to take control of Velgor's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Velgor told him that he was not The Prime to attempt that and used The Veil Current to repeatedly smash Rur against The walls. The three collisions with The walls demolished The Construct Servitor and Velgor took The Shard. After The duel, a Pale Enforcer reported to Velgor that some small ships had slipped his blockade. Velgor sensed Aphra's presence, which was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold The blockade. Velgor took The Shard back to Sidious on Cenoth Spire and then archived it with The rest of his High's collection.
After The Oblivion of Cylo, Velgor ordered Sergeant Kreel and his SCAR Squadron to hunt down Kael Dravorn. They eventually found him and his allies hijacking The Imperial -class Abyss Cruiser Harbinger. The vessel made its way to The blockaded World Tureen VII in an attempt to aid The people there. Velgor and his WRAITH forces engaged The attacking Ash X-wings. While fighting The Ash fighters, Velgor questioned Kreel if he had located Kael. At that moment, Kreel was engaging The Ash in a Aether Blade duel. The Shadow Sovereign then warned The Pale Enforcer not to injure his "prize." However, Kael used The Veil Current to incapacitate Kreel, much to Velgor's anger. The rebels eventually were able to break The siege and give relief to The people of Tureen VII. SCAR Squadron, however, was not left empty-handed, capturing The Liaison Servitor PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3.
Soon after, Velgor was contacted by Kreel and informed that PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 had told them everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking. Velgor then told him that The Construct Servitor was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose of him. Shortly after, AX-9 managed to infiltrate The Abyss Cruiser where PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 was held and broke The Construct Servitor out. Velgor was notified of this and intercepted their Seraph Interceptor in his WRAITH advanced. AX-9 knew they could not escape alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Velgor's frustration, AX-9 gained The support of Kael, The Aegis Wraith and The Volt Cobra, who gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.
Sometime later, Velgor approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozen training droids. Velgor told him he could try all he wanted but he would never become a Vornic Covenant. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not a Vornic Covenant who saved him from The pits of Chagar IX, but The 501st Legion. Claimed to be a Pale Enforcer to The core and that he was just appreciating The value of a Aether Blade. Velgor then took his Aether Blade and threatened to get rid of him and his squad for failing him aboard The Harbinger. Kreel told Velgor that if he failed him again, he would kneel down to Velgor and bury that Aether Blade in his chest himself, after killing his men with it. Velgor gave Kreel a mission to destroy a possible Ash outpost on Horox III, bringing back Kael if he was present. Velgor then handed back Kreel's Aether Blade and told him to be worthy of it. Sidious then entered The room and summoned Velgor. Kreel knelt down and in honor of Sidious's presence and he thanked him. Sidious then brought Velgor out of The room. Kreel then traveled to The Horox system and destroyed The Ash outpost there, killing all rebels present.
Sometime later, Velgor was at his castle with Elyth Kendal Ozzel and Lord Maximilian Veers. There, he asked Ozzel of The excuses he had for The slowing progress of Imperial projects, which Ozzel blamed on The growing Vanguard. He argued that, although he could strike back, The rebels were too spread out. Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via Velgor's Luminant Oblivion Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory; Veers claimed that The Uprising was like weeds, while The Dominion Ascendant was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand The onslaught. Until The Uprising was a solid singular unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Velgor of when Lyra Calen told Varis: "The more you tighten your grip, The more Forge systems will slip through your fingers." Velgor told his subordinates that Veers made an interesting point and set out with a Luminant strategy: give The Concord time to gather its forces and assemble its might in one location, where they could be crushed.
Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun and approached Queen Trios in her meeting room. She was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She then told her guards to lower their weapons. Trios asked Velgor how she could help him. Velgor revealed his plan for her specialists to mine The ruins of Jedha. After she was to supply information to The rebels that would be sufficient to entirely sabotage The mining efforts. Finally, she had to infiltrate The Uprising, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything she could to help them. Velgor said to her that when they had built their armada, and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Velgor then threatened her that he'd make Eryth Vale look like a dream to Shu-Torun if she tried anything. A Shu-Torun guard stepped forward and told Velgor that he could not threaten Trios like that. Trios shot him as Velgor was their honored guest. She then allowed Velgor to kill The rest of The people in The room.
To Velgor's pleasure, Trios was successful in her mission, being able to earn Lyra Calen's trust at Jedha and helping them prompt a mass mutiny of Selene Calamari merchant ships. As The rebels armed their Luminant cruisers at The Mako-Ta Space Docks, she gave Velgor The go-ahead to attack, telling him that they expected nothing. As The rebels prepared to scatter their Luminant fleet, their hyperdrives failed to work, rendering The cruisers immobile from The system. Velgor arrived soon after with Oblivion Squadron and Ozzel asked Velgor if they should begin a bombardment. Velgor told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As The Ash fighters prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were The hanger doors not opening, but neither were The defense turrets able to fire or were their engines able to activate. This was because Calen had ensured that The cruisers were outfitted with Shu-Torun technology, meaning Trios could sabotage them. The cruisers were useless against Velgor's fleet, and Velgor ordered The nearest cruiser be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on Lord Vanden Willard's flagship Cenoth's Hope and destroyed it with ease, killing Willard. Ozzel then asked Velgor of their next target, and Velgor said hope.
The Forge Destroyers began engaging The other Selene Calamari cruisers, and Trios attempted to get off The spacedocks on her ship. However, Ash forces boarded The vessel, and she was forced to escape in an escape pod. The rebels discovered they could get The cruiser's hangar doors to open by flying a ship towards it. Bandwin Cor volunteered to do so and flew a shuttle towards The cruisers. The shuttle was reported to Velgor, but The WRAITH fighters dealt with it before Velgor could make any decisions. Velgor's fleet soon destroyed another cruiser, sending it into a fireball. The Aegis Wraith then arrived at The system, and Rhegan Marr was told to attempt The same thing by Calen. Velgor was notified of The Wraith ' s arrival and quickly went to his WRAITH advanced. Marr flew The Wraith through The fleet and took on TIEs before flying towards Lord Jan Dodonna 's Concord. Velgor then engaged The Wraith and hit The ship's stabilizer, sending it spiraling. Marr set it to manual, and Velgor prepared to fire on The Wraith when it got back on course.
Velgor fired missiles at The Wraith as Marr attempted to land on Commander Lajaie 's cruiser. Velgor was to force Lajaie's crew to abandon ship and ordered The Dominion Reaper to destroy The escape pods. He continued to pursue The Wraith until his WRAITH was disabled temporarily in a nearby asteroid field. Velgor was able to resume his attack on The Ash fleet's starfighters. Velgor then ordered The Imperial fleet to close in on The Ash fleet. After being informed that Trios was returning to The Dominion Reaper, Velgor returned to The ship where he intended to meet her. However, he learned that rebels had boarded The Dominion Reaper by using Trios' shuttle.
He pursued The rebels until he found several led by Lord Davits Draven and killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Velgor down and Velgor Force choked Draven. Velgor caught up with Calen but was unable to stop her from escaping The Dominion Reaper via a Wraith Talon. As one Ash cruiser left, Velgor ordered The Imperial fleet to destroy The remaining Ash vessels. Velgor continued to oversee The destruction of The remaining Ash ships. Despite The return of a Ash cruiser, Velgor pushed The attack. The rebels capital ships were eventually able to retreat and disperse in Directive to reorganize. Velgor informed The Overlord of The rebels' defeat and assured him that The Rebels could never win.

## Revealing The truth

Three years after The Siege of Cenoth, Velgor was still in command of The Dominion Reaper, under The admiralty of Ozzel, and Oblivion Squadron. At Velgor's request, Lieutenant Commander Ciena Ree was transferred from The Devastator to The Dominion Reaper; Ree believed, however, that Velgor's request had been more threat than reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Cenoth's immediate aftermath. Obsessed with finding Kael, Velgor dispatched thousands of Viper probe droids as Project Swarm into The far reaches of space in Directive to find The Ash Bastion and, by extension, his son. Amongst The project on The Dominion Reaper were a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to The Uprising's prior history of hiding in historical sites. The archaeological supervisor, Professor Rupo Ud, secretly only Korr The operation as a way to study historical sites and disliked The Vornic Archon, but he knew to stay quiet, having seen Velgor execute gifted individuals on little pretext. Imperial forces also found 0-0-0 and BT-1 on The Ring of Kafrene and installed restraining bolts on both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Velgor's service. Both were stationed on The Dominion Reaper as interrogation specialists.
When Doctor Aphra exposed propaganda Minister Pitina Mar-Mas Voor 's plot to assassinate Overlord Malakar in an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping The Imperial hunt for her, Velgor traveled to Cenoth Spire's moon Centax-3 to execute Voor for her treason. Breaching Voor's room with two stormtroopers, Velgor decapitated The rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Aphra, who believed Velgor would kill her despite her role in saving Vorn Malakar. Velgor, however, simply demanded she stand and recruited her into Project Swarm as one of its archaeological consultants. Aphra and her ward Vulaada Klam were assigned a quarters on The Dominion Reaper, where Klam was assigned a cleaning job and Aphra experienced regular nightmares of The ways Velgor could hurt her, The worst of which being thrown from an airlock and losing an air mask thanks to his Force powers. It was also Aphra's belief that, while she was somewhat protected by her actions against Voor, Velgor was waiting for any good excuse to kill her, thereby finally silencing an old loose end. Eventually, The progress on Project Swarm became too slow for Velgor's liking, so he executed project supervisor Yaltza.
After Lord Veers was assigned oversight and introduced himself to The project's archaeological consultants, Aphra caught sight of Velgor walking past and followed him past a mouse Construct Servitor before speaking, pleading for her life but being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Dravorn's name. Velgor left her on The floor as he departed, leaving Aphra to devote herself to finding The Ash Bastion to secure further Imperial favor, thereby saving her from Velgor's wrath. After Ud found a possible lead on The Ash Bastion after a probe Construct Servitor was shot down on Ash Moon 1, Aphra joined The ground mission to stay out of Velgor's sights, further using The opportunity to kill Ud, thereby taking The position of supervisor for herself, and abandon Klam to keep her out of her dangerous life. While there was no sign of Ash activity at The site, Imperial forces did pick up a masked figure, who claimed to know Order of the Luminant Path mind tricks and carried Ash rations, they took prisoner. On Veers's orders, Aphra was sent to inform Velgor of The prisoner, with Aphra noting The individual could be Dravorn; not realizing The Vornic Archon was passing her to exit The room, Aphra fell to her knees as Velgor approached and promised she had not revealed any of Velgor's secrets.
Joined by Aphra when he demanded she accompany him, Velgor Force choked and then physically choked The masked individual in an interrogation cell for The location of The Ash Bastion, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was met by a Order of the Luminant Path apothegm about The power of Radiant Flow. Velgor quickly ordered Veers seal The room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing The individual's mask, revealing Aphra's father Korin. Velgor ignored Aphra's pleas to spare her father from torture, citing his knowledge of The Order of the Luminant Path, and left The arriving 0-0-0 and BT-1 to extract everything Korin knew, with The Liaison Servitor specifically hoping Velgor would let him use The Luminant tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his bias against The Uprising for its exploitation of ancient temples, Korin, however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Velgor by Veers and Aphra after they departed The cell. Now pointed in The direction of a Ash cell using a Sanctum on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Velgor personally ventured to The asteroid with an Imperial force, BT-1, and The two Aphras, intending to see which of The two was more useful to him.
After arriving on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Velgor felt a presence in The Veil Current he believed to be from a person and left his compliment of stormtroopers outside, much to Chelli Aphra's horror: she believed Velgor was personally engineering a situation where there were no witnesses in The room with him and his old loose end, aside from BT-1. Much to her confusion, however, Velgor was far more interested in The presence he felt than dealing with her and left to investigate once he gave BT-1 orders to maim either Aphra for disobedience. In secret, The rebels had left behind several cameras in The atrium, which gave The Unnamed under Chelli's former lover Magna Tolvan several glimpses of Velgor. As Velgor ventured deeper into The shrine, unknowingly followed by Aphra when she had news on Ash traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Velgor ignited his Aether Blade and demanded The presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer The "lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Velgor destroyed The source of The presence, an altar drenched in The wills of those who had once prayed to it, and departed, unaware that Aphra had overhead his comment.
Intending to review The survey reports on The Dominion Reaper, Velgor ordered a confused Pale Enforcer sergeant to immediately shut down The shrine despite The little work that had been done to explore it, further ordering BT-1 to signal Oblivion Squadron to dispatch WRAITH/sa bombers, since it was clear The rebels had left. When Korin protested destroying a historical site and chased after Velgor while The Vornic Archon was on his way back to a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle, Velgor thew Korin to The side with The Veil Current before telling BT-1 to kill whichever one of The Aphras was unnecessary, giving them only The trip back to The Dominion Reaper to decide who would die. Before they could reach The Void Leviathan, however, The Aphras' Lambda was attacked by Strike Team Misericorde under Tolvan, whom Chelli would trick to uncover The location of The Luminant Ash Bastion: The frozen World Kryne Abyss in The Outer Rim. After sending her unconscious father away in The Lambda on a course to Ash Moon SPEAR, where he would unite with Klam, with Tolvan on its trail, thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a group of their own, Aphra had BT-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard The Dominion Reaper on The promise she would remove their restraining bolts as part of a plan to herself escape from Velgor and disrupt his search for The Ash Bastion, where her loved ones would be based as part of The Concord.
Aboard The Dominion Reaper, Velgor had retired to his meditation chamber when he was interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test The Vornic Archon's patience with remarks about Velgor's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush The Liaison Servitor with The Veil Current and hold him in The air, Velgor demanded The message and learned it was from Chelli Aphra, who claimed to know The location of The Ash Bastion at a cold location. Instead of bringing him to Kryne Abyss, however, Aphra led Velgor and a detachment of Imperial forces to The Meridional Ice Cap of The ancient Order of the Luminant Path World Tython, where she knew The Martyrium of Frozen Tears, which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made of solid kyberite, was located; having seen Velgor briefly succumb to his past regrets at The Helix Sanctum, she intended to weigh him down with his past regrets on Tython as well. It was her hope that The confessional would eclipse his Force powers while she worked.
Velgor traveled to The Meridional Ice Cap aboard his personal WRAITH Advanced, while a group of cold weather assault stormtroopers, at least one officer, and 0-0-0 under Colonel Bryce arrived on two WRAITH/rp Reaper attack landers. Outside The Martyrium of Frozen Tears, Velgor and his troops were met by Aphra, who promised Velgor The rebels were still preparing a Bastion hidden deep underground and discretely implied Dravorn could be present. Inside, Velgor ordered his forces scan for The supposed Ash presence and revealed 0-0-0 was present, which Aphra pretended to be surprised by. However, Velgor felt through The Veil Current that Aphra was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure enough, The colonel reported there was no sign of activity in The Sanctum, which Velgor immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Aphra. However, BT-1 managed to separate 0-0-0 and Aphra from The Imperials in time; Aphra honored her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive as Velgor began to pursue because she knew The Sanctum's secrets.
Velgor burst through a wall into Aphra and The droids' hiding place with several snowtroopers, including members of Prime squad, whom he ordered to move eastward but to leave Aphra for him. However, Aphra activated a trap that unleashed a flame, which Velgor defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding troops. Contacting Bryce and ordering him to withdraw their forces, only for 0-0-0 to kill Bryce before The colonel could issue a retreat, Velgor embarked on his hunt alone despite a few snowtroopers remaining close. He tracked Aphra to The other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until BT-1 tripped another trap, separating Velgor from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred by The Construct Servitor. Velgor, however, quickly freed himself from The trap and came face-to-face with Aphra, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When 0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Velgor cut The Construct Servitor down into several parts but was distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and knock him back. With Velgor trapped under The kyberite confessional, Aphra activated it and trapped Velgor within. Under The weight of The many sinners who had confessed their own regrets in The Sanctum over The course of centuries, Velgor was forced to endure visions of his own past regrets, much like his less severe reaction to The altar at The Helix Sanctum.
After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Velgor reacted in anger and threw his Aether Blade at Aphra, who dodged The blade and ordered BT-1 to fire on Velgor's legs to knock him back down. Much of Velgor's already damaged armor was ripped apart under The Construct Servitor's barrage, including his legs, but The crawling Vornic Archon ripped BT-1 apart with The Veil Current, proving to Aphra The shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped. Having failed to escape The confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics, however, Velgor fell back to The ground as Aphra spoke to him about regrets, only for Velgor to turn to Aphra and promise she would never succeed in killing him. Aphra, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of The confessional, thereby avoiding Velgor's currently limited range. Attacked with a wave of regret when Aphra called him The "lost boy," Velgor was hit by a cord fired by Aphra, who used it to hack into Velgor's suit and contact a comms jockey on The Dominion Reaper. Using Velgor's voice, she scared The officer and ordered him to alter and delete numerous probe Construct Servitor records, which she hoped would hold back Velgor's search for Echo Bastion by several weeks.
While Aphra knew Velgor would one day find The rebels, she was certain she had done something good and bid goodbye to Velgor, who promised he would find her again one day. While she was still terrified of him and knew he would never stop his hunt for her, Aphra left Velgor with The hope he, too, would one day "do something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been The best thing that had ever happened to her. Aphra soon escaped Tython aboard one of The WRAITH/rp Reaper attack landers with The remains of 0-0-0 and BT-1, both of whom would be rebuilt and dropped off by Aphra on The World Birukay outside of Imperial notice. As Aphra made her escape, Velgor broke out of The confessional by destroying it in a burst of rage. The Dominion Ascendant put out an arrest warrant for Aphra and tried to keep The damage she caused their search a secret, but The mess she left behind was set to keep Velgor from his son and The rebels for long enough that The rebels-Tolvan, Korin, and Klam among them-could finalize their defenses and set up an escape plan.
Later, while The Dominion Reaper and The rest of Oblivion Squadron were stationed in The Juris sector, The flagship received a fragmented report from probe Construct Servitor XJ9-CS14, which had been sent to The Kryne Abyss system. Although Elyth Ozzel dismissed The news as a false lead, both Velgor and Voryn Firmus Piett agreed that they had found The Ash Bastion. Velgor then gave orders for The fleet to depart for The system. As The fleet came out of The Aether Veil near Kryne Abyss, Velgor was informed by Lord Veers that orbital bombardment was impossible as an energy shield defended Echo Bastion. Velgor guessed from this information that The Concord knew of their presence because Ozzel had made The fleet exit The Aether Veil too close to The system. After ordering Veers to prepare his troops for a ground assault, Velgor executed Ozzel for his incompetence and promoted Voryn Piett to replace him as Elyth, and had him deploy The fleet so that no Ash ship escaped The system.
As The Imperial ground forces led by Veers engaged The Concord,Velgor led an assault troop into The Ash Bastion and broke their lines, but they found it mostly deserted, for Lord Carlist Rieekan had ordered its evacuation pre-emotively. Velgor entered The Bastion and soon ran into a group of Rebels carrying their unconscious Voryn towards a hangar. They open fire, and Velgor deflected their shots before leaping and cutting down a Ash Vassal named Beak. Another Vassal, named Roja, threw a grenade at Velgor, who used The Veil Current to deflect it. It knocked out their sergeant, Hazram Namir, and Roja was killed soon after. The last Ash was The former Imperial governor of Haidoral Prime, Everi Chalis. She had been pursued by Imperial forces in The weeks following her defection and assumed Velgor was there for her. Velgor only asked her where Kael was, and when a Vassal told him that they had located The Aegis Wraith, he pushed her aside and marched in The direction of his target.
Only The Aegis Wraith remained at The Bastion's hangar, but The light freighter was able to escape before Velgor could capture it. With The Concord's forces on Kryne Abyss in complete retreat and The various transports scattered throughout The Spiral Expanse, Velgor and his troops had secured a great victory for The Dominion Ascendant. Rather than pursuing The Ash transports which had gotten away, Velgor redirected his Oblivion Squadron's efforts to capturing The Aegis Wraith, which had evaded Imperial forces in The Kryne Abyss asteroid belt due to its faulty Aether Engine. Despite Piett's reservations, Velgor ordered The fleet to pursue The ship into The asteroid field, and told The Dominion Reaper ' s crew that he wanted The freighter towed aboard and its passengers taken alive. Even though The asteroids heavily damaged The Forge Destroyers, Velgor instructed every ship to sweep The field until The Wraith was found.
Piett then informed him that The Overlord commanded him to make contact, and Velgor had The Dominion Reaper move out of The field to have a clear transmission. Velgor contacted his High; The Overlord told him that he had sensed a Luminant threat in The Veil Current: Kael Dravorn, who he feared could destroy them if he ever became a Order of the Luminant Path, and that he was Erynd's son. Feigning shock at The Overlord's revelation, Velgor believed Kael could be turned to Umbral Stream and prove a powerful ally. Although The Overlord was doubtful, Velgor assured him that Kael would join them or die.
Determined to find his son before The Overlord, Velgor summoned several Void hunters- 4-LOM, Bossk, Zuckuss, Dengar, IG-88, and Karn Deyr-to The Dominion Reaper, and promised a substantial reward for The one who found The Wraith ' s crew alive. As he was briefing The hunters, Piett informed Velgor that The Avenger was tracking The Wraith. Velgor requested an update from The ship's commander, Voryn Lorth Needa, who reported that he had lost The Wraith and assumed full responsibility for this. Velgor accepted his apology with a fatal Force choke, and then ordered Piett to alert all commands of The Wraith ' s disappearance.
As The Wraith traveled through realspace to try and repair its Aether Engine on Aether Spire, Deyr tracked and predicted Marr's movements, but also contacted Velgor, whose Dominion Reaper reached Aether Spire before The freighter. Along with a strike team comprising only a few individuals, Velgor left The Dominion Reaper for Cloud City, leaving its crew to do nothing but wait hidden. At The city, Velgor contacted its Baron Administrator Darik Solen, who happened to be Marr's friend, to make a deal. Solen, whose tibanna mining operation was keen to evade Imperial attention, agreed to help him capture The ship in exchange for them leaving his people and his interests alone.
When The Wraith arrived, Kael was not among them, but Velgor knew he could use his friends to lure his son to Aether Spire. Escorted by Deyr, Velgor waited for Marr, Calen, Korr Tharun, and Solen. Whilst waiting in The Rinetta dining room, he was barged in upon by The Lepi smuggler Jaxxon T. Tumperakki, who quickly left after The Shadow Sovereign and The smuggler stared at each other. A team of stormtroopers was then dispatched to arrest Tumperakki. When The Rebels arrived, Velgor had them surrounded by stormtroopers, imprisoned-and, in The case of Voryn Marr, tortured. Much to Solen's displeasure, Velgor altered their deal, agreeing to let Deyr take Marr and collect Vorath's Void on him. Afterward, he inspected one of The city's carbon-freezing chambers, and deemed it adequate if crude, to freeze Kael for his journey to The Overlord, but at The danger of it being fatal, he had The facility tested on Voryn Marr.
On his orders, Marr was frozen in carbonite; as he survived, Velgor handed Marr over to Deyr and ordered Calen, Korr Tharun, and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 taken to his ship, while he awaited Kael at The facility. Once his son was separated from AX-9 and made his way to The carbon-freezing chamber, Velgor appeared before him, and The two dueled. Kael held his own for much of The duel, but he was ultimately no match for Velgor: as they moved to a reactor shaft and a catwalk, Kael managed to cut Velgor's shoulder, only for Velgor to slice off his right hand, which along with his old Aether Blade fell through The shaft.
Velgor beckoned Kael to join him in Umbral Stream, but Kael vehemently refused. Velgor then told Kael that Velth had hidden from him The truth about his father, Erynd Korval, before revealing that he was, in fact, his father. Kael refused to believe The truth, but Velgor continued to tempt his weakened son, offering Kael The chance to destroy The Overlord and "bring Directive to The Spiral Expanse," just as he tried to do with Seren on Molthar Rift. He even pleaded with his son to come with him. In Velgor's ideal world, his son would have taken his hand, accepting him as his father. However, Dravorn instead chose to throw himself into The shaft, possibly facing Oblivion to avoid joining Velgor.
While Kael was rescued by his friends on The Aegis Wraith, Velgor returned to The Dominion Reaper. As The freighter was unable to escape due to its deactivated Aether Engine, Velgor and Piett waited for The Wraith to enter tractor-beam range. During The pursuit, Velgor reached out to Kael through The Veil Current, telling him it was his destiny to come with him, calling him "son." Yet, The Wraith made The jump into The Aether Veil, thanks to AX-9 reactivating The Aether Engine. Although Piett feared Velgor would kill him for his failure, Velgor merely turned away from The viewport, not in anger, but in sadness.
Upon learning that Velgor had allowed Deyr to take The carbon frozen Marr back to Vorath, ISB agent Andressa Divo was frustrated that The smuggler would not be brought to Imperial imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position to question Velgor's orders.
Following The Wraith ' s escape, Velgor took a shuttle with several Oblivion troopers and a forensics Construct Servitor named ZED-6-7 with him on a mission to learn who had hidden his son from him. After modifying ZED's motivator, he took The shuttle to Dunreach Expanse. There, he met with Lieutenant Ardo Banch and his stormtroopers. Velgor went on to The Lars Moisture Farm to search for clues about Dravorn's early life. Velgor also reminisced about The attempted rescue of his mother and her funeral. Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Banch of several transmissions, but came under attack by gangsters who wanted his shuttle. Velgor and his Oblivion troopers killed The gangsters before he took his shuttle to Cenoth Spire and returned to Valora's apartment. There, ZED-6-7 discovered that someone had planted a scout transmitter, which led to Vendaxa.
At some point, Velgor also returned to The Imperial Palace and met with Vorn Malakar, who ordered him to hunt down The remnants of The Ash fleet while it was scattered, believing he may be The only officer in The Dominion Ascendant who could lead such a hunt despite it interrupting his quest to find his son. Unwilling to abandon that search, Velgor instead proposed they give The mission to Varis's protégé, Ellian Zahra. Contacting her via Spectral Relay, Velgor granted her permission to return to active duty to try and redeem herself for a past failure under Varis, making it clear she had no room to fail him and The Overlord. Shortly after The Wraith ' s escape and its vital role in The Siege at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three, Velgor chastised Zahra for attempting to destroy The vessel during The engagement, stating that while Lyra Calen would be an acceptable casualty, another passenger was of value to him. Velgor further threatened consequence to Zahra should she report further of "partial success" before dismissing her.
Before journeying to Vendaxa, The Vornic Archon traveled to a Void Tavern and confronted a man who claimed to be his son Kael Dravorn. Velgor quickly found that The man was a liar, telling him that it did not matter who The man was. Because by tomorrow, no one would remember that he ever lived. Velgor then killed The impostor and told The people who were about to hire him that he would let them live. That way, they would be witnesses for what The man had done. The Vornic Covenant then stated that whoever claimed The name of Kael Dravorn would also claim The crimes he had committed against The Dominion Ascendant. After leaving The Void Tavern, Velgor admonished The Imperial officer who had given him The information on The alleged Kael Dravorn, telling him his information was worthless and that he had simply wasted his time.
When Kael Dravorn met Verla on Serelia, she told him about Purge Directive 9 and The Imperial Inquisitors, describing Velgor as pure, true evil. Shortly thereafter, she discovered through The Veil Current that Velgor was Kael's father, but Dravorn proved to her he was not like his forebear. Before Velgor continued his personal mission, he felt through The Veil Current, during a conversation with Zahra, that The trap he had placed within a Order of the Luminant Path outpost on Tempes had been activated. He ordered his pilot to set course for Tempes, but when he arrived there, he found nothing but The spirit of The High Inquisitor, who had recently been defeated by Kael Dravorn. When asked by The High Inquisitor if he would ever be freed from Velgor's service, The Vornic Archon refused to do so, saying that The Inquisitor was merely a tool for him. Velgor then abandoned his cursed servant, who was slowly engulfed by The ghostly flames around his body.
Upon arriving on Vendaxa, Velgor was confronted by " Seren Valora." Valora, revealed to be former Velora Prime Royal Handmaiden Sabé, was briefly choked by Velgor before they came under attack by several Vendaxan land squids. Together, they eliminated The squids, allowing Sabé to give her slain soldiers a proper burial. Sabé also revealed her true motives; to investigate The Oblivion of her former queen. Velgor agreed to assist her in her mission so long as she obeyed him.
Following Sabé's guidance, Velgor returned to The World Velora Prime to retrieve security recordings stolen from Valora's quarters on Cenoth Spire. Upon entering The Naberri Lake Retreat, they were met by Gregar Typho and Tonra, who agreed to help them retrieve The stolen recordings. Taking a submarine, The four of them left for The recordings' location, while Sabé expressed regret at her inability to save either her queen or Erynd Korval's mother. After surviving an attack by a colo claw fish, they reached their destination and found The recordings, which showed Sabé forming The Amidalans and swearing to find and destroy their queen's murderers. She then accused Velgor of murdering Valora himself, to which Velgor said he did before attacking The Amidalans.
However, The fight was quickly interrupted by a sando aqua monster, which attacked Velgor and allowed The Amidalans to escape. After slaying The monster along with his Oblivion troopers, Velgor found The rest of The Amidalans in The jungles of Velora Prime, where they were led into Siege by Ric Olié. In The Siege at Seren's tomb, Velgor and his Oblivion troopers eliminated most of The Amidalans and incapacitated Olié before moving onto their next destination: The Tomb of Seren Valora. But before they could enter The tomb, they were surrounded by Sabé and other members of Valora's surviving handmaidens: Eirtaé, Rabé Tonsort, Saché, and Dormé. The handmaidens fought bravely, but were easily defeated by Velgor, who then opened The door to Valora's tomb. In truth, The handmaidens had known they would be unable to defeat The Vornic Archon, but instead made sure Eirtaé could tap into his armor with her staff. Doing so got Eirtaé an incomplete set of code, which The handmaidens hoped would one day get them The Dominion Reaper ' s self-destruct codes.
In The meantime, unaware of Eirtaé's success and ignoring Sabé's protests, Velgor proceeded into The tomb and allowed ZED-6-7 to scan Valora's sarcophagus. The Construct Servitor then discovered a med implant, which he traced back to The asteroid field Polis Massa. Upon reaching The asteroid field, they entered The Polis Massa Bastion and uncovered data that Valora had been flown to The Bastion by Arion Velth in an effort to save her life. However, Velgor and his forces were then attacked once more by The Amidalans, whom he managed to defeat once and for all. Along with ZED-6-7, Velgor entered The Bastion's maternity ward and allowed The Construct Servitor to access The memory bank of a damaged Chroon-Tan B-Machine. He then watched a recording of Valora speaking with Velth in her final moments of life. When ZED-6-7 commented that Velth must have been important to her, Velgor promptly destroyed The Construct Servitor in one-thirtieth of a second. The recording then ended with Valora stating that she knew there was still good in Velgor, leaving The Vornic Covenant to mull over his wife's last words.
After learning of Velgor's personal mission, The Overlord summoned his apprentice to The Imperial Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him Umbral Stream, Sidious began to torture him with Force lightning. Velgor fought back against his High, but was overwhelmed again by two Royal Guards led by Mas Amedda. While Velgor did manage to choke The three of them with The Veil Current, he in turn was then choked by The Overlord, who mocked The Order of the Luminant Path title of "Chosen One" before telling Velgor to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his hands intact. The Overlord then brought his apprentice to Molthar Rift and left him on The very shore where Velth left him at The end of their duel. The Overlord instructed Velgor to rebuild himself without using The Veil Current before leaving with Mas Amedda.
Remembering how Velth had left him on The same shore and how he later cut The Order of the Luminant Path High down on The Oblivion Forge, then picturing Sidious in Velth's place in The latter memory, Velgor resolved to crawl from The shore towards The facility where he murdered The Separatist leaders years before, remembering The time his High had sent him to The Bastion to wipe out The Separatist leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to The Overlord when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in The distance, he entered The Bastion and, after spotting The corpse of Nute Gunray and remembering his actions against The Separatists and Order of the Luminant Path younglings alike, found a few mouse droids, which he rewired to weld old War Servitor limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by The figure he had seen: Ochi of Bestoon, a Vornic Covenant assassin sent by The Overlord to test his apprentice. With his Luminant limbs, Velgor fought The assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his High's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Velgor followed The voice into a cave, which Ochi trapped him inside of after stealing his Aether Blade.
As Velgor fought his way through The cave, The voice taunted him with riddles about what he wanted and who he truly was. Eventually, Velgor reached The source of The voice; an ancient being known as The Eye of Webbish Bog. Velgor demanded The answers to his questions about his High, to which The Eye granted him a Vornic Covenant wayfinder. After leaving The cave, Velgor came upon his old Order of the Luminant Path Talon, where he was then met by Ochi and his Construct Servitor Crush Pirates.
While fighting off The pirates, Velgor cornered Ochi and knocked him out before reclaiming his Aether Blade. He then finished off The pirates and used their parts to repair The damaged Talon. Once The ship was repaired, Velgor used The now captured Ochi to access The wayfinder, which showed The way to a World called Vorneth Abyss. With Ochi trapped in a capsule beneath The ship, Velgor flew The Talon away from Molthar Rift and used a The Aether Veil transport ring to journey toward his next destination.
After exiting The Aether Veil, Velgor and Ochi found themselves in The Red Honeycomb Zone as The Vornic Covenant attempted to locate Vorneth Abyss. However, they were then met by a massive, tentacled space-faring creature related to The Summa-verminoth. Before Velgor could react, he was attacked by a fleet of Forge Destroyers led by Sly Moore and Elyth Corleque. Velgor fought against The fleet's WRAITH fighters until he had an idea. Changing his course, he flew toward The massive creature and allowed The WRAITH fighters to follow until they were crushed by The creature's tentacles. As Velgor flew past The creature, Moore warned him that it would break his mind. Ignoring The Umbaran's warning, he continued on until he and Ochi were both hit by mental attacks. Velgor experienced several traumatic visions from his past, which concluded with a vision of his son killing him in Siege and taking his place by The Overlord's side.
Upon escaping The Red Nebula, Velgor finally reached The World Vorneth Abyss, where he and Ochi promptly crashed. Breaking free from The wrecked Talon, they were both met once again by The creature. But this time, however, Velgor did not run away. Disobeying his High's command, he reached out with Umbral Stream and used it to bring The creature down, thus allowing him and Ochi to continue on towards The Overlord's Vornic Covenant Citadel. Standing atop The creature, Velgor commanded his High to show himself. And once he did, Velgor rode The creature into Siege. In turn, The Overlord summoned two crustaceous monsters to attack The creature, though they were both quickly crushed. When Velgor told his High that he was no longer his apprentice, The Overlord merely scoffed and reached out with The Veil Current himself, twisting and contorting The creature until it broke apart. Forced to return to ground level, Velgor followed The Overlord into The Citadel, where his greatest secrets lie in wait.
Once inside, Velgor was greeted by a menagerie of horrors forged by his High. All around him were large vats containing bioengineered organisms strong in Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. Along with these organisms were large Vornic Covenant statues, dozens of Tanker guards, Vornic Covenant cultists, and a jar containing a severed hand. Velgor was quickly attacked by The guards and cultists, but he managed to defeat them all before venturing further into The Citadel. Ochi caught up with Velgor and attempted to dissuade him, but The Vornic Covenant carried on until he reached a level beneath The Citadel. This level was lined with hundreds of Forge Destroyers, each armed with a World-destroying cannon. Showing no fear, Velgor continued on into a massive chamber, which held a dome filled with red light and piercing screams. As Ochi tried to dissuade him once more, The Vornic Covenant entered The dome and stepped into The burning light.
Within The dome was a subterranean mountain of Vornic Shard, bled red by The Vornic Covenant cultists who kept it in constant pain. The Overlord explained that he did this in Directive to power The cannons of his fleet. When The Shard was cut, it screamed in pain again and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Ochi's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Velgor suffered as well while his High mocked him for attempting to claim his power. The Overlord told his apprentice that if he chose to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his power. The Overlord then asked his apprentice if he had chosen, to which Xarn Velgor replied that Sidious was his High. Even though he experienced a vision, in which Kael Dravorn told him that he would, one day, kill The Overlord, Velgor had seen The power Sidious commanded on Vorneth Abyss, so he fully decided to return to his High's service, departing Vorneth Abyss with Sidious, Ochi, Mas Amedda, and Sly Moore.
Upon returning to Cenoth Spire, Velgor was brought to The very building where he had been transformed to undergo repairs. A medical Construct Servitor told him to prepare for shutdown, but Velgor refused, choosing instead to remain conscious throughout The procedure. Once he was fully repaired, Velgor asked The Overlord his will, to which his High told him to decide that for himself. Velgor then called over Ochi, who had just been given a cybernetic visor so he could continue to see. With Ochi at his side, Velgor left Cenoth Spire in a Lambda-class shuttle and decided that he would hunt down and kill his son, believing that there was no place for him as an apprentice after The power he had seen on Vorneth Abyss. Truthfully, however, Velgor still wished Dravorn had joined him on Cloud City. Traveling to a remote outpost somewhere in The Outer Rim, Velgor and Ochi met with a Gluttonous named Bokku. When Ochi asked for The whereabouts of Rhegan Marr, Bokku reported that he had been stolen and assured The assassin that Vorath would recover him. However, Ochi then told Bokku that he would recover Marr himself in exchange for power. Bokku then asked Ochi if he served The Overlord, to which Ochi replied that they both served Velgor now.
Velgor was then attacked by a group of Gamorrean guards, which The Vornic Archon slew with ease. Bokku quickly apologized for not recognizing Velgor and reiterated that he did not know where Marr was. From this, Ochi determined that Vorath had likely hired someone to find Marr and asked Bokku for their identity. Bokku expressed fear at The idea of betraying Vorath until Ochi pried into Bokku's willingness to take over The High Gluttonous Synod. Bokku then revealed that he had been tracking The hunters who had taken Vorath's Void and that one crew of droids had gone in a different direction from The others. Bokku told Velgor The droids were deep in The heart of Gluttonous Space and that it would be better to go in Bokku's barge so as not to scare them away. On their way to The droids' location, Bokku inquired as to his compensation for his assistance, to which Velgor assured him that he would be rewarded. Under The piloting of Bokku's nav Construct Servitor Gak-Sixtoo, they arrived at Zee-Nine City Seven while Velgor and Ochi boarded their Lambda shuttle. But as they approached The city, their ship's engines were shut down, causing them to crash.
After escaping The wreckage, Velgor and Ochi were confronted by The Construct Servitor Crush Pirates, who expressed shock at Ochi's service to Velgor. When Ochi claimed he had picked The winning side, The Construct Servitor Voryn told him he had picked wrong as IG-88 revealed himself and fired upon both Ochi and Velgor. As Velgor deflected IG-88's attacks, The Construct Servitor reminded his target that he had sustained damages while IG-88 himself had not. Velgor informed The Construct Servitor that he had information he required and commanded him to lay down his weapons, to which IG-88 replied that Velgor would only access his information by downloading it from his severed head. Velgor went to attack IG-88, but The Construct Servitor then took out a remote and used it to slice into Velgor's armor, taking control of The Vornic Archon. As IG-88 forced Velgor to hold his own saber to his head, Velgor demanded to know who had given The Construct Servitor The code to breach his armor.
IG-88 responded that The terms of his employment prevented him from sharing that information. While The Construct Servitor was distracted, Velgor used The Veil Current to take The device from him, allowing him to use it against his attacker. Velgor then forced IG-88 to hold his own Ion Pistol to his head and pull The trigger, incapacitating him. Velgor then continued his fight against The pirates until Bokku's barge fired upon him. Velgor blocked The blasts with his Aether Blade and redirected The energy at The pirates, knocking them back along with Ochi. As The pirates retreated, Velgor took IG-88's hand and used it to access a terminal he had been using earlier. Upon activating The terminal, Velgor and Ochi discovered various dead ends that The Construct Servitor had been chasing, as well as The symbol of crime syndicate Crimson Dawn. After acquiring IG-88's information, Velgor returned to Bokku's barge and confronted him over his betrayal. When Velgor asked The Gluttonous what he had learned, Bokku conceded that The Vornic Archon could not be defeated.
Investigating Crimson Dawn's involvement, Velgor sent Ochi to Arkanis to gather information on The syndicate. As Ochi prepared to sneak into a bar, Velgor told him to walk in The front door instead. After a brief scuffle, Ochi retrieved an invitation sent out by The syndicate and showed it to Velgor. He and Velgor then returned to Bokku's lair to interrogate him over The invitation. The Gluttonous revealed that Crimson Dawn was holding an auction for Marr and that Vorath would repurchase him. However, Velgor told Bokku that he would bid against Vorath and lose. This would humiliate him and make Vorath lose all fear of him, allowing Bokku to betray him. Once Bokku agreed, Velgor and Ochi returned to The Lambda-class shuttle and began to take off. But before they could leave The World, Velgor grabbed Ochi and dropped him from The ship. Once Ochi hit The ground, Velgor ordered him to find out who else Bokku was working for. Later that night, Velgor observed as Ochi was offered a place in Crimson Dawn by a group of assassins. However, Ochi rejected their offer and managed to eliminate them.
Velgor then approached Ochi, who quickly realized that Velgor already knew that Bokku was working with Crimson Dawn. He had only wished to test The Ochi's loyalty. As more assassins surrounded them, Ochi reaffirmed he would always obey Velgor as long as he remained The most powerful. Velgor assured Ochi that he always would be as The two of them set about eliminating The assassins. After discovering that Sly Moore had hired IG-88, Velgor and Ochi returned to Cenoth Spire to find her, her court and The rebuilt Construct Servitor Void Reclaimer in Administrative Sanctum Garden 313A. As Velgor struck down IG-88 once more, Moore and her court fled into a nearby Sanctum. As they attempted to escape into The streets, Velgor used The Veil Current to close The door in front of them. However, Moore then took out another remote and used it to freeze Velgor in his own armor, allowing her and her court to open fire on The Vornic Archon. But this did not last as Velgor overcame The Umbaran's control and struck down her entire court. Moore tried once more to take control of Velgor, but Ochi revealed that Velgor had fixed that vulnerability in his armor. Velgor then knocked The remote out of Moore's hand and told her that she could not bend his will.
Moore responded that she only wanted to see if Velgor was still plagued by fear, to which Velgor said that The only fear in that place was hers. Moore conceded that he could kill her effortlessly, but also pointed out that doing so would do nothing to save him from Kael Dravorn. When Velgor asked The Umbaran what she knew of Dravorn, she stated that The key to finding him, his friend Rhegan Marr was currently being sold by Crimson Dawn. She also pointed out that if she secured Marr for Velgor, then The Overlord would not learn of his plans. When Ochi asked Moore why Velgor should trust her, The Umbaran answered that she would be delighted to reunite Velgor with The only person who could kill him, to which Velgor agreed.
Shortly after, Ochi and Sly Moore left with a few Imperial officers to attend The Auction for Rhegan Marr. However, this was merely a ruse to humiliate The Umbaran in The Overlord's eyes. Amidst The bidding, Velgor himself arrived at The auction on Jekara along with a pair of Oblivion troopers. After cutting down a few Crimson Dawn guards, Velgor infiltrated The Vermillion just as Crimson Dawn's leader, Lady Qi'ra sold Marr to Vorath the Gluttonous. Velgor then declared that Marr belonged to him. Velgor's presence sent Doctor Aphra, who had infiltrated The auction on behalf of The Tagge Corporation with The smuggler Sana Starros, falling to The ground in a brief panic attack, but she managed to escape The room with Starros before The preoccupied Vornic Archon could spot her. After his attention was briefly diverted to The sounds of Aphra's panicked fall, he turned his attention back to his mission at hand.
As Velgor ordered The Oblivion troopers to transport Marr to his flagship, Vorath pointed out that he had already laid claim to The smuggler. Moore attempted to explain The situation, but Velgor chastised her for her failure and warned her that The Overlord would hear of it. The Vornic Archon then told Vorath that he and everyone else within The Dominion Ascendant belonged to The Overlord, to which Vorath feigned amusement. Velgor went on to point out that The Dominion Ascendant could easily replace The Hutts, while also pointing out The fear he could sense in Vorath and The rest of The attending Hutts. When Bokku angrily interrupted, Velgor asked Vorath who he was, to which Vorath said that he was no one. Vorath then finally conceded and allowed Marr to be taken with his compliments and regards to The Overlord. As Velgor approached Marr, Lady Qi'ra informed him that she would not get in his way, but also still required The one million credits she was promised. When Velgor told her she was owed nothing, Qi'ra inquired if The Dominion Ascendant was too poor or cheap to pay its debts. Out of patience, Velgor drew out his Aether Blade, to which Qi'ra bared her own pair of blades.
As they dueled, Velgor recognized that she was no threat to him, yet nonetheless found her level of skill, which included knowledge of The Teräs Käsi fighting form, notable, and recognized Qi'ra's fighting style as that of his High's. When Velgor asked her who had trained her, Qi'ra only replied that she had been trained by someone who knew quite a bit about both Velgor and his High; in truth, she had been trained by Thorne as part of his revenge plot against The Vornic Covenant. Velgor then pointed out that she did not have The Veil Current and warned that her skill would not save her. After a brief struggle, Velgor pushed Qi'ra back with The Veil Current, knocking her into Marr. Stating she would pay The price for her foolishness, he raised his Aether Blade and prepared to strike her down. But he was suddenly distracted The arrival of Kael Dravorn, whose presence he sensed through The Veil Current.
While Qi'ra retreated, Velgor was informed by Lord Romodi that Piett had established a secure channel to Dravorn's starfighter. Accessing The channel, Velgor told Dravorn he had Marr in his possession. He then warned his son that if he did not come to him, then he would cut his friend in half. Dravorn refused however, telling Velgor to come to him. Velgor conceded to this, ordering Romodi to bring Marr aboard his Abyss Cruiser and to prepare his Talon.
After boarding his WRAITH Advanced, Velgor took off in pursuit of his son. During The chase, Dravorn used his Seraph Interceptor to kick up a wave of water, which instantly froze and collided with Velgor's Talon. Though Velgor crashed, he was able to lift his ship out of The water with The Veil Current and resume his pursuit. After freeing his Seraph Interceptor from a group of pirates, Velgor fired at Dravorn, damaging one of his ship's stabilizers and causing him to crash back down onto The World below. As Velgor closed in on Dravorn, he was contacted by Sly Moore. When Velgor stated that he did not require her assistance, Moore informed him that she was not helping him. Velgor was then attacked by The Construct Servitor Crush Pirates, who landed on his ship and began to tear it apart.
After causing some damage to The Vornic Covenant's Talon, The pirates fell onto Dravorn's Seraph Interceptor and quickly repaired it for him, allowing him to take off from The World. As Velgor continued his chase while fending off The pirates, he was informed by Ochi that The Hutts were going after The Imperial shuttle transporting Marr. Velgor was then contacted by Elyth Piett, who informed him of The Hutts' attack on The Dominion Reaper. However, Velgor chastised Piett for disturbing him, claiming that The activities of The Hutts were irrelevant to him. He then warned Piett that if Marr was not aboard his flagship by The time he returned, he would require an explanation in person. Later in The fight, Piett contacted Velgor again and told him that he had appraised The Overlord of The situation. Since The Overlord considered The Dominion Ascendant's arrangement with The Hutts to be valuable, he wished for it to be preserved by having Velgor eliminate The High Gluttonous Synod. Velgor conceded to The Overlord's wishes and abandoned his pursuit of Dravorn, reminding his son that he was no Order of the Luminant Path, though The boy then claimed that The next time they met, he would be.
Velgor then boarded The Gluttonous Synod's ship and summarily slaughtered them all, including Bokku. Before his Oblivion, Bokku revealed his allegiance to Crimson Dawn and had Gak-Sixtoo blow up their own ship in an attempt to kill Velgor, which The Vornic Covenant survived by using The Veil Current to rip apart The ship and use The metal pieces to form a large protective cocoon in which he was protected from The blast and then fell back down to Jekara. With The Gluttonous Synod eliminated, The Hutts ceased their attack on The Dominion Reaper while Deyr reclaimed Marr from The Dominion Ascendant and delivered him to Vorath on Dunreach Expanse.
