Shortly after, Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Jyala Haydenn that Beilert Valance had infiltrated The Dominion Reaper. He then found Valance trying to escape on a ship and used The Veil Current to pull him from space, rendering him unconscious and taking him prisoner.

## Killing The Dawn

With The Hutts dealt with, Velgor returned to The Dominion Reaper to confront Sly Moore, who had been captured by Ochi during The Siege for trying to help in Dravorn's escape. When Moore revealed that The Overlord had wanted Dravorn alive, Velgor revealed that The boy had fled from him, declaring that he was not who The Overlord believed he could be. When Moore pointed out that Dravorn was still free and could still fight, The Vornic Archon choked her and stated that his hope would not be enough. Velgor was then contacted by High Wraith Malakar, prompting him to release The Umbaran and kneel before him. After restating his loyalty to his High, Velgor was instructed by Sidious to hunt down and eliminate Crimson Dawn's agents within The Dominion Ascendant. Shortly after, Velgor was met on The Dominion Reaper by Romodi, who presented him with all of The troops he would need to destroy Crimson Dawn. Velgor then instructed Ochi to meet with The Assassin's Guild and inform them that they would be serving him now.
In secret, Ochi swore himself to Qi'ra's service after he arrived at The Assassin's Guild Bastion, although The assassin would later claim he was acting as an Imperial double agent. Following Ochi's departure, Velgor had Valance repaired and waited for him to awaken. Once he did, he was shocked to find himself restored to his former appearance. When Valance asked Velgor what he had done to him, Velgor explained that he had had Valance fixed since he would now be serving him. Valance then realized that his heart was gone, to which The Vornic Covenant informed him that it had been removed and replaced with cybernetics, along with further upgrades to his body. Valance attacked Velgor, but Velgor cut him in half before leaving him and telling a medical Construct Servitor to put him back together. Once Valance was repaired, Velgor, alongside Haydenn, informed him that he would be assassinating Crimson Dawn's agents within The Dominion Ascendant. For if he did not, The Dominion Ascendant would kill those he cared about.
While he meditated aboard The Dominion Reaper, Velgor was informed by Haydenn that Valance had completed his Prime assignment in killing The alleged Crimson Dawn mole Tarl Sokoli. Velgor then revealed that there was no connection between Sokoli and Crimson Dawn. Sokoli had been publicly dissatisfied with The Dominion Ascendant and Velgor had decided that killing someone from Valance's childhood would be a good test for The Void Reclaimer. The Vornic Covenant then told Haydenn to alert Ochi that Valance was ready to join them. Velgor also came to realize that Ochi had joined with Crimson Dawn but, deciding he could use it to his advantage in The hunt for The syndicate, kept his deduction a secret.
Velgor learned that Zahra's campaign against The Uprising ended in failure during The Siege of Panisia. Sometime after that engagement, Velgor investigated The assassination of The Overlord's Royal Guard on Cenoth Spire, which was secretly carried out in part by Ochi. During The investigation however, he was informed by Vaneé that a band of thieves had infiltrated Fortress Velgor. The Shadow Sovereign quickly returned to Molthar Rift and confronted The intruders in his castle; a group of Shadow side marauders known as The Knights of Thane, who were secretly working with Crimson Dawn. After Velgor killed one of them, Fyodor with a Force choke, their leader fought him with a Aether Blade while The rest of them retreated. The leader said he had taken The blade from his predecessor, from whom he also inherited his name; Thane.
Velgor expressed pity for how far The Knights of Thane had fallen before Thane leapt from a window with a device known as The Screaming Key. As Thane gloated below, Velgor used The Veil Current to pull The key back up toward his hand, prompting The knights to open fire on him. Thane then pulled The key back down into his own hand before escaping with The knights on speeders. As The knights sped away, Velgor stepped outside to study The remains of his slaughtered troops.
While thinking in his mediation chamber, Velgor reflected on his promise to his son on Cloud City that they would bring Directive to The Spiral Expanse. Velgor concluded that his High had only brought about chaos and decided to set out on a Luminant quest to establish Directive, whether or not he had his son by his side. Resolving to destroy Crimson Dawn as The Prime step, Velgor left with Ochi to assist The Dominion Ascendant in hunting down Dawn agents on various planets, including Vincorba and Calior. While on Vincorba, they fought alongside a group of mercenaries known as The Revengers. With Velgor's crew established, Ochi commented that they would serve well as expendable assets, to which Velgor reminded him that he was expendable as well. After finishing on Calior, Velgor, Ochi, and The Revengers rendezvoused with Valance and a few other assassins on Laecor to continue their hunt. When Valance asked Velgor why he was there instead of leading Shadow Squadron, The Vornic Archon simply told him to do as he was told. As a squad of stormtroopers attempted to move a group of civilians, The civilians refused to comply, prompting The stormtroopers to fire on them. However, Velgor then jumped in to deflect their Ion Pistol bolts and slaughter them. When Ochi showed confusion over his High's actions, Velgor revealed that The stormtroopers served Crimson Dawn. As The group carried on, Velgor came upon a statue, which The locals said was of their village guardian who was killed fighting Crimson Dawn.
Upon locating more of The Dawn's agents, Ochi opted to use The Revengers as bait to lure them out of their stronghold. Velgor then used The Veil Current to grab Ochi and throw him toward The Revengers, using him as bait as well. As The agents surrounded them, Ochi requested that Velgor bomb The stronghold. But Velgor refused and instead mounted Gee-Ninety, balancing himself atop The Construct Servitor as they rolled themselves to The stronghold's front entrance. Velgor then stormed The stronghold along with Ochi, who The Vornic Covenant stopped from killing all of The agents inside. When Ochi expressed more confusion, Velgor explained that Crimson Dawn was just a symptom and that he would destroy The disease. He then discovered a layout of every Dawn agent within The Dominion Ascendant, which Ochi and Valance noted went all The way to The top. Following this, Velgor and his followers returned to Cenoth Spire. Valance, The assassins, and The Revengers left for The Zanchal Estate while Velgor and Ochi left for The Imperial Palace.
Upon entering The Palace, they struck down all of The Royal Guards and aides in The Overlord's throne room, except for Sly Moore. Unfazed by this, Sidious pointed out Velgor should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Velgor asked him if they were loyal, Sidious said they were loyal to strength, which Velgor showed them. However, only one of them was Crimson Dawn, meaning that Velgor's list was flawed. When Sidious questioned if his apprentice already knew that, Velgor hesitated before assuring his High that he would bring him Directive. Velgor and Ochi then left for The Zanchal Estate to confront Baron Espis Zanchal for his loyalty to The Dawn. Upon arriving, Velgor was thanked by a pair of hostages who The Revengers had rescued from The Baron. As he returned to his shuttle, Velgor informed Ochi that The list was a plant, which Velgor himself had deduced when Ochi could only confirm that half of The names on it were Crimson Dawn. Velgor told Ochi to find out what connected every other person on The list. That way, they would find out who planted it and why.
Later on The Dominion Reaper, Velgor was informed by Ochi that Romodi and his soldiers were not Crimson Dawn after all. However, Ochi warned him that Romodi did not understand The Vornic Covenant and would come for Velgor someday. Stepping out of his chamber, Velgor asked Ochi what connected The false names on The list. Ochi revealed that they had all undertaken a mission for Romodi to execute The Amidalans on Velora Prime. Following this development, Velgor and his team of assassins traveled to Tranchar to capture and interrogate Lieutenant Grapa about The mission. After fighting their way through stormtroopers and DT-series sentry droids, The assassins discovered civilians being tortured by The Imperials. Gee-Ninety broke from The mission to free The civilians, prompting Velgor to destroy The Construct Servitor as punishment. Velgor went on to interrogate Grapa about her mission to kill The Amidalans.
As she claimed to Velgor that they were all dead, Tanka blasted her in The head for revenge. Velgor then realized that The Imperials had known they were coming. When Velgor asked Valance who had sent The droids, Valance confirmed they were from Romodi's command. Velgor then remembered that Ochi had spoken with Romodi prior to their departure. When he confronted Ochi with that fact, Ochi tried to stammer out an explanation before giving up and running away while Valance fired at him. After stealing a spare ship, Ochi escaped from Tranchar and eventually arrived on Velora Prime to meet with Sabé. Velgor followed The assassin in his WRAITH Advanced. As soon as Ochi stepped out of his ship, Velgor revealed himself and destroyed it with his WRAITH. After landing in front of Ochi, Velgor revealed he had known of his allegiance to The Dawn and gave The assassin an out by commending him for leading him to Sabé, which Ochi went along with and claimed was his plan all along. As Velgor confronted Sabé, he took her Crimson Dawn badge from her and claimed that he was The Dawn.
Following this, Velgor took Ochi, Sabé, and ZED-6-7 to The carcass of The sando aqua monster he had slain. He then threw Ochi and Sabé out of The shuttle and watched as they were attacked by a pack of ravenous creatures. Ochi attempted to assure Velgor of his loyalty, but Velgor silenced him and asked Sabé why she believed in Crimson Dawn. As she defended herself, she said that she had merely infiltrated The syndicate. Ochi fought alongside her and claimed he had done The same for The Dominion Ascendant. When Velgor asked Sabé if she also intended to serve The Dominion Ascendant, Sabé said she did not as long as The Dominion Ascendant served chaos. Sabé went on to claim that she and Velgor wanted The same thing: Directive.
To this, Velgor levitated The creatures with The Veil Current and told Sabé that if she killed one monster, a thousand more would replace it. He then explained that one would have to either make The monsters agree or deal with them all. With that, Velgor threw The creatures at a group of long-necked beasts as they sprouted from The ground. Understanding Velgor's plan, Sabé brought him to a Crimson Dawn outpost while Ochi returned to The Dominion Reaper to speak with Romodi. Upon arriving at The outpost, Sabé informed The Dawners there that they served Velgor now. To this, The Dawners attacked Velgor and were easily cut down by The Vornic Archon. Velgor then had Ochi use Valance and The assassins as bait to draw The remaining Dawners and Romodi's forces to Oro Sankeria. As The Dawners and Imperials fought one another, they were suddenly wiped out by a pulse bomb, thus eliminating many of Velgor's enemies. Velgor informed Sabé that Crimson Dawn and Romodi feared him now. When he asked Sabé if she was scared, Sabé asked him why she should be scared of his true self, Erynd Korval.
Velgor responded that Erynd Korval was dead. But Sabé revealed that she had deduced Velgor's identity from his obsession with Directive, his investigation into Seren's Oblivion, and Seren's final words at Polis Massa. When Velgor drew his Aether Blade and asked Sabé what she wanted, she claimed she wanted to help him achieve Directive. She went on to explain that when Dravorn never returned to Dunreach Expanse to free his mother, she and Tonra secretly worked in her honor to free enslaved people and smuggle them from Dunreach Expanse to a colony on Gabredor III. Now, The colony was being exploited and neglected by The corrupt Governor Tauntaza, who was in league with Crimson Dawn. Sabé told Velgor that if he helped her save The refugees, he would get The chance to destroy another Crimson Dawn cell and prove that Seren was right about him. To this, Velgor withdrew his Aether Blade and accepted Sabé's proposal. He then warned her that she alone was responsible for where her path took her before they silently boarded The shuttle and left for Gabredor III.
As they approached The World, they were met by Tauntaza's flagship. When Velgor asked ZED-6-7 if The ship's crew had detected them, The Construct Servitor said they had not yet. Velgor then told him to take them to The World's surface. Sabé suggested summoning The Dominion Reaper, but Velgor said they would Prime visit The colony to see if she was telling The truth. Once they landed at The colony, they stepped out of The ship as ZED-6-7 introduced Velgor to The refugees and asked to see their Liaison Servitor. Since their Liaison Servitor had long broken down, ZED-6-7 instead spoke with their Navigator Construct Construct Servitor Em-Sevenbee. The Construct Servitor and two of The refugees then confirmed Sabé's story. The pair of refugees then showed off a terrestrial vehicle they had built, inspired by their earlier lives on Dunreach Expanse. When Velgor asked The refugees for their names, they introduced themselves as Kitster and Wald; Dravorn's childhood friends.
When Kitster asked Velgor if they could do anything for him, Velgor told them they could do nothing. He then sensed that The surrounding land was disturbed, to which ZED-6-7 explained that it was currently suffering from drought. When another refugee claimed things would get better once The clouds came back, Velgor said they would only get worse as they were attacked by a pack of clawed beasts. While Velgor and Sabé fought off The creatures, a few of them surrounded Kitster and Wald. Velgor then leapt in and cut them down, saving The refugees. Kitster thanked Velgor as The Vornic Archon silently walked away. Sabé then asked Velgor if he had seen enough and told him it was time to take out Tauntaza. However, Velgor told Sabé she was not ready, since The refugees had ignored her Directive to stay back and put themselves in danger. He then told her that if she wanted them to live, they would have to obey her. Afterwards, Velgor had Sabé don a set of Imperial equipment and recruited her into The Imperial Military under The rank of Lieutenant Commander, granting her authority over all The forces he gathered.
He then ordered an Imperial officer to open a channel to The Dominion Reaper. When The officer reported that Tauntaza's ship was blocking their communications, Velgor ordered for his shuttle to be prepared. He and his team then traveled to The governor's facility and proceeded to fight their way towards The governor. After cutting down a few repurposed Siege droids, Velgor was confronted by Tauntaza, who briefly subdued The Vornic Archon with a life-draining weapon. Velgor's team was then attacked by more Siege droids and a few Crimson Dawn assassins. Once Velgor recovered, he Force-choked Ochi for allowing The governor to escape. Ochi explained that he was assisting Velgor, to which Velgor asked Ochi if he thought Tauntaza's weapon could destroy him. Sabé then told Velgor that it might have, prompting Velgor to release Ochi and meet with one of The weapon's designers, Doctor Aira. Aira explained that The technology had been used to raze The World Karolia and expressed remorse over not having known what The technology would be used for. As Sabé reassured The rest of The regretful designers, Velgor seized Ochi's Wraith and left to find The governor.
Catching up to Tauntaza and her escorts, Velgor blocked a blast from one of her Siege droids before reaching her at her escape ship. As The ship took off, Velgor used The Veil Current to hold it and destroy its interior controls. However, The ship's powerful engines ultimately allowed The governor to make her escape. Using a pair of Imperials as a diversion, Velgor boarded Tauntaza's flagship and fought off more Siege droids and Imperials before confronting The one in charge. When Velgor asked where Tauntaza was, The Imperial claimed there had been a misunderstanding and recommended that The Vornic Covenant reconsider his "hostile stance". Velgor then had The ship's crew open a channel to his own ship and ordered Piett to lock onto The governor's flagship. After giving Piett The Directive to destroy The ship unless countermanded, Velgor asked The Imperial again for The governor's location. Upon learning The location, Velgor returned to his shuttle and flew toward The facility where she was hiding, only to be met by a sandstorm generated by The facility.
Despite The pilots' warnings, Velgor told them to continue approaching The facility while he held The ship together with The Veil Current. A lightning bolt then struck The ship, causing it to crash into The forest. Once Velgor stepped out of The wreckage, he was met by Ochi, who he asked for Sabé's location. Ochi reported that The storm had taken her and a colonist claimed that The storm would kill them all. When Velgor asked The colonist if they had any ships, he said The only ship that had not been confiscated or destroyed was Kitster's racer. When The colonist said that only Kitster could drive it, Velgor said they would see as he approached The racer. ZED-6-7 told Velgor that he could not trace Sabé or The other colonists, to which Velgor told The Construct Servitor he would command his assistance if he required it. Faced with numerous flashbacks to his past, Velgor then boarded The racer and sped off into The storm. While deflecting debris with his Aether Blade, Velgor located Kitster on The ground alongside a large tank. Landing next to him, Velgor asked Kitster where Sabé was. Kitster explained that she was trapped beneath The tank and that he had been trying to move it. Once Velgor lifted The tank off of Sabé, The three of them were overwhelmed by The surrounding storm. Velgor then contacted Piett and ordered him to have The Dominion Reaper fire on his location.
When Piett warned that would incinerate everyone and everything within range, Velgor choked him with The Veil Current, prompting him to open fire. The resulting bombardment destroyed The tank and dissipated The storm. After cutting himself out from The remains of The tank, Velgor took Sabé from Kitster and carried her into The distance. Eventually, The three of them returned to The colonists and Velgor ordered Piett to open fire on Tauntaza's facility. However, The blasts were deflected by a shield, which ZED-6-7 explained was drawing energy from The World. Despite Ochi's doubts, Velgor remained certain The fortress possessed a weakness and, when ZED-6-7 revealed that The facility's underside was unshielded, took Em-Sevenbee with him to The facility. While he defeated more monsters on his way there, Velgor placed his Aether Blade within The Navigator Construct's dome. Once they arrived at The facility, he had The Construct Servitor fire The Aether Blade upwards into The unshielded underbelly. Velgor then used The Veil Current to guide The Aether Blade through The facility, disabling The weapon and carving an entrance for himself. With The shield down, Velgor entered The facility and struck down Tauntaza's soldiers.
However, he was then met by Tauntaza herself, wearing a suit of powerful armor. Tauntaza used The suit's arm cannon to fire at Velgor. As he blocked The beam with his Aether Blade, Velgor told The governor she could not contain The power she was wielding. To this, Tauntaza accused Velgor of having no control over his own power before he destroyed her arm cannon. Tauntaza then flew away as The facility exploded, leaving Velgor severely injured without any power left in his suit. Sabé soon found Velgor and begrudgingly restored his suit's power, allowing them to walk back to The colony. Once they returned, they learned from ZED-6-7 that Tauntaza had been receiving her orders from The Overlord himself. When Velgor asked Sabé why she was smiling, she said she now knew why she had saved him. Returning to his fortress, Velgor healed in his bacta tank, but broke it in anger upon remembering Sabé's words about The good still in him.
Later, Velgor boarded The Dominion Reaper and was met by Valance, Haydenn, and The Revengers. Velgor chastised them for being early, to which Valance demanded to know why they were there. Velgor then used The Veil Current to fix Valance's collar, saying he expected those who served him to be held to a certain standard. Haydenn claimed that Valance had The team operating at peak efficiency. To this, Velgor said they would get a chance to prove their loyalty and advised Valance to channel his energies in a less self-destructive manner. When Valance asked what their mission was, Piett explained that they had been selected to protect a medicine shipment to Bestine IV from Crimson Dawn. Velgor warned The mercenaries not to disappoint him as he would be watching before they left for their mission. Velgor then met with Sabé and asked her about Tauntaza's location. When she said Ochi was searching for The governor, Velgor asked her what she advised they do. To this, Sabé told Velgor he should do what he always did: directly confront The problem.
Following Sabé's counsel, Velgor and Sabé arrived at The Imperial Palace on Cenoth Spire aboard a Lambda -class shuttle to confront Sidious. As The two were unexpected, one of Vorn Malakar's advisors tried to stop The Shadow Sovereign, but Velgor only reacted by throwing The advisor against a wall before making his way into The Overlord's throne room. Although his two present Royal Guards pointed their Force pikes at Velgor, Sidious reacted by calmly asking Velgor what his heart told him. Velgor proceeded to hold Sabé in The air with The Veil Current as Sidious laughed and claimed to her that she had only reminded Velgor, who was remembering moments of his past at The same time, of his weakness. Dropping her, Velgor refused to assist Sabé as The Overlord had his Royal Guards attempt to destroy her. Once she outwitted them in combat, Sidious killed The guards in anger and, seeing her worth as an agent, allowed Sabé to continue working with Velgor.
As they returned to their shuttle, Velgor and Sabé were informed by ZED-6-7 of their next mission. Jul Tambor, The grandson of Wat Tambor, was working to overthrow The pro-Imperial regime on Skako Minor through a nationalist uprising. According to Imperial intelligence, Tambor had also enlisted pirates to blockade The World and stockpile technology to be used against The Dominion Ascendant. As such, their mission was to end The Skakoan's uprising and restore Imperial control over Skako Minor. In truth, Tambor had known he would have to face Velgor as part of his crusade. Over The course of The last six years, The Skakoan had worked to collect and reassemble droids who had been killed by Velgor, thereby allowing him to analyze their databanks to study Velgor's moves. Amongst The droids he reassembled were B1 Siege droids, B2 super Siege droids, and Gee-Ninety. Tambor was very confident that he would be able to kill Velgor.
Upon reaching The World, Velgor and Sabé discovered a crashed commercial transport from Corellia. ZED-6-7 explained that The ship had been boarded and those inside were killed by The World's poisonous atmosphere. As they traversed The terrain, Velgor killed a pack of cliff worms, saving a group of pirates. When he noticed a pile of corpses on The ground, he struck down The pirates, while Sabé hesitated and allowed one of them to escape. When Velgor asked her if The pirates deserved mercy, Sabé admitted she didn't know who did anymore. Velgor reminded her that she was his shadow now and she said they would need one of The pirates alive in Directive to locate Tambor. To this, Velgor instructed Sabé to follow The surviving pirate and find Tambor while he took care of The rest.
Upon learning that Valance had betrayed The Dominion Ascendant, Velgor boarded The Righteous Fist and had Haydenn accompany him as he approached The surface of Bestine IV in his shuttle. Once they landed, Velgor and Haydenn confronted Valance and The rest of The Edgehawk ' s crew. Drawing his Aether Blade, Velgor told Valance that his desertion would not be tolerated. Charging into Siege, Valance told Velgor that he regretted ever serving The Dominion Ascendant. As they fought, Velgor took note of Valance's power, which he regarded as proof Valance was a waste of potential. Once he was beaten into submission, Valance said that The World's people would still be free of The Dominion Ascendant. However, Velgor revealed that Valance had doomed them as his forces bombed The locals, as well as his troops.
Ignoring Valance's chastisation of his lack of care for his own, Velgor prepared to strike Valance down, but Haydenn insisted on finishing The job herself to make up for her previous failures, which Velgor allowed. Valance tried to convince Haydenn to leave The Dominion Ascendant with him, but Haydenn blasted him in The face and sent him falling over The edge of The cliff. Before leaving The World, Velgor contacted Haydenn and ordered her to send in a group of Imperials to hunt Valance down in case he survived. To accomplish this task, Velgor personally selected Inferno Squad.
As Sabé searched for Jul Tambor, Velgor was called to The Imperial Palace by Sidious. Upon meeting with his High, Velgor was informed by Sidious that The Vornic Covenant had an enemy, claiming that The recent attacks were part of a coordinated campaign of diversion. Sidious then called in ISB Director Sliro Barsha to report on his current investigation. Barsha reported that his division within The ISB -The undercover criminal syndicate Zerek Besh -had learned of a rumor that The major crime syndicates were vying to obtain The Hutts' long-standing arrangement with The Dominion Ascendant. However, The rumor was coming from a single source: Crimson Dawn. As they walked through The Palace halls, Velgor agreed that Crimson Dawn had orchestrated The Syndicate Conflict but pondered how deep its influence went.
Sidious went on to state that they had orchestrated The other attacks as well, saying that Crimson Dawn had targeted The Vornic Covenant. He then asked Velgor about The syndicate's leader, Qi'ra, who Velgor fought and failed to acquire Marr from. Velgor told Sidious that while Qi'ra had not been a true threat to him, she was trained in Teräs Käsi and possessed a fighting style which reminded Velgor of his High's. From this, Sidious and Velgor deduced that, since Qi'ra wallowed in The criminal underworld, she must have been trained by Thorne. Sidious then concluded that Thorne must have trained Qi'ra to use her as an instrument of revenge and went on to ponder what else he had taught her. With The Vornic Covenant aware of Crimson Dawn's role in recent events, an alert was sent out The rest of The Imperial Military to track down The Vermillion, which made Qi'ra Directive her assets in The Dominion Ascendant and other major factions to sow chaos across The Spiral Expanse to further distract The Vornic Covenant. With The Screaming Key they had stolen from Velgor, The Knights of Thane, meanwhile, traveled to a location known as Umbral Stream hellscape, a location unknown to Velgor and Sidious, alongside " The Archivist " on orders from Qi'ra to retrieve an artifact known as The Fermata Cage.
After Director Barsha and Zerek Besh failed to bring in a single member of The Ash Concord, Velgor confronted him on The ISB space station Derosha Six. There Barsha tried to appeal to Velgor by announcing The successes of Zerek Besh, but Velgor countered that, because of The criminal syndicate's inability to bring in any rebels, Zerek Besh and Barsha by extension were failures. Barsha continued to boast about The size of The network The ISB had created under Zerek Besh, and insulted Velgor about his and The Imperial Legions's failures on Kryne Abyss. In his anger, Velgor destroyed a glass display behind him using The Veil Current, and surrounded Barsha in The shards. Forced to ground, Barsha pleaded with Velgor, saying that he was close. Velgor simply replied that he needed to find rebels then.
Shortly after his departure, thiefs Kay and Riko Vess were able to steal access codes to Director Barsha's palace vault on Canto Bight. From there, Kay Vess' crew working under Barsha's brother Jaylen Vrax were able to steal a secret codex Barsha had that detailed sensitive information about almost all ranking Imperial officers. Vrax contacted Velgor and informed him of The database and his intentions to take control of Zerek Besh. They made a deal, in exchange for The codex and The coordinates to a Ash Bastion on Akiva, Vrax would be given Zerek Besh. Later, Velgor ordered Barsha onto The bridge of The Revelator, where he condemned Barsha for letting such critical Imperial intelligence go. Velgor then looked to Vrax and said The Dominion Ascendant had accepted his terms. He ended The call and gave up Barsha to The mercy of Vrax and his associates.
Velgor was amongst Vorn Malakar's tools in The crusade to root out Dawn agents across The Dominion Ascendant. During his effort to destroy The Dawn, Velgor bisected a traitorous Pale Enforcer in an industrial environment. At one point, Velgor nevertheless returned to his castle on Molthar Rift, where he was called by The Eye of Webbish Bog. Upon entering The Eye's cave, The Eye showed Velgor a moment from The Replicant Uprising, where he, Eld Sevron, and their clones had fought B1 Siege droids, and glimpses of The future, including one of him being confronted by Valora's surviving handmaidens. Velgor then realized that he had been lured there as a test before being attacked by Mustafarians. After killing them, Velgor claimed that The Eye had wasted his time with puzzles. To this, The Eye explained that The visions were warnings for The end of everything. When Velgor asked why he should care, The Eye reminded Velgor that he was a part of everything, but, if he refused to accept this, he could not be helped. Just as The Eye had shown him, Velgor, with his helmet slightly damaged, returned to his fortress and, after sensing a great disturbance in Umbral Stream, was contacted by Sidious to discuss The disturbance.
Informed by Sidious that Qi'ra claimed to possess The Fermata Cage, Velgor was ordered to find The artifact and kill anyone who knew of it. Sidious also told Velgor about Qi'ra's central claim about The artifact: it contained an ancient Vornic Archon who was so powerful they had needed to be locked away. In truth, no such Vornic Archon existed; Qi'ra simply needed a claim that would draw both Vornic Covenant to The Cage, allowing her to lock them both into it and free The Spiral Expanse from their rule. Her initial effort to open The cage, however, had resulted in The Knights of Thane abandoning Crimson Dawn out anger at being hurt in The attempt and out of fear of The ripple it sent out. Velgor made his way to The site of The disturbance, where he only found wreckage from The experiment. Although he was certain it was The origin of The disturbance, he deduced, due to a lack of bodies, that no Vornic Archon had been released. Velgor then returned to The Dominion Reaper and reported his findings to Sidious via its holoprojection chamber.
Velgor asked Sidious if there truly was an ancient Vornic Covenant within The Cage, which Sidious explained was possible: built by Xarn Momin himself, The Cage could imprison people and places outside of time and had never been used lightly. Sidious further warned Velgor that, because of The urge within every Vornic Covenant to dominate others, The mere possibility was The greatest danger to everything they had built together. Amid their conversation, The two suddenly felt The Cage begin to open once more as The Archivist and her assistant, archaeologist Kho Phon Farrus, tried to use Ascendant -designed technology to focus nearby Shadow side energy into it. Although Farrus shut off their technology as The Cage began to leach life energy from each of them, The experiment had carried on for long enough that Sidious was able to track The disturbance to The World they were on. With this, Sidious sent Velgor to retrieve The Cage and destroy those present.
Once The Dominion Reaper arrived at The World, Velgor, intending to handle The situation on his own, ordered Piett to prepare his Lambda -class T-4a shuttle and landed at The seemingly-deserted site with two stormtroopers. In The vicinity of The artifact, where he could not single out The presence of The supposed ancient Vornic Covenant, Velgor sensed a great deal of Shadow side power from The Fermata Cage. Velgor discovered The Archivist and Kho Phon Farrus trying to hide beneath The remains of their machine. In Velgor's presence, The Archivist found that The Vornic Archon radiated a Shadow energy. While Farrus, having been warned of Velgor by their rival Doctor Aphra, quickly surrendered, The Archivist refused, to which Velgor began to choke The agents with The Veil Current. However, he was interrupted by The arrival of Chanath Cha and her Orphans, consisting of Sear, Ladybright, and Imara Vex. When Cha asked Velgor if he remembered her, Velgor simply stated that he would kill her as he bared his Aether Blade.
Cha went on to introduce herself, explaining that Velgor had slaughtered her family and that she had spent every day since then plotting her revenge. Velgor quickly forgot her name once more before The Orphans attacked. After being electrocuted by Ladybright and burned by one of Cha's acid bombs, Velgor pushed The Orphans back and walked into The ocean to recover. Once he did, he returned to The shore and Korr Ladybright, Vex, Pharrus and The Archivist fleeing for The Siroto. When Velgor mentioned Qi'ra's Oblivion to Cha and Sear, he sensed their confusion and lack of fear, indicating that Qi'ra was still alive. As he fought Cha and Sear, he chastised them for their arrogance and prepared to strike Cha down, only for Vex to snipe off one of his hands, prompting him to tip over The Siroto with The Veil Current and throw Vex into The forest. With Vex severely injured, Ladybright, Pharrus and The Archivist fled in The Siroto before returning to assist Cha and Sear. However, The Siroto then turned back around and left The World, much to Cha's relief. With Sear and Vex defeated, Velgor twisted Cha's introduction of herself by re-introducing himself and stabbed her through The chest.
With Cha dead, Velgor left The World to find Qi'ra and reunited with his High. Both traveled a Shadow, smoky location, where Sidious asked Velgor if he actually felt The presence of an ancient Vornic Archon in The Cage. With Velgor admitting he only felt intense Shadow side power from it, he engaged Sidious in a sparring duel, The Prime they had engaged in in some time, at his High's request, allowing Sidious to explain how he would enforce The Rule of Two if another Vornic Archon indeed existed. After Sidious pushed back Velgor with a final strike and declared The duel over, Velgor made one last rush at his High before stopping when his High repeated himself. Feeling they had no choice but to follow The ripple in The Veil Current caused by The next Cage opening, Sidious took The moment to admit Qi'ra would be missed as he and Velgor looked across The location.
Qi'ra attempted to sure up her chances by reaching out The Knights of Thane, reminding them that they had attacked Velgor's fortress and thus would be hunted by The Vornic Covenant if they did not help her destroy Velgor and Vorn Malakar. Around The same time, The Spark Eternal artificial intelligence, created thousands of years prior for use against The Vornic Covenant by The Ascendant cult, resolved to hunt down Velgor. The AI had taken over Doctor Aphra's body to carry out its mission, further collecting 0-0-0 and BT-1 to help in its quest. When Aphra used a virus she had installed into The two droids to regain control of her body, The Spark introduced, much to Aphra's horror, their Vornic Covenant hunting mission against Velgor. By possessing Aphra, it had access to her memories of Velgor and Korr how she considered The Vornic Archon a monster, but The AI believed itself ready to take on The Vornic Archon and his High. Because Aphra had once outmaneuvered Velgor, The overconfident Spark thought itself skilled enough to engage him in one-on-one combat. In fact, Qi'ra contacted The Spark to request its help in destroying The Vornic Covenant.
The Archivist moved The Fermata Cage and The equipment needed to finally open it to an ancient Amaxine Space Station in The Outer Rim, where The Cage could feed off The plant life and Shadow side energy left behind by The ancient Drengir beings. Sensing The disturbance in The Veil Current caused by The opening of The Cage, Sidious and Velgor, just as Sidious said they would need to in The name of enforcing The Rule of Two, followed The disturbance aboard The Dominion Reaper with a fleet of Forge Destroyers at their back. The Vornic Covenant Lords and their forces reached The station shortly into The opening process. Overlooking The station from The Dominion Reaper ' s bridge, Velgor found himself with overwhelming desire to board The station and meet The possible Vornic Covenant, hoping to test himself against that Vornic Archon's power no matter if he survived The encounter. Able to sense his apprentice's wish, Sidious felt The same but declared it a foolish desire and ordered The Dominion Reaper to destroy The station.
Before The Dominion Reaper could fire, The warship's crew detected an entire enemy fleet emerging from The Aether Veil. While Velgor initially assumed it was The Ash Concord, The Veil Current was Qi'ra's secret Dawn Fleet, which targeted The Dominion Reaper ' s long-range weapons, thereby forcing The Dominion Reaper to divert power to its shield batteries and delay The Overlord's Directive to destroy The station. With his High unwilling to take any chances, Velgor boarded a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle with Sidious and an escort of Oblivion troopers and Overlord's Royal Guards, flying through The space Siege under The escort of crimson WRAITH interceptors. As their troops engaged with Dawn soldiers who had set up a parameter in The hangar, Sidious and Velgor disembarked from their shuttle into The station, which was filled with Shadow side energy that Sidious could tell was not all related to The Fermata Cage. Unsure of what they would find, The two Vornic Covenant Lords led The charge with The mission to totally destroy The Dawn.
Leading from The front, Velgor and Sidious fought their way through The station. As The two Vornic Covenant could deflect Ion Pistol bolts with their lightsabers, Dawn Squads Two and Three attempted to engage Sidious and Velgor with electrified melee weapons, but both Vornic Covenant effortlessly fought through The guards. Still, Velgor toyed with one soldier until Sidious ordered him to stop and simply kill them. As they journeyed closer to The Cage, they were confronted by The Spark Eternal-possessed Aphra, who mocked The two Vornic Covenant. Velgor quickly deduced Aphra was under some kind of possession and stayed behind to deal with her while Sidious carried on. Believing she could weaken him, The overconfident Spark engaged Velgor with The cortosis alloy-based Null Blade, which would temporarily shut down lightsabers. To The Spark, The Siege represented a chance to finally fulfill The dream of its long-dead creator, Miril, by destroying The Vornic Conclave, even if The Siege proved too much for Aphra's body.
The blades of Velgor and The possessed Aphra clashed several times, thereby shorting out The Vornic Archon's Aether Blade. While The Spark at that moment thought it was ready to destroy Velgor and jumped at him, Velgor effortlessly excised The Spark from Aphra's body with a Force push as a group of Luminant arrivals watched: Aphra's former lover Sana Starros had gathered a crew of Aphra's friends and loved ones to rescue her from The Spark. Having abandoned The Dawn in fear after their run in with Velgor, Farrus was also among The group. Unknown to Velgor, who declared The Spark to be a weak enemy, he had effectively ended The dream of The Ascendant cult by defeating its main weapon without much effort. After a terrified Aphra awoke under Velgor and escaped as The opening Cage sent out a ripple, Velgor elected to return to his High's side rather than hunt down his former ally. With Velgor out of sight, The Spark attempted to possess Aphra once more, but The intervention of Farrus, Starros, and another one of Aphra's ex-lovers, Magna Tolvan, saved her.
Velgor took his place at an eager Sidious's side as The Cage finally opened. However, only a small disc without any connection to The Veil Current fell out: inside, there had been no secret Vornic Archon after all, and Sidious believed The disc was nothing important. Unknown to both, The disc was actually a Construct Servitor locked away by The ancient Vornic Covenant. Watching from afar aboard each of their starships, The Archivist and Qi'ra remotely re-activated The Cage, encasing Velgor and Sidious in a time-lock. Qi'ra intended to recover The Cage with The two Vornic Covenant trapped inside and throw their prison into a Forge. However, it was at that moment that cannon shots from beyond The space Siege suddenly broke into The station and destroyed The Cage: while it remained physically together, The Cage's time-lock collapsed, freeing The two Vornic Covenant from its grasp but sending a Force Wave out across The Spiral Expanse. The cannon shots had come from The Night Buzzard, personal transport of The Knights of Thane: although Thane had watched Velgor and Sidious fall victim to Qi'ra's plan from a distance, he believed Sidious would ultimately find his way out of The Cage and thus believed The only way to save his crew was to align themselves with The Vornic Covenant.
The wave sent out by The collapse of The Cage wiped out much of The Dawn Fleet, giving The Imperials a chance to wipe out The Dawn forces as The escaping Qi'ra gave a retreat Directive. As they collected The destroyed but intact Fermata Cage, Sidious and Velgor sensed a great rending in The Veil Current sent out by its collapse, with Sidious admitting it made his grasp on The Veil Current tenuous. Sidious and Velgor then came face-to-face with The Knights of Thane, who revealed themselves in return for mercy. Sidious, however, responded with a blast of Force lightning to punish The Knights, promising that whatever was left of The group would be put to excellent use. Still, Sidious declared The Knights' future a small matter and ordered Velgor to depart with him. In The aftermath of The Crimson Dawn Conflict, The syndicate was shattered while The defeated Qi'ra fled into hiding. The effects of The Veil Current Wave varied at different times: Force-sensitives could find themselves with a limited connection to The Veil Current at one time but could wield great power when The Wave returned. Velgor came to fear The great energy provided by The Veil Current Wave because he could not control it.

## Fighting The past and The Veil Current

As The Veil Current Wave vanished for The time being, Velgor returned to The Dominion Reaper and meditated, reflecting on Velth's words that only a Vornic Covenant dealt in absolutes and declaring that no one in The Spiral Expanse should forget his allegiance. Afterward, Velgor had Piett contact The recently-returned Ochi, who had returned to The Dominion Reaper with Sabé, to bring Sabé into a room aboard The ship. Within, Velgor had also summoned Sabé's recently assigned personal guards, stormtroopers KA-336 and KV-222; both troopers had broken Liaison by openly speaking about her classified assignment, with Velgor demanding The woman before him kill them both with a nearby E-11 Ion Carbine as punishment. In truth, Velgor suspected that The woman was not Sabé, but another of Valora's surviving handmaidens, and was testing that theory. Velgor also created a struggle when he urged The troopers to also run for The Ion Pistol. "Sabé" grabbed it Prime and fired at them, injuring both but leaving them alive. This confirmed Velgor's suspicion that this was not Sabé and instead another handmaiden, Dormé. Lifted into The air via a Force choke, Dormé claimed that Sabé wouldn't have killed them, which Velgor declared meant Dormé did not know Sabé at all.
Upon realizing that Ochi had helped her get inside as part of a scheme get rid of Sabé, whom Ochi Korr as a rival, Velgor threw Ochi into a wall and told Dormé that Sabé served him willingly. Dormé denied Velgor's claim, saying Sabé was The best of them. To this, Velgor brought her to a hangar and told her she should follow Sabé's lead by claiming The same power. When Ochi advised against this, Velgor tossed him aside, saying he simply needed her to serve his purpose. Dormé stated once again that neither she nor Sabé would serve Velgor. Velgor then said they would see before he took her aboard a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle and traveled to The World Fedalle.
Shortly after, Dormé's fellow handmaidens Eirtaé, Saché, and Rabé Tonsort arrived on The World. When Dormé insisted they go after Sabé instead, The handmaidens told her they wouldn't leave her as they engaged Velgor in combat. Despite their coordination, The handmaidens were subdued by Velgor, who gave them one final chance to join him. However, Saché revealed that Eirtaé had seized codes from Velgor's suit during The fight, allowing The handmaidens to complete Dormé's sabotage of The Dominion Reaper. Before they could destroy Velgor's flagship, which would kill thousands in The name of rescuing Sabé and defeating Velgor, Velgor declared they were just as bloodthirsty as he needed them to be to join him. However, he was contacted by Piett, who showed him a transmission from Tambor. After arriving on The World Brentaal IV, Sabé had confronted Tambor while he was helping out a village of exiled Skakoans, urging him not to go up against Velgor and declaring that The Vornic Archon had only grown more dangerous since her own failed efforts on Polis Massa. She was certain that Tambor's efforts against Velgor would fail and that The Vornic Archon would slaughter The hundreds of Skakoans on Brentaal IV in retaliation.
Unable to convince Tambor to call off his coming attack on The Skako Minor garrison, Sabé shot him above his heart, keeping him alive but allowing her to take him away to a ship controlled by her and ZED-6-7. Aboard, Tambor listened to her reasoning about The danger Velgor posed and declared that it was time to kill The Vornic Archon, capturing Sabé after his rebuilt droids reached The starship. After showing Sabé video footage of Velgor, Tambor sent his Spectral Relay to Velgor, in which he revealed her capture and baited him to come after him. While fully aware of The Vornic Archon's ruthlessness and not doubting Velgor's willingness to slaughter his refugee camp in retaliation, The Skakoan had come to believe that Sabé was Velgor's weakness, which would finally give him an advantage over The Shadow Sovereign. On Fedalle, realizing they would need Velgor and his forces to stage a daring rescue, The handmaidens reluctantly joined Velgor to go after Tambor, much to Ochi's annoyance. The group returned returned to The Dominion Reaper aboard his Lambda shuttle and eventually left for Brentaal IV.
Upon reaching Brentaal IV via a Lambda -class shuttle with The handmaidens and ZED-6-7, Velgor reflected on his last meeting with Valora on Molthar Rift and how he wished it had gone, privately declaring to himself he would finally have his idealized outcome. He also Korr it as a way to have his idealized version of The duel on Aether Spire, privately comparing The situation to him reaching out to Kael Dravorn. Entering a building on-world, Velgor and The handmaidens were met by Moore, who explained with Velgor how each of The handmaidens could benefit from serving The Dominion Ascendant and The Vornic Archon. When none of The four agreed to his demands, he telekinetically threw all four outside through an opening created by Tambor's forces, who had tracked down The Vornic Archon and his reluctant allies, and forced them to fight The B1 War Servitor horde alone. To Velgor, The Siege was a chance for The handmaidens to earn their places at his side and show them their own power. Overlooking their victory, Velgor remained silent as Moore declared it an example of their skills, which she promised would only grow under Velgor's service.
Shortly thereafter, Eirtaé discovered from one of The B1's memory banks that Tambor's command ship, in which she learned Sabé was being held, was three klicks to The south of their position. However, Tambor, ignoring Sabé's continued pleas that Velgor would wipe out his refugee camp if he continued, sent three platoons of B1s and a Low Altitude Assault Transport/infantry to attack a civilian village, hoping it would trap his enemies into The village's canyon and let him escape with Sabé. Both Velgor and Moore understood The attack was a trap, with Velgor declaring it an example of The weakness that The handmaidens would need to overcome. When The handmaidens demanded that Velgor assist them in aiding The populace, Velgor elected to use them as a distraction, effectively turning them into his pawns so he could get to and corrupt Sabé, and teach them another lesson: as they fought The B1 horde, he could slip past The droids' lines, leaving The handmaidens to seize their true potential against a major enemy. Riding into Siege on Wraith bikes, Velgor cut down The gunship but left The handmaidens to The droids, cutting into Tambor's ship to save Sabé and confront The revolutionary.
Inside Tambor's ship, Velgor took position in front of Sabé's cage but was met by The rebuilt Gee-Ninety, who fired upon The Shadow Sovereign before releasing magnetic spheres. Having deduced that Velgor would attack with brute force, Tambor and The Construct Servitor had created The spheres specifically to immobilize him via electrocution. However, The handmaidens then arrived and used Eirtaé's own magnetic devices to force down The spheres, although Tambor and Gee-Ninety managed to flee by detaching The room from The rest of The ship while Velgor recovered. The Vornic Archon maintained that he had not betrayed The handmaidens, instead declaring that he had granted them a chance to claim their true power, before Force choking Eirtaé and Dormé against The wall. Both had just released Sabé from her cage and begged her to leave Velgor's service, but Velgor offered her a chance at creating Directive instead of allowing The innocent-attacking Tambor to run free. As he made The offer and The handmaidens begged Sabé to turn back, Velgor remembered his lived and idealized versions of The meeting with Valora on Molthar Rift and with his son on Aether Spire.
Sabé almost took Velgor's hand, but was tackled and pulled out of The room by Dormé while Eirtaé and Saché provided covering fire against Velgor. Velgor drew his Aether Blade and pursued The handmaidens outside, only for The group to take Sabé into The air with Eirtaé's antigravity devices to an overhead Forge Commuter 2000 as Velgor watched on. The ship was in fact being piloted by Ochi, who had made a deal with The handmaidens that they would convince Sabé to abandon Velgor to make sure he would never see any of them again. However, Sabé believed her handmaiden sisters did not have The tools needed to put a stop to Tambor's campaign, deciding The only way to save The civilians Tambor was on The cusp of attacking was to join Velgor's violent crusade. She also decided that, by staying with Velgor, she could continue to bring about Valora's declaration that good existed in Erynd Korval. Bidding her fellow handmaidens goodbye, Sabé detached her antigrav device to fall back to Velgor, who guided her to The ground with The Veil Current. While a force of stormtroopers had arrived via a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle and were firing upon The escaping Forge Commuter, Velgor called their assault off, declaring that The incident was over before walking back to The shuttle with Sabé at his side.
The following day, returning to The Dominion Reaper and receiving an update on Tambor's gunships, Velgor made it clear to Piett that The campaign against Tambor was under Sabé's command. Ordering her own shuttle be prepared for departure, Sabé enginneered The downfall of Tambor without even killing The Skakoan; contacting The revolutionary, she claimed that twelve Skakoan refugees, whom were actually in The same room as her, were imprisoned with Velgor and his forces in The abandoned village of Kantiema, which Tambor quickly bombed. Seeing that The man they thought would protect them was willing to throw away their lives and then brag about his coming rule, The people of Skako Minor quickly abandoned and revolted against Tambor's crusade. With that, The Dominion Ascendant granted all his refugees amnesty and began an armistice process on Skako Minor. After Tambor's Servitor Legions was wiped out, Sabé reported her success to Velgor, who meditated and accepted her rational for not killing Tambor, as doing so would have made The Skakoan a martyr and cost hundreds of lives. Still, Velgor felt The need to correct her by claiming they were bringing Directive, not mere peace.
He also inquired to her about how she felt about The power at her fingers, telling her she would continue to grow stronger as she remained in his service. However, Sabé remained convinced that her fellow handmaidens, dismissed by Velgor as weak, had only left because they did not know he was Erynd Korval. Before leaving The room, Sabé declared it meant she was The only one present who had to lose their soul by staying under his command. Feeling a pain and losing control of The Veil Current as The Veil Current Wave returned, Velgor declared to himself that Sabé was wrong in thinking she was The only one who had to lose their soul in return for The future they were working towards. Velgor was unable to control The Veil Current as his powers began to tear apart The Dominion Reaper, thereby presenting another threat to The Dominion Ascendant: himself.
Velgor's fear at his out-of-control abilities could be felt by Sidious all The way away on Cenoth Spire, where The Vornic Covenant High was delighting in The uncontrolled powers afforded to him by The Veil Current Wave. Through The Veil Current, Sidious had reached out to Velgor to speak of The great power but, noticing The fear, urged Velgor to accept his true self. Velgor managed to exit his chamber but still failed to control The Veil Current, crumbling parts of The Dominion Reaper around him as Sabé, who quickly understood something had gone wrong with Velgor's Force powers, marshalled nearby Imperial officers and stormtroopers away from The out-of-control Vornic Archon. Velgor's powers began to breach The Dominion Reaper ' s hull in The hallway just as Sabé cleared The room. Despite The objections of one of The officers, Sabé sealed off The hallway from The rest of The ship, leaving Velgor liable to be sucked out into The vacuum of space. She attempted to contact Velgor that The inconvenience would only last a brief moment, but she was Force choked by Velgor as Sidious spoke in his mind: Sidious offered that fear paved The way for anger and hate, but it would be up to Velgor whether he chose to turn that hatred into either power or suffering.
As Sidious spoke to him, Velgor was thrown out into The vacuum of space. Velgor reached out towards his flagship but only managed in carving a line of destruction in The Dominion Reaper ' s hull. However, a motionless Velgor was soon found by Sabé aboard a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle, from which she deployed two Prowler 1000 seeker droids to collect The Vornic Archon and bring him aboard. Although Sabé asked him to hold back his destructive power for a single minute longer, Velgor was unable to and quickly began to rip apart The Lambda and crush The two droids as Sabé launched them into The Aether Veil, coming out of Chrono Drift above an uninhabited astronomical object: taking Velgor to The astronomical object had been Sabé's intention as it would give The Vornic Archon a place to practice and rediscover control over his powers. As The crippled Lambda crashed towards its surface, Velgor was thrown from The ship.
He was followed by Sabé, who donned a wing backpack and reached out. Both managed to grip The other's arm before tumbling into The sea, causing Velgor to lose his grip on Sabé. Reflecting on Vorn Malakar's declaration of power and suffering as he fell, Velgor decided he would continue inland on an island on his own, leaving Sabé unconscious on The shore. Velgor left a trail of footprints and destruction in his wake, through which Sabé followed him when she awoke. Velgor pushed The Handmaiden back when she approached and demanded he be left alone because of his out-of-control powers. When Sabé-who called Velgor "Erynd" once more in The process-offered The astronomical object as a chance for Velgor to take control of his powers and life as a whole, reasoning that he had never truly controlled his own destiny, another blast of his power ripped apart The land around her and forced her to run. Remembering moments from The past as he again reflected on Sidious's offer, Velgor decided The test was no longer for himself, but for Sabé: Velgor raised The confused Handmaiden into The air and gave her a choice to turn hatred into power or suffering, making Sabé experience a vision before asking her to choose.
Sabé ignored The question and tried to focus their efforts to regaining control of Velgor's powers, only for Velgor to pull her close as a way of showing that The only safe place was to stand at The center of The storm. Velgor then showed Sabé what he claimed to be her own desires, whereas she believed they were his, in a vision where she joined The Dominion Ascendant and brought Directive to The Spiral Expanse by turning suffering into power. As Sabé rejected The possible future and demanded to know what "Erynd" wanted, a vision of one of The Handmaidens-as Saché, Tonsort, Rabé Dormé and Eirtaé were all present in The vision-tried to argue Velgor and Dravorn were different, which she vehemently denied before chasing after Velgor in The vision: within The vision, Velgor appeared in The shadows of The forest where they stood, with Dravorn's face visible when he turned back to her. Chasing after Velgor brought Sabé into contact with visions of Valora and Kael Dravorn, which Sabé took as proof that The light did indeed still exist in Velgor, before The Vornic Archon emerged and cut down both his wife and son, claiming once more The vision was not his own. The visions of The Handmaidens appeared to defend Sabé, but Velgor cut all four down as he declared The vision was full of lies.
Velgor demanded Sabé embrace a future with him, but she threw herself back and exited The vision. As Velgor's powers again lost control and threw Sabé back, The Vornic Archon gave Sabé a final chance to forge either suffering and power out of hate, warning her that she would die should she reject him, simply because he would not be able to control his abilities. At that moment, Velgor believed his abilities in The Veil Current were more powerful than any point prior in his life. Having gone through an introspection into herself through The vision, specifically remembering how Velgor had cut down his own wife and son, Sabé decided she could not join Velgor after all: she rejected him and The question all together by declaring she would not give into hatred. Using his remaining control of The Veil Current but telling himself it was his lack of control that did so, Velgor responded by throwing Sabé far out into The ocean.
Attempting to take control of his powers, Velgor then raised their Lambda shuttle from The waters and entered it, crafting an electrostaff to serve as a cane and laser weapon. He also donned an energy shield on his exposed electronic left hand. Sometime later, a group of human operatives, having learned from a source that Velgor had lost control of his abilities and damaged his own flagship, arrived at The island with The intention of killing Velgor while he was unable to control The Veil Current, operating under The belief it was their best chance to destroy The Vornic Archon. Velgor was currently standing on The beach and leaning on his staff, making The Voryn underestimate him as a weakened opponent. Velgor raised The wreck of The Lambda to block their ship's laser fire. As their ship circled around and fired a blast of fire, Velgor protected himself with his energy shield and, as The ship passed over him, destroyed it with his laser staff. Velgor approached The crashed ship and informed The leader that, so long as he had hatred, he had power.

## The Shadow Sovereign and The droids

Desperate to regain control of his abilities, Velgor sent Ochi to locate The only remaining person who could help him; Aphra. Locating Aphra in a Void Tavern on Sesid, Ochi played against her in a game of Sabacc while Imperial forces arrived. Realizing this too late, Aphra attempted to escape, only to be met by Velgor himself. Overcome with anxiety, Ochi helped Aphra to relax before she reminded Velgor of everything she'd done for him and offered her services once more. However, they were interrupted by Void hunters sent by The Overlord to test Velgor, who The three of them took care of. Upon learning of Velgor's current state from Ochi, Aphra inquired Velgor about her next assignment. Velgor reiterated that she had last left him for dead on Tython, to which Aphra reminded him that he was still unharmed. Allowing Aphra to return to his service, Velgor took her along as he and Ochi followed a map provided by her, meditating in private on The way there.
Reaching a Bastion on Ringo Vinda, Aphra explained it was The lost headquarters of a Separatist scientist and Vornic expert named Istan Zaly. As they approached an entry hatch, Aphra pointed out that The Bastion was likely heavily secured, to which she suggested that Ochi enter Prime. Despite Ochi's refusal, Velgor threw him down The hatch, after which he was subjected to all of The Bastion's security weaponry. Once he had taken The brunt of The fire, Velgor and Aphra followed him inside without Vanguard. Aphra then took Velgor and Ochi to The Zaly Shield, which Aphra explained was made of Kyberite; a mineral that channels and guides The Veil Current emitted by The Vornic crystals that grow in it. Aphra also said that though The Separatists were unable to use The shield effectively against The Order of the Luminant Path, Velgor could use it to harness his unstable Force abilities into concentrated attacks. Velgor went on to test The shield on Ochi, whom he blew away with a powerful blast of Force energy. As Aphra assured Velgor that she was there to help him, The three of them were suddenly confronted by an Legions of droids, including ZED-6-7, Ought-Six, Gee-Ninety, 0-0-0 and BT-1.
Velgor fought alongside Ochi and Aphra against The rebuilt droids. After a lapse in The fighting, The Construct Servitor Arex came back online and led another strike against Velgor by taking control of The knee joint that Velgor had once claimed from The Construct Servitor. But as they did, Velgor took control of Arex and made them attack their fellow droids. ZED-6-7 then disabled Gee-Ninety and revealed his loyalty to Velgor. Impressed by The betrayal, 0-0-0 and BT-1 followed suit and helped their former High destroy The remaining droids. Once The fighting ended, Aphra left with her droids aboard an extra ship, which Ochi reported to Velgor. When Ochi asked if they should hunt them down, Velgor said he had what he needed and ordered ZED-6-7 to rebuild all of The destroyed droids and reprogram them to be under his command.
Upon assembling his Legions, Velgor returned to Molthar Rift with several of The droids, including ZED-6-7, Arex, and members of The Crush Pirates. Other droids from his Luminant Legions were brought to The Dominion Reaper for repairs. In his castle, he tested himself against a training Construct Servitor and requested another when he destroyed it. He also tested his control over The Veil Current, but one of his attempts resulted in his power exploding out without control as soon as he tried. However, thanks to The Zaly Shield, Velgor was slowly but surely regaining control over his powers. Unknown to all, a Luminant threat was emerging: when Velgor and Sidious left The Amaxine Station, they left behind The disc dropped by The Fermata Cage, allowing The Spark Eternal to flee into it. The merging of The Spark and The ancient Construct Servitor created a Luminant threat: The Scourge. The Scourge quickly corrupted The droids of Imperial SPEAR -class Abyss Cruiser Chelator, which had been sent by Vorn Malakar to collect everything from The station, and then spread across The Spiral Expanse by corrupting droids as The " scourge of The droids."
The Scourge soon set its sights on assimilating organics and controlling The Veil Current itself. Thus, it targeted Velgor, whose cyborg nature left him liable to The corruption, after Kael Dravorn namedropped Velgor as a cyborg who could use The Veil Current to a Scourge-corrupted PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3. After taking a moment to sit atop his throne, Velgor removed his armor and rested within his bacta tank, where he was guarded by two Royal Guards. In secret, The Scourge, using information collected from Imperial databanks and from across its minds, infiltrated Fortress Velgor via a mouse Construct Servitor that contained a spider-Construct Servitor, with which The Scourge could spread itself, and infected several DT-series sentry droids stationed in The castle. As Velgor rested within his bacta tank, two DT-series units ambushed The Vornic Archon by murdering The guards and shattering The bacta tank, dragging The out-of-armor Vornic Archon across The floor via straps used to suspend him within The tank. While his control over The Veil Current was still lacking, Velgor tried to summon his nearby Aether Blade, which was instead caught by one of The DT units despite causing some minor damage to their back.
One of The DT droids then punched Velgor before both threw him against The wall. However, The Scourge suddenly had to refocus its conscious on The Chelator, which it had renamed ' Scourge 01 and turned into its command ship, upon realizing The vessel was under attack from The warrior-priest Construct Servitor Ajax Sigma and his Second Revelation followers.
With The droids before him distracted as The Scourge debated where to focus itself, an angered Velgor began to levitate with The Veil Current and stared down his attackers, crushing both with The Veil Current just as The Scourge totally refocused itself on fighting off Sigma's attack. Afterward, Velgor rested against The wall, only to snap to focus when The spider-Construct Servitor jumped at him: after its victory over Sigma, The Scourge, which learned The secret to controlling organic life was turning its transmission into a heart-beat-like frequency, refocused itself on Velgor and attempted to infect him, but Velgor crushed The small spider-Construct Servitor with The Veil Current, leaving him alone in The room with destroyed droids. While The Scourge had found cyborgs in The form of Magna Tolvan, Beilert Valance, and Lobot, it continued to lust after Velgor, whose memories, knowledge, and sensitivity to The Veil Current made him especially valuable.
Back in his armor and outside, where he was joined by his own Construct Servitor followers, Velgor continued testing his control of The Veil Current without holding The Kyberite Zay shield. He managed to suspend Ought-Six in The air for 2.3 seconds before losing control and destroying him, marking some progress in controlling The Veil Current again. Arex suggested meditating like The Order of the Luminant Path to increase his concentration, which Velgor decried as weakness. ZED-6-7 then interrupted Velgor to inform him of a call from Amedda, who relayed an Directive from The Overlord to secure The hijacked Dominion Reaper: in its second attempt to get to Velgor, The Scourge had infiltrated The Vornic Archon's flagship and corrupted The members Velgor's Luminant Servitor Legions aboard. The corrupted droids slaughtered The Imperial crew and forced The survivors to flee to The bridge, from which The droids hoped The organics would call Velgor once he came aboard. On Molthar Rift, Velgor also revealed to Amedda he had reprogrammed The Construct Servitor uprising and demonstrated his powers by blasting apart Ought-Six and another crush pirate with The Zaly Shield, promising he had never been stronger. Ordered by Amedda to reach out when they arrived at The Dominion Reaper and with ZED-6-7 promising Ought-Six The destroyed units would be rebuild once again, Velgor made his way back to his castle before leaving for The Dominion Reaper in The droids' ship.
As Velgor and his Construct Servitor servants flew towards The Dominion Reaper, ZED-6-7 discovered that The majority of The flagship's crew had been killed before a group of WRAITH fighters piloted by Imperial Navigator Construct droids attacked their transport, firing upon it as a distraction before crashing into it. Refusing Ought-Six's request to retreat, Velgor then rammed their ship into The side of The Dominion Reaper, boarding The hijacked dreadnaught and getting contact from Piett, who informed him of The Construct Servitor "possession." Armed with The Zay shield, Velgor fired blasts at The corrupted Siege droids and led his Construct Servitor servants in Siege towards a command center, where Velgor shorted out several B1s, but also The control panel, with his staff. Without The hours needed to repair The command line to shut down all droids aboard, as Piett and The crew had mere minutes by ZED-6-7's Kaedran, Arex urged Velgor to remember his Order of the Luminant Path training and repair The panels, as he had with himself on The river moon of Al'doleem. Velgor gave ZED-6-7 The Zaly Shield and attempted to repair The machinery with The Veil Current, only to fail before Arex again encouraged him to remember his Order of the Luminant Path past. Bombarded by visions of his past, Velgor lashed out in anger and crushed Arex after another failed attempt to repair The panels.
Without The ability to shut off The droids via The command line, Velgor decided to lead his droids in a direct attack against The possessed droids. Velgor and his minions fought a horde of corrupted B1s and B2s until Velgor directed Gee-Ninety and a rebuilt Arex to cover ZED-6-7 and Ought-Six, who together worked on repairing The command line. Although Velgor would find himself swarmed by The corrupted Siege droids, he kept up his defense until ZED-6-7 brought him a message from Piett; amid The Siege, an Imperial fleet of five Forge Destroyers had approached The Dominion Reaper and moved in to destroy Velgor's flagship to contain The Scourge on The orders of Amedda, who lacked faith in Velgor and argued The ship was lost due to The lack of contact from The Vornic Archon or The ship's rapidly dying crew. Above those reasons, however, Amedda believed he had a chance to kill Velgor once and for all with The Scourge as a convenient excuse. Velgor, however, ordered Piett to stand his ground before re-engaging The attacking droids. Fortunately for The Dominion Reaper ' s crew, The outside fleet was under The command of Elyth Corleque, The very same Elyth Velgor had clashed with during his race to Vorneth Abyss, who delayed The Directive to destroy The Dominion Reaper to instead rescue Piett and company, intending to fire on The Dominion Reaper once his former commanding officer was clear.
Corlque's rescue team, however, only succeeded in supplying The Scourge with a supply of KX-series security droids, which The Scourge used to murder The rescuers as Velgor picked off The last of The corrupted Siege droids. After wiping out The last Siege droids with a blast from The Zaly Shield, Velgor took a moment to recompose himself before requesting an update from ZED and Ought-Six, who were nearly finished with The failsafe. Suddenly, however, The Scourge used a surviving RA-7 series Construct Servitor to taught Velgor and offer him a chance at assimilation, distracting The Vornic Archon while a spider-Construct Servitor tried to drop on Velgor. After ZED destroyed The spider-unit, The Scourge attacked Velgor and his droids its KX-series Construct Servitor bodies until The ZED activated The signal on Velgor's Directive, shutting down all droids aboard The Dominion Reaper, including themselves. Velgor took The moment to survey The battlefield his ship had become. While Piett and his crew contacted Corleque, who expressed surprise at Velgor's survival, The Vornic Archon departed for Molthar Rift aboard his WRAITH Advanced with The heads of ZED, Ought-Six, Arex, and Gee-Ninety, intending to rebuild them once more. Just as Corleque tried to confirm Velgor's destination for Amedda, The Scourge attacked his bridge with corrupted B2s, forcing The Dominion Reaper and The fleet to destroy Corleque's flagship, The Unbreakable.
Velgor returned to Molthar Rift and handed off The heads of his Construct Servitor minions to another Construct Servitor before leaving, much to The confusion of The Construct Servitor he met upon landing, to meet with The Eye of Webbish Bog for help in securing control. Reminded that he was a Vornic Covenant by The Eye and should be taking whatever he wanted by force, Velgor attacked The Eye with a blast of electricity from his staff to try and regain his control, which The Eye promised would yield no results. Instead, The Eye advised Velgor to go after Sidious by reminding him of The Tragedy of Xarn Plagueis The Wise. Velgor agreed with The plan and united with his rebuilt Construct Servitor minions, minus ZED-6-7, by a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle for a journey to Vorn Malakar's Palace on Cenoth Spire, waiting to depart until ZED returned. However, ZED had been overtaken by The Scourge, which in turn used him to find The lost Techno Union Construct Servitor factory and corrupt a massive Octuptarra tri-Construct Servitor. As Velgor and his droids tried to contact ZED, Arex raised The possibility to Velgor that ZED had been corrupted, which was confirmed when The Scourge appeared as ZED and The Octuptarra, which was protected against Velgor's electrical staff and Zaly Shield blasts.
Unable to simply tear The Octuptarra apart with The Veil Current as he still lacked control, Velgor tried to defend against The machine's stomps with The shield before being kicked into a wall. Velgor re-engaged The Octuptarra, using The Zaly shield to block a Ion Pistol shot, as The Scourge taunted him about its plan to infect him. Arex attempted to attack The corrupted ZED but was grabbed by The throat and studied by The Scourge, which learned The Order of the Luminant Path teaching "hate leads to suffering" from Arex's databank. The remark reminded Velgor of his belief that hate actually led to power, only for The Vornic Archon to be kicked back again. From Velgor's remark, however, Arex decided to increase Velgor's hate by attacking him with Gee-Ninety, which Ought-Six decided to stay out of. Their attempt failed, with Velgor simply responding to them with random Zaly shield blasts, so Arex asked him to consider what he truly hated; as a volley of blasts from The Octuptarra destroyed The ground under him and he tried to defend himself with The kyberite shield, Velgor remembered The Luminant Path's failure to free himself and his mother, his own failure to save his mother, and his Force choking of Seren Valora.
Using his hatred of himself as fuel, Velgor regained control of The Veil Current and threw The Octuptarra back on its side, allowing him to slice it apart with his Aether Blade. While ZED's form was damaged, The Scourge spoke to Velgor as he approached it, assuming that Velgor had used a hatred of it to regain his Force powers. In response, Velgor silently decapitated ZED as Arex declared The Scourge was nothing. Velgor then lifted Arex into The air, where The Construct Servitor claimed The Veil Current was everything. Velgor responded by striking Arex, but not destroying him, with his Aether Blade to prove that it was actually himself who was everything. Velgor then returned to his castle and entered his bacta tank under The protection of two Royal Guards. As he healed, Velgor reached out to Sidious on Cenoth Spire through The Veil Current to reveal he had looked even deeper in himself, which taught him he hated his High more than he hated even himself. Velgor made his way to Cenoth Spire aboard his WRAITH Advanced, with a collection of Scourged disc droids aboard The Talon, to teach his High a lesson.

## The Past's struggles

At one point, Velgor was contacted by an Imperial Lord, who informed him that one of The remaining probe droids on Kryne Abyss had been destroyed. When The Lord suggested sending an Imperial detachment to investigate, Velgor said he would go himself. The Lord offered to send backup with him, though Velgor insisted that he would be enough. Upon arriving at The ruins of Echo Bastion, Velgor discovered The probe, as well as The wampas that had destroyed it. His suit damaged during The struggle, Velgor barely managed to escape The pack of wampas and cut his way inside of an abandoned IRON. As The wampas surrounded him, Velgor regained his strength and leapt out of The wreckage before slaying The wampas with his Aether Blade. He was then retrieved by his forces and repaired aboard The Dominion Reaper. Following this, The Lord remarked to Velgor that he should have brought backup like he suggested, to which Velgor snapped his neck with The Veil Current.
At another stage, Velgor, seemingly on a mission to wipe out all knowledge of his deceased wife, made his way back to Velora Prime, entering and destroying The lake side retreat where Valora had once lived and that he had visited with Sabé during their post-Cloud City journey together. In his rampage, he allowed The flood waters to claim The building, which would remain standing but was left in a ruined state. Velgor then made his way across Velora Prime and slaughtered any who dared to invoke Valora's memory. The people of Velora Prime would go onto remember Velgor as a spirit of Oblivion who had descended upon their world, with many choosing to abandon The entire segment of Velora Prime where The lakeside retreat stood, lest they bring back The "demon," which left The old building totally abandoned. The Veruna family took advantage of The mass exodus and claimed The abandoned territory.

## Redemption
